## v253 — Nov 16 2026: 🏓 Pickleball (social pools and games, points only)
- WHY: Steve (Nov 16 2026) asked what games pickleball players play, like a Calcutta or betting game, and wanted it in Oddable.
- DECISIONS (Nov 16 2026): (1) POINTS ONLY, NO MONEY: every member starts with 100 bragging points; Calcutta bids, the pot and payouts are points; the page says "Oddable never takes, holds or pays money, and points can't be bought or cashed" on the home screen, the Calcutta page, its ⓘ and the New event form. (2) LOCAL-ONLY for now: events live in localStorage (`pb1-ev-<id>`, plus `pb1-me` = who you are picking as); NO server calls of any kind (the test routes every non-local request and saw none). (3) Groups pass an event around with a share code (`PB1:` + base64 JSON of the whole event); "Join with code" imports it, asks Replace my copy / Keep both if you already have it. (4) The page does not load gate.js / sync.js (nothing to gate or sync); decide later if it should sit behind the Oddable sign-in.
- Files: `pics/pickleball.html` (page + CSS, same colours/themes as index.html: navy default, violet via `pics-theme`, dark via `pics-dark`), `pics/pickleball.js` (all logic + UI, hash routes `#/`, `#/new`, `#/join`, `#/e/<id>[/games|/stand[/<unit>]|/calc[/pot|/pay|/m/<member>]|/skins[/<unit>]|/pick[/m/<member>]|/share|/players]`). Entry: a "🏓 Pickleball" button at the end of the sport row in index.html (plain onclick, no "Not a pick model yet" warning since nobody bets here) and an `<a>` at the end of sports.html's row. index.html change = that button + the version bump only.
- Formats (ⓘ on each explains it in plain words): Round robin (rotating partners; partners from the circle method so with 8 or 9 players nobody partners twice; byes picked by fewest byes so far, spread never more than 1; default rounds = enough for byes to rotate evenly, 3-12; optional timed games allow ties). King of the Court (seeded by DUPR, top 4 on court 1; winners move up and split, losers move down and split; court-1 winners stay, bottom losers stay; extra players wait on a bench and swap in on the bottom court, fewest byes first; next round is made when every court is scored; ranking = court/result of the last full round, so court-1 winners are 1-2). Challenge court (winners stay, losers to the back of the line, next two challenge; a pair that wins `maxW` in a row (default 3) steps off; runs until Finish event). Ladder (singles or fixed doubles, starts in DUPR order, challenge up to 3 spots above, challenger win = swap, loss = no change, weeks counter; order is replayed from the scored challenges). Single / double elimination (seeded by DUPR, standard 1-vs-last seeding, byes to top seeds, DE losers bracket + grand final + reset game if the losers-bracket team wins game 1; bracket games point at the games they come from, so a changed result clears the later games that depended on it). Ladder/brackets play as Doubles (fixed pairs, best with lowest DUPR; odd player = alternate) or Singles.
- Add-ons: Calcutta (owner + bid per player/team before play; a member can't bid more than they have; auction closes at the first score; pot split default 50/30/20, only bought entries count for places, ties share; whole points by largest remainder so payouts always sum exactly to the pot and all members' points always total members x start). Skins (each game = a skin worth N points to each winning player/team; tied timed games carry it to the next game scored). Pick'em (event winner pick closes at the first score, 5 pts; per-game picks close when the game is scored, 1 pt; a pick is void if the game's players change; leaderboard drills to each member's picks).
- Owner rules applied: every summary tile drills to its detail with ‹ Back (games, standings → player/team games, Calcutta pot/payouts/member, skins → player, pick'em → member); standings/lists are wrapped cards (no tables, no sideways scroll); numbers sit right, names left; score entry opens inline under the game (no floating sheet), so nothing floats over content.
- Share-code safety: an imported code is untrusted: `clean()` refuses odd ids and forces numbers to be numbers; every name is escaped (test: an `<img onerror>` name shows as text and never runs; a bad game id is refused).
- Tests (Playwright, 390 and 1280 px, own server on port 8780, 75 checks each, all pass; script kept outside the repo): all six formats with 8-9 players and 2 courts created through the New event form and scored through the UI; RR 9 players = 9 rounds, 18 games, each sits once, no partner repeats; RR generator checked for 4-16 players x 1-3 courts (byes spread <= 1); King movement after round 1 checked exactly, winner change of an earlier round refused, 9-player bench rotation; challenge court 3-wins-and-off; ladder swap / no swap / 3-spot limit; SE 9 players (8 real games, 7 byes) and finish 1/2/T3/T5; DE 9 players with a reset game (everyone else out after exactly 2 losses) and DE doubles; Calcutta sum = pot (183/183, also with tied semifinal losers) and member points total 1000 for 10 members; skins carry 2+2+2; pick'em leaderboard; share code round trip; drill + back on every tile; no page errors; no outside requests; no sideways scroll. NFL page still loads with the v252 tiles (Alert 14-6 vs spread in the mock), 0 page errors. The Pickleball chip is at the end of the sport row, which already scrolls sideways on phones (same as NCAAB).
- OPEN: server sync of events between phones (today a share code is a snapshot; send a new code after more games); whether the page should require the Oddable sign-in; no edit of players after an event is created (make a new event); challenge court and earlier King rounds can only fix points, not change the winner, once the next game/round is set; pick'em/Calcutta members are the players plus extra names typed at creation (no adding members later); not tested on a real iPhone/Safari (clipboard copy falls back to select-and-copy).


## v253 review fixes — Nov 16 2026 (same version, from the independent review of v253 Pickleball)
- Ladder ids: game ids now come from `newGid(ev)` = highest `g<number>` + 1, so a cancelled challenge never frees an id (before: cancel g1, the next challenge reused g2 and a score could land on the wrong match). `migrate(ev)` runs in `load()` and `clean()` and renames a repeated id on old saved events (later one gets a new id, its picks are dropped) and re-saves.
- Share-code safety: `clean()` now refuses bracket links that point at a missing, later or the same game (no loops, no stack overflow), bad seeds, a bracket without its final (`F1-0`/`F2-0` for double elim), side lists that aren't 1-2 real units, `nx` to a missing game, a ladder order that isn't every unit once, and a King court count that needs more players than exist; `alt` keeps only ids in `ps`; format lookups use hasOwnProperty (`constructor`/`__proto__` refused, also in the New event check). Each event row on the home list and the event page are rendered in their own try: a damaged saved event shows an error card with Delete it → Yes, delete.
- Ties: `finish()` returns real tie groups outside brackets. Round robin ranks by WIN % (byes never cost a place), then point diff, then points scored; challenge court by wins, then win %, then diff; equal keys = one group (shown as `1=`). King: the two court-1 winners of the last full round share 1st, court-1 losers share the next place, etc. Calcutta splits a tied place's % evenly (sum still = pot); pick'em gives the winner pick to anyone who picked a member of the 1st-place group.
- Round robin partners: when courts force more than one sitter, each round is searched (fewest byes sit; pairs chosen greedily then improved with swaps to avoid repeat partners; then games to avoid repeat opponents). Partner repeats per event, 40 events each, before → after: 10p/2c 5.60 → 0.23, 8/1 1.85 → 0.20, 6/1 2.10 → 0.07, 12/2 1.00 → 0, 16/2 1.32 → 0, 20/3 1.75 → 0, 7/1 1.43 → 0.15; byes stay even.
- Challenge court: the newest result can be fixed or cleared while the next game is unscored (the game it set up is removed and the queue + win streak are restored from a snapshot `g.snap`); once that next game (or any later game) is scored, the winner is locked as before. ⓘ adds "With no one waiting in line, the losers rematch the winners."
- King with waiting players: they come in on the bottom court by fewest games played (bench first on ties); on ONE court the winners hold the court (split up) and the losers + bench rotate. ⓘ updated to say so.
- Player list: a trailing number is a DUPR only if it is a decimal from 2.0 to 8.0 (`Ann 3.5`, `Bo 4.0`); `Mike 1` / `Court 2` stay names.
- Join: pressing Import again replaces the "Replace my copy?" box instead of stacking another.
- Wording (no-money rule): Calcutta icon 🏅 (was 💰), "Points pot", "Points awarded" / "Points awarded (if it ended now)", "No points awarded yet", "Points pot split %"; "pot" only appears as "points pot".
- Entry chip: index.html puts 🏓 Pickleball right after NCAA (was last, off screen at 390 px); sports.html moves it next to the active league after building the row (the row auto-scrolls to the active league). Visible without swiping at 390 on index and on sports.html for NBA/MLB/NHL/NCAAB; nothing fixed covers it.
- Tests: the v253 test (74 checks) plus a reviewer-cases test (29 checks: ladder cancel + old-id repair, bad codes, damaged-event card + delete, King/RR ties with Calcutta + pick'em, RR win %, challenge-court undo, King 1-court bench, DUPR parsing, one join box, wording, ⓘ) pass at 390 and 1280; NFL page loads with 0 errors.

## v253 review fixes 2 — Nov 16 2026 (same version; second reviewer's two defects)
- Challenge court after Finish event: changing or clearing the newest result is refused while the event is finished ("Tap Reopen first"), because the next game is never rebuilt while finished (before, the losing pair vanished from court and queue). Reopen now runs `chalResume()`: a court whose newest game was scored while finished gets its next game, so court + line always hold everyone.
- Damaged saved records: `listEvents()` returns every `pb1-ev-` record; one that is not JSON, is null, has no id, or whose id doesn't match its key comes back as `{bad, key, name}` and shows the damaged-event card. The card and the event page use the REAL storage key for Delete (before: it removed `pb1-ev-`+ev.id, so a mismatched record couldn't be deleted, and no-id/bad-JSON records were silently hidden). The event page also shows the card for such a key instead of "not on this device".
- Cache: `pickleball.html` loads `pickleball.js?v=253.2` so phones pick up the fix without a version bump (sw.js is network-first, so its cache name is unchanged). The next real bump puts it back to `?v=###`.
- Tests: reviewer-cases test now 35 checks (adds finished-event lock, Reopen rebuild, fix-after-reopen, damaged keys listed + deleted on home and event page); v253 test 74 checks; both pass at 390 and 1280.

## v254 — Nov 16 2026: every game of the week is in the Games list (owner: "Still show the upcoming games even if they are in the locks.")
- What was wrong: on the Games board (NFL and NCAA, `newBoard(games)` in `pics/index.html`) up to two upcoming games per week were missing from the list under the week bar. The list was built with `list.filter((g) => NFILT === 'watch' || !((bp && g === bp.g) || (ur && g === ur.g)))`. `bp` = the old "Bet of the Day" game, `ur` = the old "Biggest Upset Pick" game. Those cards sat above the list until v241, so their games were left out of the list to avoid showing them twice. v241 removed the cards but not the filter, so the two games vanished. The chips still counted them ("All 14", "Upcoming 8") while the list showed 12 / 6. In the test week the two hidden games were MIN at GB (the 🏠 road dog pick) and JAX at TEN (the 🤝 Helper) - which is why it looked like "games in the locks are hidden".
- Fix: the list is now `list.map(nCard)`. Every game of the week shows for every chip, and the number of cards equals the number on the chip. The 📣 Oddable Alert banner (`alertBox(wk)`) is untouched, so Alert games show in the banner AND as full cards in the list. Decision (Nov 16 2026, owner's request above): a game is never left out of the list because it is a Lock, road dog, Helper or any other featured pick.
- Removed (dead since v241, searched first - nothing else used them): inside `newBoard`: `bo`, `cand`, `good`, `bp`, `botd`, `uo`, `ucand`, `ur`, `upset`, `TRB` (the old Bet of the Day / Biggest Upset / Double Lock + Lock top cards). CSS that only styled those cards in `pics/index.html`: every `.botd…` and `.upset…` rule (with their `.bh2` / `.bmoney` parts). Kept, still used elsewhere: `oddsML`, `Phi`, `SD`, `trDD`, `trGrade`, `trophyData`. No pick, record or number changed.
- Looked for the same leftover elsewhere: Live page (`livePageRender`) - no such filter, every chip lists its full group. Multiview picker (`mvPick`) - leaves out finished games and games already in Multiview and shows the first 24, on purpose, nothing to do with removed cards. `pics/sports.html` (NBA/MLB/NHL/NCAAB) has the same filter, but there the Bet of the Day and Biggest Upset cards are STILL shown above the list, so the games are on screen - left as is. `classic.html` has no such filter.
- Tested (local Playwright, 430x900, mirror mode, outside network blocked, 2026 games.csv; week 10 mocked as 4 final, 2 live, 8 upcoming with spreads): cards vs chip before -> after: All 12/14 -> 14/14, Live 2/2 -> 2/2, Upcoming 6/8 -> 8/8, Final 4/4 -> 4/4. After the fix the list holds the Lock game (BUF at NYJ), the road dog pick (MIN at GB), the not-picked division road dog (PIT at CIN) and the Helper (JAX at TEN). Alert banner text identical before and after. 0 page errors. No sideways scroll (page 430 wide, nothing in the board wider). My Bets banner covers nothing at the top and is tucked away once scrolled. Not checked: live site, a real phone, real ESPN data, the NCAA board with NCAA data (same function), the full `render()` path (the harness calls `newBoard` directly).
- Version v254: index.html (comment, header pill, footer "Oddable v254 (all games listed)", trophy.js / gate.js / alert-frozen.json `?v=254`), sports.html `gate.js?v=254`, sw.js `pics-v254`, pickleball.html (comment, `pickleball.js?v=254`, footer) per the bump list above - no Pickleball code changed.

(2) Version numbers overlap: main is now v254 with this fix, while `groups-v1` / preview also call their work v254. Agree the numbering before merging preview work to main. ## v255 — Nov 16 2026: 🎯 Prop ideas of the week (ideas only, no lines)
- Owner (Nov 16 2026): "Prop bets of the week? Can you add that?" Asked which version, he chose: "App suggests, no lines". So the app lists players running hot as prop IDEAS. There is no sportsbook line and no price.
- DECISIONS (Nov 16 2026): (1) Ideas only. Never called picks, locks or recommendations; "Lock" still means only the weekly 🔒 Lock. (2) Not in the 📣 Oddable Alert, not in any record, units or tile. Nothing in v255 changes a pick, record, unit or the Alert. (3) No line is suggested: "Track as my bet" leaves the line empty for the user to type from his sportsbook. (4) The honesty number is shown on screen whatever it says; the rule was not tuned to make it look good. (5) No player photos, no logos, no new libraries, no Odds API credits.
- THE RULE (odds-api/propideas.js, `core()`; the text is also in the payload as `rule` and in the ⓘ sheet): stats = passing yards, rushing yards, receiving yards, receptions (passing TDs only as extra info on a passing idea). A game counts as played when the player has any passing, rushing or receiving line in that game's ESPN box score; a stat with no line that day counts as 0. A player qualifies for a stat when: he has played 4+ games and has that stat's line in 4+ games; his last game played is his team's most recent finished game; his season average is at least 180 passing yards, 35 rushing yards, 35 receiving yards or 3 catches a game. HOT = average of his last 3 games played vs his average over the games BEFORE those 3 (not the whole season): at least +20% AND at least 12 yards (1 catch for receptions) higher. Ranked by percentage, then: one idea per player (his best), at most 3 per stat, at most 2 per team, top 8. Only teams with a game in the target week that has not kicked off; a team whose game is under way is left out until it is final.
- THE WEEK: ESPN's current regular-season week while it still has a game that has not kicked off (so mid-week only the games still to come); once every game has started, next week's games. Kickoffs are also checked against the clock at answer time, so a game drops out at kickoff without waiting for the next schedule read. Outside the regular season: no ideas, with a note.
- DATA AND CACHING: ESPN only. `scoreboard?limit=400` (current week) + `scoreboard?limit=400&week=N&seasontype=2` per unfinished-in-cache week + one `summary?event=ID` per finished game (boxscore.players[].statistics[], columns found by LABEL: YDS, TD, REC). A finished game is parsed once and kept: in memory and in Supabase `oddable_sync`, one row per week, id `lock:pi26w<week>` (same dbGet / dbPut-ignore / dbPatchIf pattern as alert.js; the `lock:` prefix keeps the rows out of player lists; about 25 KB a week). A week marked `done` is never read from ESPN again, even after a restart. Without SUPABASE_URL / SUPABASE_KEY it is memory only (payload `persist: "memory"`) and a restart refetches the season (about 150 calls now). Politeness: 3 summaries at a time with a 350 ms pause, one build at a time, the request NEVER waits for ESPN (it answers with `building: true` + `progress`), ESPN is re-read at most every 15 minutes once built, 2 minutes after a failure. A game whose summary fails twice is left out and the payload says so (`missing`, `note`); it is tried again every 10 minutes.
- HONESTY NUMBER (`carry` in the payload, `core().carry`): for every game a player has played this season, look only at his games before it. If he was a regular for a stat by the rule he goes in `base`; if he was also hot, in `hot`; if he would also have made that week's capped list of 8, in `shown`. `above` = that game's number was over his own season average up to then. A player who missed his next game is not counted; a player with two stats counts once per stat. Under 30 hot streaks sets `small: true` and the note says so. REAL 2026 NUMBERS (149 finished games, weeks 1-10, read from ESPN through the owner's Chrome on Nov 16 2026 and run through the same `core()` code): hot 152 of 388 (39.2%), all regular starters 423 of 1096 (38.6%), the capped lists 26 of 48 (54.2%, small). So being hot has told us almost nothing this season; the screen says exactly that. Both figures are under 50% because a season average is pulled up by big games.
- REAL IDEAS on Nov 16 2026 (same run): for week 10 with only LAC at BAL left: Quentin Johnston LAC receiving yards (last 3 57, 37, 58; 27.0 before; +88%). For week 11: Ja'Marr Chase CIN rec yds +200% (141, 155, 231 vs 58.5), Tyler Allgeier ARI rush yds +174%, Treylon Burks WAS rec yds +170%, Christian McCaffrey SF rec yds +154%, Kaelon Black SF rush yds +119%, Chuba Hubbard CAR receptions +113%, Makai Lemon PHI receptions +111%, Colston Loveland CHI receptions +89%. No passing idea qualified.
- SERVER: new `odds-api/propideas.js` (`core()` = pure rule: parseBox, parseSb, build, evalAt, cap, ideas, carry; service: buildNow, kick, payload, handle, dbLoad, dbSave), route GET `/propideas` wired in `server.js` (public, CORS `*`, no new request header). Payload: season, week, currentWeek, builtAt, building, progress{done,total}, unavailable, rule, ideasOnly, source, persist, ideas[], carry{hot,base,shown,small,note}, gamesLeft, missing?, note?. Each idea: id, name, team, pos (ESPN's box score has no position, so it is empty), stat, label, gid, week, opp, home, kickoff, last3, lastAvg, before, beforeN, seasonAvg, games, pct, gap, log[{w,opp,home,v,line,last3,td?}], passTD? (passing only). Tests: `node odds-api/propideas.test.js`. THE SERVER PART NEEDS THE calcutta-odds DEPLOY: until it is live the app shows "Prop ideas are not available right now."
- APP (pics/index.html): `propIdeasBox()` directly under `alertBox(wk)` in `newBoard`, NFL only (`CFB` returns ''; NBA/MLB/NHL/NCAAB live in sports.html and do not have it). Collapsed by default to one line "🎯 Prop ideas of the week · 8"; open/closed kept per phone in localStorage `pi-open`. Expanded: the fixed label "Ideas only. No sportsbook line or price. Not part of the Oddable Alert.", the honesty sentence (`piCarry`), then wrapped cards (`piCard`). Tap a card -> `piOpen(i)`: sheet with ‹ Back, his game log table (Week, Opponent left; the number right under its header; Pass TD column on a passing idea), the rule in a sentence, and "Track as my bet" -> `piTrack(i)`, which fills the EXISTING prop form (BF.step 'prop', BF.pg / pl / ps, BF.pn empty, saved by the unchanged `propSave`); no second bet type. ⓘ = `INFO.propideas`. `piLoad()` fetches `PIU` (derived from `ALU`, the same odds-server host as /alert) with a 12 s timeout: every 15 minutes when ready, every 20 s for 10 tries then every 2 minutes while building, every 2 minutes when unreachable; never when the tab is hidden; results kept in memory for the session. States: "Loading…", "Building this week's ideas… (40 of 149 games read)", "Still building…", "Prop ideas are not available right now.", "No players are running hot…".
- TESTED: `node odds-api/propideas.test.js` (label parsing incl. shuffled columns, the hot rule on Chase's and Johnston's real logs, thresholds, caps, missed-last-game, mid-week filtering, roll to next week, in-progress teams, carry-over by hand, each game fetched once, TTL, ESPN down, a failing game, restart from the saved rows, 405); `node odds-api/alert.test.js`; `node --check` on propideas.js and server.js; server.js booted locally on a test port: GET /propideas answered at once with building, then unavailable (the shell cannot reach ESPN), no crash or hang. Real ESPN shapes and the whole 2026 season were read through the owner's Chrome (own tab, read only) and run through the same `core()` source. App: Playwright (mirror mode, outside network blocked, /propideas mocked from the real ideas) at 430x900, 390x844 and 1280x900: collapsed/expanded, 8 cards, sheet and Back, ⓘ, Track as my bet pre-fill (game, player, stat set, line empty; saving with no line is refused by the existing form; with a line it saves a normal prop bet), building -> retry -> ready, unreachable, NCAA (no section, no fetch), no sideways scroll, banner covers nothing (tucked away once scrolled; the sheet is above it), box sits between the Alert and the week bar, 0 page errors.
- NOT TESTED / KNOWN LIMITS: the deployed route on Render (not deployed by the coder) and the cross-origin call from the real page; the Supabase write against the real table (fakes only); a real phone; the full `render()` path (the harness calls `newBoard` directly). ESPN lists a receiver only when he was targeted, so a game with no target shows as not played for a player with no other line. Injuries, role changes and the opponent are not looked at. Stat corrections after a game is final are not picked up (a finished game is read once). A traded player is left out until he has played his new team's latest game. `shown` in the payload is not on screen.
- Version v255: index.html (comment, header pill, footer "Oddable v255 (prop ideas)", trophy.js / gate.js / alert-frozen.json `?v=255`), sports.html `gate.js?v=255`, sw.js `pics-v255`, pickleball.html (comment, `pickleball.js?v=255`, footer). No Pickleball code changed.
