## v256 — Nov 16 2026: Team page (tap a team anywhere)
- Owner (Nov 16 2026), with a screenshot of another app's team page: "Can you add this. Touching the logo anywhere or the stats page."
- DECISIONS (Nov 16 2026): (1) Oddable's own layout in the app's existing look; the other app's layout, artwork and styling were not copied. (2) No new logos and no player photos (the roster ignores ESPN's headshots); the page header is the team's colour, not a logo. (3) NFL only: `tpAt` / `tmA` / `tpOpen` do nothing in NCAA mode. ESPN's college endpoints were not checked field by field, and the spread record and "Our picks" use NFL-only data (trophy-season.json, the Alert). (4) The page adds what the other app does not have: the record against the spread and every Alert pick the team was in. (5) No game screen was built: a game row opens the game the way the app already does. (6) News is headline links only, credited to ESPN, opening espn.com in a new tab; no article text.
- HOW IT OPENS: any element with `data-team="ABBR"` opens the page. ONE capture-phase click listener on `document` handles them all and stops the event, so the card / row / summary underneath does not also fire (chart toggle, drill sheet, details open, bet form). Helpers: `tpAt(abbr)` (attribute for a block) and `tmA(abbr, innerHtml)` (a `span.tmt`, dotted underline). Tap height: a `::after` box makes every target at least 40 px tall without moving the layout (spans: 40 px band; card team blocks: 8 px larger each way).
- WIRED: Games board card team block, logo + abbreviation + record (`nCard`); Live page card team block, logo + abbreviation (`.gtm`); 🏟 Field view header abbreviations (`fvHtml`); 📣 Alert banner rows and the "Not picked" line (`alertRow`, `alertBox`); 🤝 Helper row and its season sheet (`helperRow`); Alert drill sheets from the banner and from the Stats / Results season tiles (`alertRow`, `alertRowSu`); Lock lists in Results and their drill sheet (`trophyBox`, `trDrill`); division road dog sheets (`divDrill`); Results tab game rows (the `details.res` summary); Best tab ranking rows (`weekBoard`) and the full game card team names (`card`); the division table inside the team page.
- SKIPPED ON PURPOSE: win % buttons and the field-bar end zones on Live cards (they are part of the card's own tap / too small); the pick breakdown sheet `lkDetail` and `divOpen` text (sentences, and they have "Open full game"); 🧪 Injury lab rows on the Stats tab (a test tool with its own controls); My Bets cards, bet forms and game pickers (a tap there must pick a game or side); Survivor page (tapping a team picks it); Multiview tiles; line-move and arbitrage boards; the team dropdown / search; the old-look rail cards and `classic.html`; `sports.html` (other sports).
- THE PAGE (`#teampage`, z-index 350: above Multiview 250 and the info sheet 300, far above the My Bets banner 40, which it covers completely while open): header in the team colour with ‹ Back and ✕, name, record and division place ("7-2 · 1st AFC North"). ‹ Back returns to exactly where the user was: the page sits on top of whatever was open, so a sheet underneath is still there; a team opened from the division table is pushed on a stack (`TPS`) and Back pops it; ✕ closes everything; Escape = Back. Tabs wrap in two rows on a phone (no sideways scroll):
  - Games (default): summary card (straight up, against the spread, home, away) and one row per week: week + date, vs/@ opponent, W/L and score or kickoff time, the team's own spread with ✓ / ✗ / push; bye week marked. Spread = `tpLine(g)`: trophy-season.json closing line for started games, else ESPN's line, else this phone's line log (same order as `trophyData`). A row opens the game: live -> Field view (`fvOpen`), finished -> Results (`lkOpenGame`), upcoming -> the board (`openGame`).
  - Our picks: every Alert item of the team's games from `alertAll()` (🔒 Lock, picked 🏠 road dogs), `divDogs()` not picked, and `helperAll()`, each labelled "the pick was X" or "the pick was against X", with the result; upcoming ones included. One line when there are none.
  - Stats: 14 season rows, team and opponents side by side (points, total / passing / rushing yards per game, yards per rush, TDs, interceptions, sacks, 3rd down %, giveaways, takeaways, turnover margin, penalties). ESPN's feed has no league rank, so none is shown (said on screen).
  - Standings: the division table (W-L-T, pct, division record, PF, PA), this team highlighted, other teams tappable.
  - Roster: offense / defense / special teams / injured reserve or out; number, name, position; no photos. A player on this week's prop ideas list shows "🎯 prop idea ›", which opens the v255 idea sheet above the page (`tpIdea`); closing it lands back on the roster.
  - News: up to 10 ESPN headlines as links (only https espn.com links are accepted).
- DATA (ESPN public, fetched by the phone, shapes read through the owner's Chrome on Nov 16 2026): `SB + 'teams/{id}/schedule'` (only when the phone has fewer than 17 of the team's games in `ALL`; otherwise the schedule and the bye come from `ALL`), `SB + 'teams/{id}/roster'` (athletes[].position + items[]), `SB + 'teams/{id}/statistics'` (results.stats.categories[].stats[] and results.opponent[]), `SB + 'news?team={id}&limit=10'`, and `https://site.api.espn.com/apis/v2/sports/football/nfl/standings?level=3` (conference > division > standings.entries[], already in rank order). The team id comes from the existing `TEAMID` map (filled by `parseEvents`). `tpGet` has a 12 s timeout; each tab is fetched when first opened and kept 10 minutes (`TPC`, `TPST`); "Loading…" then either the data or "not available right now. Try again". `teams/{id}` itself is not called: its `standingSummary` read "2nd in American Football Conference" for a division leader, so the place is worked out from the standings instead.
- ALSO: `piTrack` now closes the team page (an idea opened from a roster). No pick, record, unit or Alert changed.
- AGAINST-THE-SPREAD CHECK BY HAND (independent Python over /tmp/nv/games.csv, weeks 1-9, its own grading): CIN straight up 6-2, against the spread 4-3-1 (push at MIA, -7, won by 7); BUF 7-1 and 6-2-0. The page shows BUF 7-1 / 6-2 and CIN 7-2 / 5-3-1 with week 10 added (PIT 18 at CIN 38, CIN -3.5 covered) = the same 4-3-1 through week 9.
- TESTED (Playwright, mirror mode, outside network blocked, ESPN mocked from the real shapes; 430x900 and 1280x900): opened from a Games card, an Alert banner row, inside a sheet (Back returns to the same sheet), a clickable row inside a sheet (the row's own tap does not fire), a Results row and a Best row (do not toggle), a Live card (chart does not toggle), the Field view header (Back returns to the Field view), and the division table (Back returns to the first team); every tab ready, loading and failed, plus Try again; bye from ESPN's schedule (CIN) and worked out on the phone with no schedule call (BUF); a team with several Alert items and the no-items line; roster 🎯 -> idea sheet -> back on the roster; game row -> Results; Escape; NCAA mode (nothing wired, nothing opens). BEFORE / AFTER on the same script against v255 and v256: Watch, Field view, Add bet on a Games card; Alert and Helper drill sheets; Live card chart toggle, pin and Field view; Results and Best rows opening; Lock row -> breakdown: identical. No sideways scroll on any tab, the banner is fully under the page, tap areas 40 px+, 0 page errors.
- NOT TESTED / LIMITS: the live site and a real phone; real ESPN responses inside the app (shapes were read in a browser tab, the app ran on mocks); the opponents column of the stats feed was confirmed to exist but its numbers were not read; stat names for 3rd down %, giveaways, takeaways, penalties and turnover margin were read from the real feed, the margin's name only as a prefix (a missing stat just drops its row). The record in the header comes from the games on the phone, so it can lag ESPN by a refresh. Team names on cards carry no visual hint that they can be tapped (the inline names have a dotted underline).
- Version v256: index.html (comment, header pill, footer "Oddable v256 (team pages)", trophy.js / gate.js / alert-frozen.json `?v=256`), sports.html `gate.js?v=256`, sw.js `pics-v256`, pickleball.html (comment, `pickleball.js?v=256`, footer). No server change in v256.

## Groups go-live — independent re-review of 1e47e93 (Nov 16 2026 evening): SHIP AFTER FIXES
Reviewer's scripts, logs and screenshots: /tmp/rev-groups (may not survive the container). Server core holds (time lock, bet lock, access, cap of 8, pot arithmetic to the cent, no payment/message/order path, old phone + new phone sync without loss, rollback leaves data usable). All round-3 fixes reproduced.
- BLOCKERS before even the owner-only launch:
  - B1: `gate()` in pics/groups.js asks the Groups server before keeping EVERY new bet from every signed-in user; if the call fails and the phone never had a good answer the bet is refused ("Couldn't reach your group"). Fix: refuse only when the phone last knew the player was in a group; never-knew = keep as personal; shorten the 20 s wait. An owner-only switch must answer bet/status with 200 "not in a group", never 403.
  - B2: G16 (decided: every bet counts for the group) — the confirm step still offers "Just for me". Remove it for anyone with an active contest (index.html, sports.html; classic.html has no group step: hide Add bet for group members there or add the step). Server should answer "no contest can count this, kept as personal" instead of locking a bet no contest covers (other sport, before the start, over the weekly limit). Do not make sync reject non-group bets.
  - B3: no owner-only switch exists; every /groups/* route is open to any signed-in account. Add env `GROUPS_OWNER_ONLY=1` checked at the top of groups.js handle(): allow the owner PIN account, the owner's phone account and a short tester list; everyone else gets "not in a group" / invite "not valid" / refused. Phone shows Groups only when the server says it is on for this account. Note: with owner-PIN sign-in only he can create/join but not place a group bet; he must sign in with phone + PIN to test bets.
  - B4: 1e47e93 cannot merge to main as is (conflicts in pics/index.html, sports.html, HANDOFF.md; same cache name). Port, new version + cache name, re-run suites and browser checks on the merged result.
- HIGH before the public launch: H1 moneyline with no ESPN odds pays even money (a 30-point favourite paid +100); H2 honor-system contests accept any win amount from the phone; H3 one failed index write strands an approved member; H4 a member not marked paid is shown as owed a prize; H5 a late bet is told "Saved to your group. Locked." when ESPN is down.
- MEDIUM/LOW and the real-system checks (two real calls racing for a seat, Supabase 1,000-row cap on the 5,000-row player read, real ESPN postponed/cancelled shapes, real phone cross-site sign-in, cold start, a real Kalshi card) are in the reviewer's report; carry them into the public-launch checklist.
- Five-tab bar: not in 1e47e93; it is in preview's index.html + integrate.js, whose "More" sheet lists the other engines with no "Coming soon", defaults the server address to the page's own (wrong on live) and calls preview-only /integrate/attach. Needs adapting when it comes in.

## v257 — Nov 16 2026: several bet screenshots at once (My Bets)
- Owner (Nov 16 2026): "Allow for multiple pic uploads for multiple bets."
- **What it does.** My Bets → ＋ Add bet → "📷 Upload bet screenshots (one or many)" now takes several pictures (`multiple`, still `accept="image/*"`). One picture = one slip (a single bet or a parlay). ONE picture with no list open behaves exactly as before (straight to the pre-filled form). Two or more (or one more while a list is open) go to a list above the bet form: read one after another ("Reading slip 2 of 5…"), one wrapped card per picture with a thumbnail, what was read (pick, line, type, legs, stake, odds/multiplier, payout) and a status: **Ready** / **Needs a look** / **Looks like a duplicate** / **Could not read** / **Saved ✓**. "＋ Add more pictures" adds to the same list.
- **Buttons.** Review & save = the same pre-filled form the one-picture import shows (bar on top: "Slip 2 of 5 · ← Back to the list"); after Save the card says Saved ✓ and the list is back. Skip removes a card. Could-not-read cards: Try again / Enter by hand / Skip. "Save all ready (n)" saves every Ready slip through `betSave` / `parlaySave` and reports "Saved 3, 2 need a look". Clear saved, Close list (asks first if unsaved slips would be dropped). NOTHING is saved by reading; only by a save button.
- **One reader, not two.** `betImport` was split, not copied: `slipRead(file, opt)` reads one picture (AI `/ai/slipjson` → AI text `/ai/slip` → on-device Tesseract, same order and messages as before) and returns the result; `slipApply` hands it to the form (old behaviour). The matchers were split the same way: `slipSingleBF(s)` (was the body of `betApplySlip`) and `slipMultiBF(s)` (was the body of `betApplyMulti`) return the form state; `betApplySlip` / `betApplyMulti` are now thin wrappers, so paste-text and `#slip=` links are unchanged. The list keeps each slip's form state as its draft. New: `betImportFiles`, `SQ` (in-memory list), `sqAdd`, `sqPump`, `sqReadOne`, `sqKey` / `sqBetKey` / `sqDup`, `sqIssues`, `sqReady`, `sqCard`, `sqHtml`, `sqPaint` (fills `#sqbox`, called at the end of `betsRender`), `sqReview`, `sqHand`, `sqBack`, `sqReviewEnd` (called at the top of `betsRender` when the form opened from a slip has closed: a new bet = Saved ✓, none = back to the list), `sqSaveAll`, `sqSkip`, `sqRetry`, `sqClear`, `sqSaved`.
- **Ready means:** game matched on this page's board AND a stake AND odds/multiplier were read (parlay: 2+ legs, every leg matched, no flagged line, payout/multiplier consistent). Anything else is Needs a look with the reason in words (no stake read; no odds read, it would save as −110; a leg not matched; a prop/total you grade yourself; game not on this football board).
- **Batch limit: 10 unsaved slips at a time** (`SQMAX`). Why: (1) the AI route allows 40 calls an hour per IP across slip reading AND Ask AI (`limited()` in odds-api/ai.js); a picture costs 1 call, 2 when the backup text reader is needed, so 10 pictures is 10 to 20 of the 40; (2) phone memory: each picture is decoded to a canvas up to 1800 px and the on-device reader is heavy. More than 10 chosen: the first that fit are taken and a plain message says how many were left out and why.
- **Pacing.** Strictly one picture at a time, 1.2 s gap between pictures (`SQ.gap`). If the AI answers 429: wait 5 s (`SQ.wait`), try that picture once more; still 429 → read it on the phone (Tesseract) and stop calling the AI for 2 minutes (`SQ.pause`), so the rest of the batch goes to the phone reader without burning calls; cards read that way say "Read on this phone… check it closely". The server's limit is a rolling hour, so waiting longer in the app would not help. A refused (429) call is not counted by the server. Other AI errors fall through to the backup reader exactly as the one-picture flow does. `aiSlipData` now puts the HTTP status on its error (message unchanged).
- **Duplicate rule.** (a) Same file chosen twice (name + size + lastModified) → not read again ("Read it anyway" overrides). (b) Same content as an earlier slip in the list, or as a bet already in My Bets: single = same game + side + type + line + stake (same game means same day); parlay = same set of legs + stake; prop = same words + stake + day. Marked "Looks like a duplicate", left out of Save all ready (re-checked before each save, so two copies can never both go in), but Review & save still works for a real second bet.
- **Decisions.** Drafts live in memory only (pictures never go to localStorage); the list survives leaving My Bets and coming back, and is gone after a reload (the help line under the list says so); nothing is half-saved because every save is one normal `betSave`/`parlaySave`/`propSave` call. Thumbnails are object URLs, revoked on Save / Skip / Clear / Close. Saves go ONLY through the normal save functions, so a later "group" step on saving cannot be bypassed here. A slip whose game is not on the football board (NBA etc.) is NOT looked up while reading (that lookup, `slipSports`, saves straight away as it always has); its card says so and the button is labelled "Find game & save". Props/totals are never Ready. `betNewId()` replaces `'b' + Date.now()` in betSave / parlaySave / propSave / slipSports so two saves in the same millisecond cannot share an id (same id format).
- **Left single on purpose.** `classic.html` (older look): its import is on-device Tesseract only (no AI reader, no pacing), one picture; unchanged. `sports.html` has no screenshot import. Survivor screenshot import (`svShot`) untouched. Paste slip text and `#slip=` links: one slip, unchanged.
- **Tests** (Playwright, 390x900, 430x900, 1280x900; AI reader mocked by picture, ESPN blocked, on-device Tesseract really ran; screenshots read): one-picture flow compared with v256 for 8 mock slips (clean single, parlay, missing stake, unmatched game, unreadable, 429, duplicate, total/prop): same form state, same message, same AI calls. Five pictures → five cards (Ready, Ready, Needs a look, Needs a look, Could not read); progress 1..5 of 5; never two reads at once; list still there after Games and back mid-read; second go of four more (429 then success = one retry then Ready; duplicate of an existing bet; same bet as slip 1; same file twice not re-read). Review & save = same pre-filled form as v256, Back saves nothing, Save → Saved ✓ and the bet is in My Bets once; saved single and parlay are field-for-field identical to ones saved by the one-picture flow. Save all ready saved exactly 2 of 9, "Saved 2, 6 need a look.", unique ids. Skip, Try again, Enter by hand + Cancel, a duplicate saved on purpose, Clear saved, reload (list gone, bets intact). 12 chosen → 10 taken with the message; full list refuses more; skipping the slip being read does not disturb the rest; 1.4 s between AI calls. AI stuck at 429 → 2 tries then no more AI calls, all slips read on the phone. A non-image file fails in plain words and the next slip is still read. No sideways scroll, tap targets ≥ 40 px, My Bets banner covers nothing, 0 page errors.
- **NOT verified.** The real AI reader and its real 429; a real phone's photo picker (iOS shows "Photo Library" multi-select for `multiple`; not tried); real bet-slip photos; the deployed site. **HEIC**: iPhone screenshots are PNG and iOS normally hands a web page JPEG for library photos, but if a HEIC file does arrive, Chrome/Android cannot decode it: the card says "This picture could not be opened… take a screenshot of the slip and upload that" and the rest of the batch carries on (tested with a fake .heic file only). Safari can decode HEIC itself; the server accepts only png/jpeg/webp/gif and the app always sends a JPEG made from the canvas, so that path should work but was not run.

## Golf go-live — owner priority and independent re-review of 2fa88eb (Nov 16 2026): SHIP AFTER FIXES
- OWNER (Nov 16 2026, 7:20 PM CT): "the golf is the first one i want completed of the list" = after Groups (which Golf lives in), Golf goes before Calcutta, local events and the full Stats dashboard.
- Reviewer's scripts/logs/screenshots: /tmp/rev-golf (may not survive the container). Scoring maths verified against brute force (stroke allocation +10..54 on 9/18, WHS course handicap, skins with carry-overs over 400 random rounds, match play, Stableford, best ball net, payouts to the cent over 2,000 random fields, a full 8-player money day). All previous-review findings fixed (20/20 on 2fa88eb vs 18/20 wrong on 544803e). No charge/pay/message/order code.
- Blockers before PUBLIC launch: B1 a playing organizer can dispute and re-settle a rival's confirmed hole alone and finish the round; B2 organizer + her own partner can overwrite and confirm the other team's whole card; B3 the "confirmed" handicap can be farmed by one person against a typed-in guest (only once Stats is in the build).
- High: H1 self-typed handicap slips into a money match added afterwards; H2 marking the leader "not paid" mid-round is silent; H3 a playing organizer can mark a finished confirmed leader "did not finish"; H4 reopening the app with no signal loses the scorecard (keep the last-opened game on the phone); H5 finished pick-six pools depend on ESPN forever (store final standings; require a non-empty field); H6 one-and-done stores a bad first "final" reading for good; H7 nobody can be removed from a game.
- Medium/Low: Enterprise golf days need Stats (keep Enterprise off for Golf); ten groups writing at once got 54/720 "Busy" (no loss; add jittered retry); whole game row rewritten per score (43 KB at 8 players, 153 KB at 40); 900 reads/10 min per address; "Side games" tab overflows under ~416 px (existing phone checks ran at 430 only); 9-hole handicaps off by one for 91 of 361 index values (double rounding); skins vanish when a team withdraws (owner rule question).
- SMALLEST SHIPPABLE GOLF ("Golf alone", ported file by file from 2fa88eb, NOT preview; golf-v1 cannot be merged): server golf.js, golf-score.js, golf-feed.js, golf-courses.js, ratings.js, pairing.js, fixtures/courses-fallback.json + the /golf/ line in server.js; phone pics/golf.js, menu item, script tag, #ogolf reload guards in index.html and pics/sync.js. Hard requirements: the Groups-era acct.js exports (who, isOwnerPhone, ownerName: arrive with Groups Phase 1) and sync.js dbGet/dbPut/dbPatchIf. Without stats.js nobody has an index and the organizer sets/OKs every number (recommended first build; B3 cannot happen). Switch off / "Coming soon": Golfer Calcutta, Bets vs the group, Team Calcutta line, pro pools until ESPN golf is verified against a real tournament, Groups hub "Add a golf contest", Enterprise.
- Before even an owner-only launch: port as Golf alone; run the live checks (real database two-phone same-hole write, real sign-in, cross-origin from a phone, ESPN golf scoreboard/leaderboard shapes during a tournament week, OpenGolfAPI course search for a course the owner plays, a full round across a host sleep, a real phone at 375-390 px); tell the owner about H4 before his first on-course test.
- OWNER RULE QUESTIONS (unanswered): 1 one-and-done billable per season or per tournament; 2 prize ties split or count-back; 3 skins validation and net skins full/half strokes; 4 withdrawn player's skins kept or back in play; 5 rain-shortened money game: pay on 9 or no payout; 6 may a playing organizer settle other teams' disputes; 7 organizer override of a player's Oddable index; 8 heads-up amount per side or per player; 9 tee time delayed a day: reopen picks or stay locked; 10 flight-boundary ties alphabetical or organizer's choice; 11 conceded holes in match play.

## v258 — Nov 16 2026: menus instead of pills + Standings, Team stats, Season (NFL)
- Owner (Nov 16 2026): "Can we change the pills to menus and add standing… team stats and season". His standing rule: dropdown pickers and wrapped cards, no sideways-scrolling rows on a phone.
- **What replaced the pills.** Both pill rows (the league row `.lg` in the header and the view row `#gvbar` under Disclosures) are gone from `index.html` and `sports.html`. In their place: one line in the header, `.mrow`, with two real `<select>` menus side by side, each with a small caption: **League** (`#lgsel`: 🏈 NFL, 🎓 NCAA, 🏓 Pickleball, 🏀 NBA, ⚾ MLB, 🏒 NHL, 🏀 NCAAB, the same seven in the same order) and **View** (`#gvsel`). The "Search team…" field and the conference picker stay on the next line. Same layout on desktop (menus capped at 260 px each). Real selects were chosen over a custom menu: the phone's own picker, Escape / tap-outside for free, nothing to position, and an open menu is drawn by the system so the My Bets banner cannot cover it. Menus are 44 px tall.
- **League menu** `lgPick(s)`: NFL / NCAA store `pics-lg` and reload (sports.html: store and go to `./`), Pickleball goes to `pickleball.html`, NBA / MLB / NHL / NCAAB go to `sports.html?lg=` through `oddWarn` exactly as the pills did. The menu is set back to the league the page is on before anything else happens (and on `pageshow`), so a cancelled warning or the Back button never leaves it on the wrong league.
- **View menu** `gvMenu()` (called from `renderInner` and once at startup so it is never empty): options from `GVS`, value follows `GV`, change calls the same `gvSet`. `pics-gv` / `pics-gv-sp` are stored as before; the bottom bar (Winning -> Best odds) and `lkOpenGame` (-> Results) still move the view and the menu follows. The View menu (`#gvlab`) is hidden where the pill row was hidden: Live, My Bets, Survivor, and the old look (`pics-style=classic`). `plbFit` now measures `header .mrow` (was `header .lg`) for the banner tuck-away. The old `.lg` / `.gvbar` CSS is left in place, unused.
- **classic.html**: not changed. Its first line is `location.replace("index.html")`, so nobody sees its pill row. No deep links to a view existed (views are remembered in localStorage only); none were added.
- **Three new views (NFL only)**, keys `stand`, `tstats`, `season` (`NVK`), drawn into `#list` like Stats / Results:
  - **Standings** `nvStandHtml()`: eight division cards grouped under AFC / NFC (Team, W-L-T, Pct, Div, PF, PA, Strk), and a two-button toggle "By division / Conference seeds" (`nvMode`, remembered in `pics-nvm`) that shows each conference as one list of 16 in ESPN's `playoffSeed` order with a "Playoff line" row after seed 7. All seven columns fit at 390 px, so none is dropped; the team nickname is added from 560 px up. No toggle if the feed has no seeds, no Strk column if it has no streak. A row has `data-team`, so the existing listener opens the team page. Data: the team page's own fetch and cache (`tpStand()` / `TPST`, 10 minutes, 12 s timeout); no second fetcher. New `tpStAll()` parses the whole feed and `tpDiv()` now reads from it (same result).
  - **Team stats** `nvStatsHtml()`: a "Rank by" dropdown (`nvStat`, remembered in `pics-nvs`) and all 32 teams ranked (equal values share a rank), with each team's W-L and its record against the spread from `tpRec(tpGames().map(tpRow))`, the same lines the team page uses (shipped closing line, then ESPN's, then the phone's log). A row opens the team page on its Stats tab (`nvTeam(a,'stats')`). Stats used, all from ONE league-wide request `https://site.api.espn.com/apis/common/v3/sports/football/nfl/statistics/byteam` (`NVTS`, through the same `tpNeed` / `tpGet`): points per game, points allowed per game, total yards per game, passing yards per game, rushing yards per game, yards allowed per game, turnover margin, takeaways, giveaways, 3rd down %, sacks by the defense, sacks allowed; plus "Record against the spread" (cover %), which needs no feed. The feed gives each category twice per team (`splitId` 0 = the team, 900 = its opponents) and values by position (`categories[].names` gives the index): read that way in `nvStatVals`. A stat the feed does not carry simply drops out of the dropdown. While the feed is loading or has failed the view still lists the spread records it already holds, with "Loading team stats…" or "Team stats not available right now. Try again" (`nvRetry`); a feed with fewer than 32 teams says "n of 32" and puts the rest at the bottom.
  - **Season** `nvSeasonHtml()`: "Team schedule" dropdown (32 teams) opens that team's page on the Games tab (`nvTeamPick`); "Week" dropdown (18 weeks, opens on the current week, `NVW` in memory only) shows that week as wrapped game cards: final score with the winner bold plus the closing line and who covered, kickoff time and the line for games to come, LIVE for a game in progress, and a Bye line. A card opens the game with the existing `tpGame`. It is deliberately not the Games board's ◀ ▶ week bar: the board is one week of pick cards and keeps its own week (`WKV`, untouched); this is the plain scoreboard for any week with no picks on it.
- **Decisions (Nov 16 2026).** (1) NFL only: college standings come as 11 conferences with no divisions and different stat names, and the team page does not open in NCAA mode, so the three views are not in the NCAA View menu; a remembered one falls back to Games there and returns on NFL. (2) Over/under records left out: ESPN's scoreboard carries no odds for games that have started (0 of 15 in week 5, read Nov 16), and trophy-season.json holds spreads only. (3) "Total yards per game" uses `netYardsPerGame` (passing after sack yardage + rushing, the official figure). (4) Spread records are labelled as facts about results, not picks. (5) No pick, record, unit or Alert number changed. (6) Other sports on sports.html got the menus only.
- **OPEN, found while building:** the team page's Stats tab (v256) labels `yardsPerGame` "Total yards per game"; in the league feed that field is the figure before sack yardage (BUF 399.7 against 386.7 net), so the team page and the new ranking can differ by a few yards for the same label. Not changed here (it is a number already shown): decide which one to keep. Also sports.html's header pill still reads v127 and its footer v140 (not on the bump list).
- **Feed shapes** read through the owner's Chrome on Nov 16 2026 (own tab, closed): standings stats include `playoffSeed`, `streak`, `divisionRecord`, `pointsFor`, `pointsAgainst`, `winPercent`; the byteam feed answers 200 from a non-ESPN origin (example.com) with no parameters on both `site.api.espn.com` and `site.web.api.espn.com`, 32 teams, current season.
- **Tests** (Playwright, mirror mode, service workers blocked, ESPN mocked in the real shapes with the real Nov 16 standings and team stats and the season's real scores; 390x844, 430x900, 1280x900, new views also 768):
  - Before / after, same script on v257 and v258: 64 of 64 captures identical at each width. NFL and NCAA, all six old views (text of the page compared), `pics-gv` / `pics-lg` stored, view and league after a reload, bottom bar and `lkOpenGame`, Live / My Bets / Survivor pages, the old look, Pickleball and each of NBA / MLB / NHL / NCAAB reached, the warning's Cancel and Continue with the real gate.js, sports.html's four views and its league items, classic.html landing on index.html. NCAA ran on an empty college scoreboard, so that comparison is thin.
  - New views: ready, loading (answer held), failed + Try again (one request, no retry loop), partial feed (20 of 32), feed without seeds, a live game. All 32 standings rows and all 32 spread records equal an independent Python calculation from /tmp/nv/games.csv plus week 10 (BUF 8-1-0 .889 1-1 287 199 W5, 7-2 vs spread; NYG 5-3-1 .611 2-1-1 203 196 L1, 6-3; DAL 3-6-1). Byes for weeks 5, 10, 11 equal ESPN's `teamsOnBye`. Team tap -> team page -> Back / Escape returns to the same view, layout, stat, week and scroll position. One standings request shared with the team page; one team-stats request. No sideways scroll, nothing clipped inside a card, tap targets 40 px+, the My Bets banner over nothing at any scroll position, 0 page errors.
- **NOT verified.** The live site and a real phone (how iOS / Android draw the two selects, and emoji in the options); the app against the real ESPN feeds end to end (shapes were read in a browser tab, the app ran on mocks; in the mock the net yards figure is passing + rushing, the real field was seen by name only); a real tie-break in the seed list (ESPN's order is shown as given); week 10 scores in the mock were worked out from ESPN's points for / against, not read; playoffs / week 19+ are not in the Season view.
- **Follow-up:** standings / team stats for NBA, MLB, NHL, NCAAB on sports.html and for NCAA football.
- Version v258: index.html (comment, header pill, footer "Oddable v258 (menus · standings · team stats · season)", trophy.js / gate.js / alert-frozen.json `?v=258`), sports.html `gate.js?v=258`, sw.js `pics-v258`, pickleball.html (comment, `pickleball.js?v=258`, footer). No server change.

## v259 — Nov 16 2026: 📍 Find local courts (Pickleball page)
- WHY: Steve (Nov 16 2026): "On the pickle ball… I don't see the search local courts", "Free for now in my app allow for change to google where i can add the api", then "INSTEAD i would rather it be in my apps settings for oddable". So: free (OpenStreetMap) by default, Google Places when he switches it on, and the switch + key live in My Apps settings for Oddable (env vars are the fallback).
- **Phone** (`pics/pickleball.js`, `pics/pickleball.html`): a "📍 Find local courts" card is the FIRST thing on the Pickleball page (above the points-only notice). It opens `#/courts`: City or ZIP box, Distance dropdown (5 / 15 / 30 miles), Search, Use my location (browser location is asked for only on that tap; denied = a plain message, no request sent). Results are wrapped cards (name, miles, address, tags, Open in Maps); tags only when the map says so: Indoor / Outdoor, Lights / No lights, "N courts" (tagged) or "N courts on the map" (courts counted from the map), Free / Fee, Customers / Members only, Shared with tennis. Source line under the list: "Courts from OpenStreetMap contributors" (links to openstreetmap.org/copyright, required) or "Courts from Google", plus "can be missing, closed or out of date". Loading, empty and failed states; Try again repeats the one request once (no loop). Last search (text + distance, never coordinates) is kept in localStorage `pb1-courts`. Location is rounded to 3 decimals before it is sent. Server URL constant `CTU = 'https://calcutta-odds.onrender.com/courts'`, the same way index.html names its odds-server URLs. Names from the server are escaped; a maps link that is not `https://www.google.com/maps/…` is rebuilt from lat/lon. The page still has no fixed or floating element (no My Bets banner here), so nothing can be covered. Footer now reads "events saved on this device only" (the court search is the page's one server call).
- **Server** (`odds-api/courts.js`, test `odds-api/courts.test.js`, route in `server.js` next to `/propideas`): `GET /courts?q=<city or ZIP>` or `?lat=&lon=`, optional `&r=` miles (default 15, clamped 1-50). Junk = 400 and no outside call. 12 searches a minute per IP (ip.js). Answer: `{ok, provider, fallback?, center:{lat,lon,label}, radius, courts:[{name, address, lat, lon, miles, courts?, courtsFrom?, indoor?, lit?, fee?, access?, shared?, unnamed?, website?, maps}], count, source, asOf, note?, stale?}`, sorted by distance, at most 60. Errors: 404 place not found, 503 place lookup down / busy, 502 court map down, 429.
  - OSM (default): Nominatim for the place (User-Agent `Oddable/1.0 (pickleball court finder; https://steves-pics.onrender.com)`, one request at a time at least 1.1 s apart, kept 1 day, a miss kept 1 hour, 5-digit ZIP sent as a US postal code), then Overpass (`overpass-api.de`, then the `overpass.kumi.systems` mirror) for `nwr["sport"~"pickleball",i]` (covers sport=pickleball, pitches and sports centres whose sport list has pickleball) plus `pickleball=yes`. `access=private|no` is left out. Courts are usually mapped one by one, so everything within 60 m of a neighbour is ONE place (a row of courts chains together) and the court count is the number of mapped courts unless a `courts` tag on a pickleball-only place says otherwise. A timed-out Overpass answer is a failure, not "no courts". Nothing is invented: none found = `count:0` + a note.
  - Google: Places API (New) Text Search `places:searchText`, "pickleball courts", restricted to the box around the point, key in the `X-Goog-Api-Key` header (never a URL). Any Google failure = OpenStreetMap answer with `provider:"osm", fallback:true` and a note; Google is tried again after 10 minutes.
  - Only a point rounded to 0.01 degrees goes to OSM / Google; distances are worked out from the exact point. Lists are cached 6 hours per rounded point + radius + provider (300 entries); if the map goes down an old list is served with `stale:true`.
  - **Provider and key, in this order:** (a) My Apps `POST /api/v1/app/settings` `{slug, secret}` -> `{ok:true, settings:{courts_provider, google_places_key}}` through `acct.js` `ma()` (now exported, with `maReady()`; same `MYAPPS_URL` / `MYAPPS_SECRET`), read at most every 5 minutes, 6 s limit, a failed read retried after 1 minute, last good answer kept while My Apps is down; (b) env `COURTS_PROVIDER` (`osm` or `google`) and `GOOGLE_PLACES_API_KEY` on the odds server; (c) osm. DECISION (coder, for the manager to confirm): the two values fall back separately, so My Apps "google" with an empty key uses the env key, and a My Apps answer with both empty means env. Google is used only when the provider says google AND a key exists.
  - The key never leaves the server: not in a response, not in a log line (all logs and the response go through `scrub()`), not in a URL. Searches are not logged.
- TESTED: `node odds-api/courts.test.js` (fakes for Nominatim, Overpass, Google, My Apps, clock): validation (19 junk requests = 400, no outside call), OSM path, grouping / de-dup, caches (6 h list, 1 day geocode, in-flight sharing, 1 s Nominatim spacing), empty, place not found, geocoder down, Overpass down / mirror / timed out / stale list, settings from My Apps vs env vs default (My Apps down keeps last good; slow My Apps), Google path, 5 kinds of Google failure -> OSM fallback, key never in any response / log / URL (checked by breaking the scrub on purpose: the test fails), rate limit. alert / cfbd / propideas tests still pass. Real server booted locally: `/courts?q=` 400 with CORS header, POST 405, OPTIONS 204. Playwright (service workers blocked, `/courts` mocked, every other outside request blocked) at 390x844, 430x932 and 1280x900, 86 checks each (258, all pass): card first and on screen, no call until a tap, loading, results, long names wrap, HTML in a name stays text, empty, 5 failures, Try again sends exactly one request, Enter key, Google and fallback labels, location denied and granted, saved search after reload, storage blocked, nothing fixed / floating, no sideways scroll at top / middle / bottom, tap targets 40 px or more, 0 page errors, creating an event still works, index.html loads with v259. Screenshots were looked at.
- NOT TESTED / OPEN: (1) **No real data.** The build shell cannot reach Nominatim or Overpass (one try each, refused), so no real McAllen answer has been seen, and Google Places has never been called (no key). After the odds server deploys, open `https://calcutta-odds.onrender.com/courts?q=McAllen%2C%20TX&r=15` in a browser, then try it on the phone. To see the raw map data first: `https://overpass-api.de/api/interpreter?data=%5Bout%3Ajson%5D%5Btimeout%3A25%5D%3B(nwr%5B%22sport%22~%22pickleball%22%2Ci%5D(around%3A25750%2C26.2%2C-98.23)%3B)%3Bout%20center%20tags%20400%3B`. OSM coverage of pickleball in the Valley may be thin; the page says so rather than guessing. (2) **My Apps `/api/v1/app/settings` was not called for real** (only the agreed shape, faked). Until My Apps has that route the server gets a 404 and uses env / OSM. (3) Not tested cross-origin from the real page in Chrome (the v166 lesson): the call is a plain GET with no custom header, so no preflight, but check it live. (4) Google terms: Places content is not meant to be stored (only place ids, and lat/lng for 30 days), and results shown without a Google map need Google's attribution; the 6-hour list cache applies to Google lists too as the job asked. Decide before switching Google on whether to drop the cache for Google. (5) Overpass and Nominatim are free shared services with fair-use limits; fine at this size, not for heavy traffic. (6) The mirror `overpass.kumi.systems` is untested. (7) Render free services sleep: the first search can take up to a minute (the page says so; 70 s limit).
- Version v259: index.html (comment, header pill, footer "Oddable v259 (find local pickleball courts)", trophy.js / gate.js / alert-frozen.json `?v=259`), sports.html `gate.js?v=259`, sw.js `pics-v259`, pickleball.html (comment, `pickleball.js?v=259`, footer). Picks, locks and the Alert were not touched.

## v260 — Nov 16 2026: score pop-up with My Bets status, Today's bets cards, card colours from My Apps
- Owner (Nov 16 2026): "can we add pop up with a my bets status each Time a live game scores?"; then a lock-screen card from another app as the look ("Example by my bets. And scores"); then his day's bets in the same card even when nothing is live; then, on the mock-up ("Perfect"): keep the Oddable colours and make the colour scheme changeable from My Apps. In-app only: no push, no permission prompt, no lock-screen work.
- **The card** (`scCard(g, o)` in `pics/index.html`, one function for both uses; no other app's logo or branding): top row = the bet in short form ("LAC +4.5 · $50") and a status pill; middle = away team (abbreviation, name, a thin bar in the team colour), its score in large digits, quarter + clock ("2nd 1:56") with a short line under it, home score, home team; 🏈 on the team with the ball and timeout dots only when the feed has them (`g.sit.poss`, `sit.ato` / `sit.hto`); then the last-play text when the feed has it. Team abbreviations are NOT drawn in the team colour (many team colours fail contrast on a dark card): the colour is the bar under the name.
- **Status wording** (`scPill`, `scBets`): the result comes from the app's own grading (`betGrade` / `betGradeOne` / `legGrade`, which now also return the margin `m` they already computed; no grading rule changed). Live = "▲ covering by 3.5 right now" / "▼ not covering, 3.5 short right now" / "▲ winning by 7 right now" / "▼ losing by 7 right now" / "tied right now" / "▲ over the total by 2.5 right now" / "▼ needs 35 more right now" (over) / "▲ under the total by … right now". Final = "Won +$45.45" / "Lost −$20.00" / "Push" (`betPL`). Parlay leg = the leg's status + "3-leg parlay · 2 of 3 hitting" (the same count the My Bets parlay card shows; "parlay lost" once a leg has lost; final leg = "Leg won / lost / push"). Tracked player prop on the game = "N so far" from the box score the app already loads. "Live 88%" is shown ONLY on a moneyline bet and only when ESPN's feed carries the win chance (`g.sit.wp`); never computed. "Right now" is never called a result.
- **Score pop-up** (`#spop`, `spScan` → `spPump` → `spShow`): no new poller. `spScan(list, src)` is called with the games just read by the polls the app already runs: `tick()` (this league's scoreboard, every 60 s while a game is live) and `xlLoad()` / `xlSports()` (games from the other league or another sport that he has bets on, every 60 s). It compares each game with the last score seen (`SP.seen`).
  - First sight of a game = recorded silently, so opening the app never bursts. `visibilitychange` to hidden clears `SP.seen`, the queue and the card; nothing is scanned while hidden; the first poll after coming back is silent again.
  - Score up = pop-up ("Touchdown BUF +7" / "Field goal …" when the play text says so, else "Score BUF +3"). Score DOWN = "Score corrected" with the new score and the same bet lines. Live → final = one "Final" card with won / lost / push; a final game never pops again.
  - Queue: one card at a time, 5 s each (`SP.ms`), 0.3 s between; one poll with many changes shows 1 + at most 3 queued (`SP.max`), then one summary "+N more scores"; a game already waiting is replaced by its newer score, not stacked; anything older than 45 s is dropped.
  - CHOICE, fast-scoring sports: basketball bets saved from the sports page (`lg` nba / ncaab) pop only at the end of a quarter / half ("End of 1st", "Halftime", or the period number going up when a break was missed between polls) and at the final. Hockey and baseball bets pop on every score. `sports.html` itself (the NBA / MLB / NHL / NCAAB page) has NO pop-up: it is a separate older build with its own poll; its hidden copy of index.html (the banner mirror iframe) is skipped (`window.top !== window`).
  - Top row: the first bet on the game (singles, then parlay legs, then props) and "+N more bet(s) on this game" at the bottom. With "All live games" and no bet: the matchup label and "No bet on this game".
  - Tap: a game with a bet → My Bets; no bet → the game where the app already opens it (live NFL → Field view, otherwise `lkOpenGame`); the summary → the Live page. Open sheets / team page / Multiview are closed first. ✕ (44 px) dismisses. `role="status" aria-live="polite"`; fade only, none under reduced motion.
- **Setting** "Score pop-ups": My bets only (default) / All live games / Off. It is in ⓘ Disclosures in the header (where the version, account and look / colours switches are), `#spsel`, localStorage `pics-spop` (try/catch). "All live games" = every live game on the league he is on, plus games he has bets on elsewhere.
- **Placement** (owner's rule): the card sits just above the bottom tab bar, centred, at most 460 px wide, z-index 400 (above the team page 350 and sheets 300 / 360), and is `position:fixed`, so nothing on the page moves. Before it shows, and every 0.4 s while it is up, `spBlock()` checks what is under it: the My Bets banner, the tab bar, the header menus / buttons, and any button / link / field that is fixed UI (not inside a scroller that can scroll: an overlay's Back / ✕, a dialog's buttons). If one is there it steps above it; if there is no free place it waits (retry every 1.5 s) or takes itself down. Never shown over the sign-in gate. HONEST LIMIT: page content that scrolls (game cards, rows in a sheet) passes under the card for those 5 s the way it passes under the tab bar; `spRoom()` adds scroll room at the end of the page / sheet while the card is up so anything under it can be scrolled clear, and takes it back only when that cannot move the screen. On wide screens the tab bar is a rail on the left, so the card sits at the bottom edge.
- **Today's bets** (`tbOpen` / `tbInner` / `tbPaint`): tapping the floating My Bets banner now opens a sheet (the app's normal sheet: ‹ Back, ✕, tap outside) instead of the small drop-down list. CHOICE: the drop-down is replaced, not kept beside it (that list was the "expanded banner covers tabs / cards" open item; `#plbd` is still in the banner's HTML, hidden, because sports.html mirrors it). One card per game he has a bet on today, every bet on that game listed with its own pill. Order: live, then upcoming by kickoff, then finals. Not started = teams, kickoff in his local time, "Not started". Live = score, clock, status right now. Final = score and won / lost / push. "Today" is the same day rule the banner uses (`betDay`); a parlay shows on the cards of its legs that are played today. Bets with no game to follow (hand-typed props, a game not on the phone) are listed under "Other bets today". None = "No bets today." The open sheet repaints on each poll. It is a view he opens: nothing pops up by itself when no game is live.
- **Card colours** = CSS custom properties on the wrapper class `.bc`: `--bc-bg, --bc-border, --bc-text, --bc-dim, --bc-win-bg, --bc-win-text, --bc-lose-bg, --bc-lose-text, --bc-neutral-bg` (+ `--bc-neutral-text`). Presets: `.bc-oddable` (default: the navy / blue gradient of the header and banner), `.bc-black` (the mock-up), `.bc-light`, `.bc-green`. Every text / background pair of every preset is 6.4:1 or better (tested; 4.5:1 required). Card colours only: the rest of the app is not rethemed.
- **Where the preset comes from**: new PUBLIC route **`GET /appcfg`** on the odds server (`odds-api/appcfg.js`) → `{ok:true, card_theme:"oddable"}` and nothing else. Order: My Apps setting `card_theme`, else env **`CARD_THEME`** on calcutta-odds, else `oddable`; the value must be one of oddable / black / light / green. The My Apps read was factored out of courts.js into **`odds-api/appsettings.js`** (`get()`: POST `/api/v1/app/settings` through acct.js `ma()`, 5-minute cache, 6 s limit, a failed read retried after 1 minute, last good answer kept) and is now ONE shared read for the court finder and /appcfg. That settings object also holds `google_places_key`: /appcfg builds its answer field by field from a whitelist and never copies the object (unit-tested; breaking it on purpose makes the test fail). The phone (`bcLoad`) fetches /appcfg once per load (not in `?mirror=1`), keeps the value in localStorage `pics-cardth`, and on any failure keeps the saved or default colours with no message. Until My Apps has the `card_theme` field the answer is `oddable`.
- **Tests.** `node odds-api/appcfg.test.js` (default, every preset, whitelist with the key and extra fields in the settings, 12 junk values, env fallback in 4 modes, 5-minute cache + last good, one shared read with courts, slow My Apps, 405 / query string / other path); `courts.test.js` unchanged and passing, `propideas.test.js`, `alert.test.js` pass; server booted locally, `/appcfg` answered 200 with the CORS header. Playwright (mirror mode, service workers blocked, ESPN mocked, scores changed between real `tick()` / `xlSports()` calls) at 390x844, 430x932 and 1280x900: 160 checks each, 480 of 480 pass: first poll silent; score, content of every part of the card; auto-hide (about 5 s); the three settings + reload; correction; final; over / under, parlay leg; catch-up queue (5 games in one poll = 4 cards then "+1 more scores", 26 s); ✕; summary; page hidden and back; NBA (no pop on baskets, pop at "End of 1st", once for a missed break, final), NHL goal; every view (Games, Live, My Bets, Survivor at every scroll position incl. the bottom; team page; Field view; Today's bets sheet): on top, covers no banner / tab bar / header menu / Back / close / fixed button, fully on screen, no sideways scroll, page laid out the same with and without the card, everything under it can be scrolled clear; a dialog with buttons where the card would sit and one opening under a card already up; Today's bets (7 cards, order, not started / live / final, two bets on one game, parlay leg, other bets, hockey team not given a football name, ‹ Back, no bets); the four presets' contrast; /appcfg on a normal (non-mirror) load: fetched exactly once, preset applied and saved, server down keeps the saved one, junk ignored; reduced motion; 0 page errors. v259 against v260 with no live game, same script: 78 of 81 captures identical (Games and every View, Live, My Bets, Survivor, Winning, team page; text and positions); the 3 that differ are one position on the Winning view with ⓘ Disclosures held open, which is taller by the new setting. sports.html and pickleball.html load with 0 errors. Screenshots were looked at.
- **NOT verified / open.** The live site and a real phone; real ESPN feeds (the app ran on mocks in ESPN's shapes; the possession, timeout, last-play and win-chance fields are the ones the Field view already reads); the deployed `/appcfg` and its cross-origin call from the real page (plain GET, no custom header, so no preflight: check it live after the calcutta-odds deploy); My Apps `card_theme` (another coder is adding the field; only the agreed shape was faked). Polls are 60 s apart, so a card can trail the real score by up to a minute, and two scores inside one poll show as one card with the combined points. A basketball quarter break shorter than the poll can be reported a little late (it is then titled with the current clock). The Today's bets cards for other sports need the game on the phone (xlSports loads only games with bets). The "Each bet" drop-down under the banner is gone from index.html; sports.html still has its own. The setting lives in ⓘ Disclosures: decide whether it should also be on the My Bets page.
- Version v260: index.html (comment, header pill, footer "Oddable v260 (score pop-ups · today's bets cards)", trophy.js / gate.js / alert-frozen.json `?v=260`), sports.html `gate.js?v=260`, sw.js `pics-v260`, pickleball.html (comment, `pickleball.js?v=260`, footer). SERVER: appcfg.js, appsettings.js, courts.js and the `/appcfg` line in server.js need the calcutta-odds deploy (a push restarts it). Picks, locks, the Alert and the model were not touched.

## Groups Phase 1 (server, dark) — Nov 16 2026 (coder agent, branch `groups-phase1` 3e41813 from main f740005; merged into main after v260 with `git merge --no-ff`, see "Groups Phase 1 merged into main" below; NO app change, NO version bump in this entry)
Phase 1 of the plan above: the Groups SERVER code is on the main line, switched off. No phone file changed (`git diff origin/main -- pics/ public/` is empty), so this entry bumps no version and the service-worker cache name is untouched. (Corrected at the merge: this was written when main was v256. Main was **v260** when the branch was merged; v257-v260 shipped in between and the next app change after the merge is v261.) Until the manager sets a variable on Render, nothing a phone does changes: every existing route answers as before (checked side by side against main's server, below) and the bet sync stores exactly what main stores.

### The switches (Render env vars on the odds server: the service is NAMED "ODDABLE" in Render, its URL / slug is calcutta-odds = https://calcutta-odds.onrender.com; exact names; read on every request)
- `GROUPS_ENABLED` — the master switch. Only the exact value `1` turns Groups on. Unset (the default), or anything else = OFF for everybody, the owner included: every `/groups/*` call gets the blocked answers below, the 5-minute tick does nothing, no Groups row is read or written, and `sync.js` runs main's merge and main's player-list query exactly.
- `GROUPS_OWNER_ONLY` — only the exact value `1` means owner-only (and only matters when Groups is enabled). Allowed then: the owner-PIN sign-in, the owner's phone account (the `OWNER_PHONE` numbers, `acct.js isOwnerPhone`), and the testers below. Unset = Groups open to every signed-in account, as designed. Removing the variable opens Groups with no code change (it is one check at the top of `groups.js handle()`).
- `GROUPS_TESTERS` — comma-separated ACCOUNT KEYS, 24 hex characters each. Never a phone number or an email (anything that is not 24 hex characters is ignored; spaces and capitals are tolerated). An account key = `limits.keyOf(account id)` = a one-way hash of the id inside the sign-in token (the same key Enterprise accounts are listed under).
- How the owner finds a tester's key (owner only, `acct.ownerOk`: the owner-PIN token, or the owner's phone token plus the dashboard unlock header `x-dash`; needs `GROUPS_ENABLED=1`):
  - `POST /groups/access` `{"op":"find","q":"<the player's name in the dashboard | player tag (4-8 hex) | email>"}` -> `{found:[{key, name, tag, kind, listed}]}`.
  - `POST /groups/access` `{}` -> `{enabled, ownerOnly, testers, requests:[{key, name, kind, at, listed}]}`: the people who asked. A signed-in person asks with `POST /groups/access/request {"name":"..."}` (3 a day per account; saved in row `lock:gaccess`, newest 200). No phone screen calls it yet (Phase 2).
  - Example: `curl -s -X POST https://calcutta-odds.onrender.com/groups/access -H 'content-type: application/json' -H 'x-token: <owner token>' -d '{"op":"find","q":"<name>"}'` (an owner token comes from `POST /acct/owner {"pin":"<OWNER_PIN>"}`). Then put the key in `GROUPS_TESTERS` on Render and save (the service restarts).
- What a BLOCKED caller gets (Groups off, or owner-only and not on the list; signed in or not; nothing is read from the database for them):
  - `/groups/bet/status` -> HTTP 200 `{"betLock":0,"canBet":false,"saved":false}` = the normal "not in a group" answer. Never 401 / 403 / 404 / 500, even with a broken body or the database down (review blocker B1: a failing status call made preview phones refuse bets). `server.js` gives the same answer if `groups.js` itself cannot load while Groups is off.
  - `/groups/invite` and `/groups/preview` -> 404 "This invite link is not valid or has expired." (also for a real code).
  - `/groups/on` -> 200 `{"on":false}`. Everything else -> 403 `{"error":"Groups is not open for this account yet.","off":true}`.
- `GET or POST /groups/on` (send the sign-in token when there is one) -> `{on: true|false}`, `Cache-Control: private, max-age=300`, `Vary: x-token`. Phase 2 shows the Groups menu item only when it is true. No new request header (content-type, x-token, x-dash only; the CORS allow-list is unchanged). It is a GET with `x-token`, so the browser sends a preflight: server.js already allows GET. NOT tried from a real browser.
- Known and accepted: the owner-PIN sign-in can create / join / run groups but cannot place a group bet (it has no synced bets row); the owner tests bets with phone + PIN. A tester taken off the list keeps their seat and bets in the group but is told "not in a group" (new bets are personal).

### What was ported, and from where
- From 1e47e93 (branch `preview` / `groups-v1` round 3), as they are there: `odds-api/limits.js`, `pairing.js`, `pairing.test.js`. With the Phase 1 changes listed below: `groups.js`, `grade.js`, `groups.test.js`, `groups.r2.test.js`, `groups.owner.test.js`, `groups.r3.test.js`.
- `odds-api/acct.js`: main's file plus the three new lines (function `who`, and exports `who`, `isOwnerPhone`, `namesGet`, `ownerName`). Main's `ownerOk` and name checks untouched (main's acct.js was identical to the merge base c9a9e93, so nothing had to be reconciled).
- `odds-api/server.js`: main's file plus the `/groups` route line and the 5-minute tick (only with `SUPABASE_URL`, and only when `GROUPS_ENABLED=1`). Main's `/alert` and `/propideas` lines are as main has them.
- `odds-api/sync.js`: see "The bet sync" below.
- LEFT OUT: `alert.js`, `alert.test.js`, `helper.js` (the server-picks / parlay branch: main's alert.js stays), `cfbd.js`, `propideas.js` (main's), `groups.stage1.fixture.js` (not in 1e47e93; preview demo data), every `preview*.js`, the in-memory store, the secret scrub, static-file serving, `|| PREVIEW` in the tick, the `ALERT` load / tick lines, `require('./groups').wire()` at boot (it only connects Calcutta / golf / events / stats engines, none of which are here; add the call when the first engine lands), `sync.js dbList` (nothing calls it), and ALL phone code (`pics/groups.js`, `pics/join.js`, the hooks in index.html / sports.html / gate.js / dash.js / sync.js).
- Dependencies on the parlay branch (1e47e93 was built on v253-parlays d7b044f): NONE in code. Checked: `groups.js` requires only `crypto`, `./grade`, `./limits`, `./sync`, `./acct`, `./ip`, `./pairing`, and optionally `./stats`, `./calcutta`, `./golf`, `./events` (each inside try / catch: missing = skipped; server boots and every suite passes without them). `grade.js` optionally uses `./games` `peek()`, which main already has. The only parlay tie is a comment ("same price rule as pics/parlay.js okPrice") and one phone-only test (parlay labels + v254 markers), which is skipped here.

### Fixes in this phase (review blockers; all server side)
- **A (B3) the switches** above.
- **B (B2) a bet no contest can count is NOT locked.** `/groups/bet/add` now asks, with the contest's own code (`judge`, `memberRules`, `weekCap` on a what-if copy: `countable()`), whether any of the player's contests would count the bet. If none: HTTP 200 `{ok:true, personal:true, saved:false, counted:0, code:"personal", gl:0, why, text:"No contest in your groups can count this bet (<why>). It was NOT added to a group and is not locked: it stays a personal bet."}`. Nothing is written (no book entry, no My Bets copy, no lock). Covers: another sport, a game before the contest starts / before the player joined / after it ends, not in any running contest (was a 409), a kind of bet the contest leaves out, more legs than allowed, "Kalshi-tagged only", a bet the server cannot check where there is no honor rule, the other side of a game already held (hedge), over the stake limit, over the weekly limit. Saved bets also carry `counted: n`; a bet that counts only under an honor rule says so (`honor:true`, "…it counts only under your group's honor rule."). A refusal from the add check (game already started, line too far, ESPN not answering) is turned into "personal" when the bet is plainly outside every contest (another sport, other dates), so a live NBA bet from a member of an NFL contest is not blocked. A late bet INSIDE a contest is still a 409 "already started… Nothing was added" (round-3 L2 unchanged). Plain `/sync/push` rejects nothing: a personal bet syncs like any other.
  - Why HTTP 200 and not an error: an error makes the phone drop the bet, which is the opposite of "kept as a personal bet". PHASE 2 MUST handle `personal:true` in `place()` / `gate()` (keep the bet, show `text`); the 1e47e93 phone code would show "Not saved".
- **C (H5) ESPN cannot confirm the game: REFUSED, not "saved, will be checked".** A bet goes into a group only if ESPN told the server just now (a good answer no older than 15 minutes; the scoreboard copy lives 10) that the game exists and has not started. Otherwise 503 "Oddable can't check that game right now (the scores service did not answer), so this bet was NOT added to your group. Nothing was locked. Try again in a minute." Chosen because it cannot be gamed: "saved, will be checked" would put a bet in the book that nobody checked for kickoff or line, tell the player it is in when it may be thrown out later, and make an ESPN outage the best time to add a late bet. Side effect (good): the reviewer's "ESPN down from add until kickoff: a -500 favourite typed at +100" hole is closed, since such a bet no longer gets in. How: `grade.js` game objects carry `seen` (when ESPN was read), and `day()` / `follow()` count unanswered reads in `ctx.miss`; grading ignores both. After an ESPN failure the server waits 2 minutes before asking again (existing cache rule), so a retry inside 2 minutes is refused again.
- **D (H3) the member index.** `memIdx` is tried 3 times with short waits (all callers). If it still fails after an approval: the bet status (`inRunning`) also looks at the groups the player asked to join, so their next bet still goes to the group; and `/groups/mine` finds an asked-for group whose row lists them as a member, shows it and writes the index entry back. (A stranded member who re-opens their invite link was already repaired by `join`.) NOT covered: a direct join or a create whose index write fails 3 times with no earlier request on record (double failure; the group row is still correct).
- NOT changed (owner's word needed), see "Decide before public launch": H1, H2, H4.

### The bet sync (`odds-api/sync.js`): every user's bets pass through it
- Main's `merge` body is untouched, byte for byte; it is only renamed `mergeBase`. A new `merge(a, b, now, prevU)` wraps it:
  1. `if (!groupsOn()) return mergeBase(a, b);` — with `GROUPS_ENABLED` unset the result IS main's. No stamp, no lock, nothing removed. (The brief allowed the stamp while off; making the whole layer do nothing was simple, so it does nothing.)
  2. ON, before: the stored bets that carry `gl` (written only by `/groups/bet/add`) are listed; their ids are taken out of both tombstone lists (a phone cannot delete a locked bet); `gl` is removed from any incoming bet the server did not lock (a phone cannot make a group bet).
  3. `mergeBase` runs on that.
  4. ON, after: each locked bet is put back as the STORED copy plus only the phone's own result marks (`res`, `fin`, `finPL`, `auto`, each leg's `res`), whatever mergeBase chose (a phone cannot edit it); then every bet gets `sa` = the server time its id first arrived (a phone-sent `sa` is ignored, an existing stamp never moves, a bet stored before stamps gets the row's previous write time).
- The two callers pass the write time: sign-up `merge({}, data, Date.now())`, push `merge(cur.data, data, upd, cur.updated)`.
- `adminUsers` (owner dashboard, My Apps feed, /acct/myname): Groups OFF = main's one query, unchanged. Groups ON = first asks with `&id=not.like.lock:*` (so lock rows can never eat the 2,000-row limit); if that is refused, main's query; the JS filter stays. New `dbIds()` (player row ids) does the same.
- Proof it equals what was reviewed: a one-off fuzz (scratch, not committed) of 200,000 random merges, twice (with and without duplicate / null stored bets): ON = 1e47e93's merge byte for byte; OFF = main's byte for byte; inputs never changed.
- NOTE for the manager: round 3 made contests read the group BOOK (`lock:gb:<member>`), so nothing reads the sync `sa` stamp any more. It is kept because it was in the reviewed code and the brief; it costs about 20 bytes per bet on every user's row once `GROUPS_ENABLED=1` (reviewer measured +57 KB on a 1.35 MB row; the push limit is 1.5 MB). Dropping the stamp before the switch goes on is a safe option (delete the last step of `merge` and the stamp assertions in groups.r2 / sync.phase1).
- Reviewer's "info" items still true: a locked bet's result mark can flap between two phones (display only), and a locked bet can never be deleted from My Bets, even after the contest ends.

  - They `require('./groups.testenv')` first: it sets `GROUPS_ENABLED=1`, owner-only off (the suites test Groups open, as designed).

Phase 1 has no phone code, so they are SKIPPED and counted (each suite prints the number: 1 + 3 + 3 + 8), never silently passed. With `ODD_PREVIEW_ROOT=<a checkout of 1e47e93>` they run and pass (done here against an export of 1e47e93's pics + HANDOFF). They switch on by themselves once `pics/groups.js` exists in this tree; Phase 2 must then update them (wording, v257).

Nothing else in the ported suites was changed.

- Mutation proof (scratch, not committed): 19 mutations, each undoing one piece of A / B / C / D / the sync layer (incl. a change inside mergeBase): all 19 caught by the named checks.
- Reviewer's scripts from /tmp/rev-groups run against this branch (`ODD_ROOT`): a2 24/3 and a3 13/2 same as on 1e47e93; a7 same row writes (ledger written twice, as before); a1: 3b and 3d now PASS (H5), 9a still fails (H2), 10a now "fails" only because not-in-a-contest is 200 personal, not 409 (one line of the script needed a null guard, in a scratch copy); a5: A4 now PASSES (H3); E1 / E2 / E5 / E7 and a6 C3 still print FAIL because those scripts wanted a non-200 status or a stored not-counted bet, and the answer is now 200 `personal:true` with nothing locked (their evidence lines show exactly that); D2 = H1; B2 / C4 / D1 are the reviewer's info items, unchanged. a4 and a8 hard-code the reviewer's worktree and the phone file: not run (sync.phase1.test.js covers a4's ground; a8 is phone code = Phase 2).
- `node --check` on all 30 odds-api .js files: clean. Boot: main's server.js and this one started with NO env vars on test ports: 19 existing routes (health, acct, sync, OPTIONS, lines, locks, propideas, cfbd, pricelog, kalshi, alert, odds, a 404) answer identically (status, body, CORS headers); `/groups/*` gives the blocked answers; also with `GROUPS_ENABLED=1 GROUPS_OWNER_ONLY=1` and no database; and with a deliberately broken groups.js the server still boots, other routes work and `/groups/bet/status` is still 200.

### NOT verified (needs the live system; do before the owner-only test and before public launch)
- Anything live: no deploy, no Render change, no Supabase read or write, no real phone.
- Whether the conditional write (`dbPatchIf`: PATCH `?id=eq.X&updated=eq.N`, `Prefer: return=representation`) is atomic in real Postgres / Supabase when two calls race (two joins for the last seat, sync against `/groups/bet/add` on one row). Tested only against in-memory fakes.
- The PostgREST filters `id=not.like.lock:*` on the real table (there is a fallback if it is refused, but a filter that is accepted and wrong would not be noticed), and Supabase's default 1,000-row cap: `dbIds()` asks for 5,000 rows and `adminUsers` for 2,000; past 1,000 players a phone account may not find its synced row ("Turn on sync…" on a group bet) and `/groups/access find` may miss people.
- Real ESPN shapes: scoreboard odds blocks per league, postponed / cancelled status names, `summary?event=`, college `groups=80` / `groups=50` coverage. The new 503 treats a 5xx / 429 / timeout from ESPN as "not answering" and a 4xx as "no such event".
- Cold start (free Render sleeps): the first `/groups/*` call after a wake-up, the 60-second wait on the phone, and in-memory state lost on every restart (rate limits, wrong-code counters, the 10-minute sync-id map).
- More than one server instance (the per-row queue is per process).
- CORS from the real page for `GET /groups/on` and every `/groups/*` POST.
- Fix B at scale: each add now also reads the player's own book and the contest ledger (read only, nothing written by the check).
- A game ESPN lists as postponed with every game of that day finished is cached 12 hours; a bet on it is refused (503) for that long.

### Rollback
1. Fastest, no deploy: remove `GROUPS_ENABLED` on Render (or set it to anything but `1`). Groups is off for everyone, the tick stops, the merge is main's again. Stored `sa` / `gl` fields stay as plain fields main ignores (a once-locked bet becomes an ordinary, deletable bet in My Bets; the group book is untouched). Tested (S4, S7, A6).
2. Code: revert this branch's commit on main and redeploy. Main's sync.js reads rows written by this code (S4). Group rows stay in the table and are ignored (the `lock:` prefix keeps them out of player lists on main too).
3. Optional clean-up later (only Groups' own rows): ids starting `lock:grp:`, `lock:gl:`, `lock:gb:`, `lock:gm:`, `lock:gcode:` and the single rows `lock:gidx`, `lock:gaccess`, `lock:ent`, `lock:entreq`, `lock:entuse`. Do NOT touch `lock:names`, `lock:alert*`, `lock:pi*` or the locks.js rows.

### Going live (manager)
- Phase 1 deploy: push to main. Set NOTHING on Render. Check after the deploy: `GET https://calcutta-odds.onrender.com/groups/on` -> `{"on":false}`; `POST /groups/bet/status` `{}` -> 200 `{"betLock":0,"canBet":false,"saved":false}`; `/health`, a real sync from the app, the owner dashboard. Check `list_deploys` shows the newest commit (v189 note).
- Phase 2 (owner only), once the phone code is in: `GROUPS_ENABLED` = `1`, `GROUPS_OWNER_ONLY` = `1`, `GROUPS_TESTERS` = the testers' keys, comma-separated (may be left out). `OWNER_PHONE`, `OWNER_PIN`, `SUPABASE_URL`, `SUPABASE_KEY`, `MYAPPS_URL`, `MYAPPS_SECRET` must already be set (they are, since v179 / v186). Optional: `ENTERPRISE_MAX_PEOPLE` (default 500). From that moment every user's synced bets get the `sa` stamp (see the note above) and the tick runs.
- Phase 3 (everyone): delete `GROUPS_OWNER_ONLY` (and `GROUPS_TESTERS`).

### Decide before public launch (NOT changed in Phase 1; each has a skipped, failing-by-design check in `groups.phase1.test.js`)
The review's HANDOFF entry names the findings; its full report was not on this machine, so the fixes below are the coder's proposals from the findings and the reviewer's script evidence.
- **H1** a moneyline on a game with NO odds on ESPN is paid at the typed price up to even money: NYG winning 45-3 with nothing posted paid +100 (reviewer: "free money: in college sports many games have no ESPN odds; take every big favourite at +100"). Proposed: such a bet does not count ("No price posted for this game"), or counts only at a price a second free source confirms. A pricing rule: owner's word.
- **H2** honor-system contests take the phone's win AMOUNT at face value (a $1 prop "won" $1,000,000). Proposed: keep the phone's won / lost for bets the server cannot check, but work the amount out on the server from the bet's own price, capped by "Longest odds that count" and the stake cap.
- **H4** a member who is not marked paid is still shown as owed a prize (out of the others' entries). Proposed: only members marked paid can be owed; the place's money passes to the next paid member, and the unpaid member sees "pay your entry to be eligible".
- Also open: drop or keep the sync `sa` stamp (above); a locked bet can never be removed from My Bets after its contest ends; the owner-PIN sign-in cannot place group bets; `mine` reads asked-for groups twice (small); the reviewer's MEDIUM / LOW list and real-system checks (previous entry).

### Phase 2 notes (phone)
- B1 (phone gate must not refuse a bet when it never knew the player was in a group; shorter wait) and the G16 "Just for me" removal are PHONE fixes: not in this phase.
- New server answers the phone must handle: `personal:true` (keep as a personal bet, show `text`), 503 from `bet/add` (show the message; it says "try again"), `honor:true` / `counted`, `/groups/on`, `/groups/access/request`.
- To-do for the owner (unchanged): upgrade The Odds API plan (about $30/month) if the app sells. Groups spends no Odds API credits.

## Groups review fixes before switch-on — Nov 16 2026 (coder agent; on top of the merge; server only, Groups still DARK; NO app change, NO version bump)
The independent reviewer's verdict on the Groups server code was DEPLOY AFTER FIXES (dark). These are the fixes:
- **Group id check in `gmut`** (`odds-api/groups.js`): the id a phone sends is now checked exactly like `gread` (`^[A-Z2-9]{10}$`) BEFORE it becomes a database key; anything else = 404 "Group not found". Reviewer's reproduction: `{"id":"<real id>#"}` on `/groups/request` and `/groups/cancel` reached the database address, where "#" starts the URL fragment, so the `updated=eq.N` guard fell off the conditional write. Reproduced here on the unfixed code (request answered 200 and wrote without the guard), fixed, tested: `groups.fix1.test.js` F1 = 135 calls (15 bad ids x 9 routes) all refused 4xx, no unguarded write, group row untouched; the real id still works.
- **`odds-api/sync.js` `dbGet` / `dbPatchIf`** (the same hole one layer down, for every caller): the row id is encoded into the address (`qid` = `encodeURIComponent`, with ":" put back). Ordinary ids (64-hex account ids, `lock:...`, `plog:...`: letters, digits, `: - _ . ~`) give the SAME address as main, byte for byte: checked on 20,021 ids in `groups.fix1.test.js` F3 and again in the main-vs-result differential (the outgoing database addresses are compared too). Only an id holding another character (`# & ? space %` ...) is sent differently, and those were broken or dangerous before. `dbPut` sends the id in the body, unchanged. NOT checked against the real Supabase (the build shell cannot reach it): the first real sync after the deploy is the check.
- **`POST /groups/invite` with `type` "__proto__" / "constructor"** answered 500 (the lookup tables were plain objects): `INVITE` and `KINDS` have no prototype now and the handler must find a function. They answer the normal 404 "This kind of game is coming soon to Oddable." Test F2.
- OPEN (owner / manager decision pending, left exactly as it was): the `sa` stamp ("server saved at") is written on group bets but nothing reads it for a decision yet. Decide: keep it for a later rule, or drop it.
- Not looked for: other request fields used as keys into plain-object tables elsewhere in `groups.js` (only the invite `type` was reported and fixed).
- New test: `node odds-api/groups.fix1.test.js` (real acct.js / sync.js / limits.js / groups.js; fake Supabase behind fetch that reads the address the way a real client sends it).

## v261 — Nov 16 2026: court search tries backup map servers (+ "saved list" label on the phone)
- Why: live check of v259's Find local courts on the free map failed. Render log: "courts: map lookup failed (fetch failed)", then the phone's wait ran out; overpass-api.de was answering 502 "server at capacity". The free Overpass servers are shared and often full. (Google Places from My Apps settings is unchanged and still the paid way around it.)
- `odds-api/courts.js` (`fromOsm`): the list is asked of FOUR public Overpass servers in turn: `https://overpass-api.de/api/interpreter`, `https://overpass.private.coffee/api/interpreter`, `https://overpass.kumi.systems/api/interpreter`, `https://maps.mail.ru/osm/tools/overpass/api/interpreter` (was the first and third only, 30 s each).
  - (SUPERSEDED Nov 16 2026 by "Map lookups: fallbacks when Overpass is down" at the end of this file: 8 s a try, stop after two servers, then the fallbacks.)
  - Each try has a 12 s limit (`OSM_TIMEOUT`). A "busy" answer (429 / 502 / 503 / 504) gets ONE more try on the same server after 1.5 s; any other failure (dropped connection, 500, bad answer) moves straight to the next server.
  - The server that answered last is asked FIRST for the next 10 minutes (`osmGood`, in memory); if it then fails it is forgotten.
  - The whole map step stops after 36 s (`OSM_BUDGET`): worst case for one search = place lookup 10 s + My Apps settings 6 s + Google 12 s + map 36 s = 64 s, under the phone's 70 s wait. A try is never started with less than 1.5 s left, and its limit is cut to the time left.
  - The Overpass query's own time limit is 10 s now (was 25 s), so a server gives up before our 12 s limit instead of working on for nobody.
  - All fail + an older list for that area is still in memory (lists are kept past their 6 hours until pushed out): that list is sent with `stale:true`, its OWN `asOf` and the note; it is then held for 2 minutes so the next taps answer at once instead of waiting 36 s again. All fail + nothing saved: 502 "The court map is not answering right now…" as before. Nothing is invented. NOTE: the saved lists live in memory, so a restart (every deploy) or the free service going to sleep empties them.
  - Log lines name the server that failed (`map lookup failed (overpass-api.de: overpass 502)`); searches and keys are never logged (same `scrub`).
- Phone (`pics/pickleball.js` `ctResults`): when the answer has `stale:true` the first card says **"Saved list from <time>."** + why, and the source line says "saved list from <time>" (was "list from"). A fresh list looks exactly as before. Checked in headless Chromium at 390 px with a mocked server (stale, fresh, stale + no courts): right text, no sideways scroll, no script error. The My Bets banner is not on this page.
- Version v261 on the whole bump list: `pics/index.html` (comment, header pill, footer, `trophy.js?v=`, `alert-frozen.json?v=`, `gate.js?v=`), `pics/sports.html` (`gate.js?v=`), `pics/sw.js` (`pics-v261`), `pics/pickleball.html` (comment, footer, `pickleball.js?v=`).
- Tests (`node odds-api/courts.test.js`, mocked fetch, fake clock; new section 5b): first server 502 → second answers; the server that worked goes first for 10 minutes, then the list order is back; a remembered server that fails is dropped; non-busy errors are not retried and do not wait; all down = 502 after 4 tries; slow servers = three 12 s tries then stop inside 36 s; busy answers + waits stay inside the budget; all down with a saved list = `stale:true` with the old `asOf`, held 2 minutes, retried after, fresh again when the map is back; another area still 502. The key checks (never in a response, a log line or a URL) run over all of it.
- NOT verified: the four map servers themselves (the build shell cannot reach them, so nobody has seen private.coffee or mail.ru answer this query); the real phone; Render. After the deploy: search "McAllen, TX" on the Pickleball page and read the calcutta-odds log for `courts:` lines.
