## v262 — Nov 16 2026: ⛳ PGA Tour live leaderboard (scores only)
- OWNER: "Add golf pga to the oddable with live updates. Groups can Calcutta the tournament or the year." This build is the first half only: the live leaderboard. The Calcutta half comes later inside Groups and should read the data layer below. No picks, no Alert, no records, no units, no betting lines for golf.
- WHERE IT LIVES: its own page `pics/pga.html` + data layer `pics/pga.js` (like Pickleball). Not in `sports.html`: that page is built around two-team games and the prediction engine, and a leaderboard shares none of it. Named `pga.*` on purpose: the unshipped Groups golf work (commit 2fa88eb) already has a `pics/golf.js`.
- League menu: "⛳ PGA Tour" added after NCAA in `#lgsel` on `index.html` and `sports.html` (and on the PGA page itself, so the trip back is one tap). Picking it goes straight to `pga.html` with NO "Not a pick model yet" modal; the page carries its own line "Scores only. Oddable makes no golf picks." NBA/MLB/NHL/NCAAB picked from the PGA page still show that modal.
- FEED: `https://site.api.espn.com/apis/site/v2/sports/golf/pga/scoreboard` (cross-origin OK). Shape read by the manager in a browser on Nov 16 2026 with the event final; it is written out in the header of `pics/pga.js`. That response has NO position, NO per-player status, NO venue and NO cut flag, so: positions are worked out from `score` with ties ("T3"); course/location/purse show only if ESPN sends them; a missed cut is INFERRED and the group is labelled "Did not play the weekend"; nobody is called WD/DQ unless the feed flags it; a player with rounds of 0 / "-" is "No score posted" (his feed score "E" is never shown as even par).
- `window.PGA` CONTRACT (for the later Groups / Calcutta work; `pics/pga.js` has no DOM code and also loads in Node):
  - `PGA.parse(json, {eventId?})` -> `{event, players}`. The ONLY function that knows ESPN's shape: when the real shape differs, change it and nothing else.
  - `event` = null (nothing on the feed) or `{id, name, course, location, dates:{start,end} (ms), purse, status:'pre'|'in'|'suspended'|'final'|'canceled', statusText, round (0 before play), rounds (4), cutLine, cutLineSrc:'feed'|'field'|'', playoff, firstTee (ms)}`.
  - `players[]` = `{id, name, short, country, pos ('1','T3','' when unranked), toPar (number, null = no score), today, thru ('F','11',''), teeTime (ms or null), rounds:[{n, strokes, toPar, holes, complete}] (rounds played only, regulation only), total (strokes of finished rounds), status:'active'|'cut'|'wd'|'dq'|'ns', statusSrc:'feed'|'inferred'|'', movement?, order (ESPN's), playoff}`; sorted leaderboard first, then those out of the weekend, then no card. `'ns'` (no score posted) is an addition to the brief's list.
  - `PGA.schedule(json, now)` -> `[{id, name, start, end, state:'done'|'now'|'next'}]` from `leagues[0].calendar` (names and dates; no winners in the feed).
  - `PGA.load()` -> `{event, players, schedule, at, src:'ESPN'}` (throws if ESPN does not answer); `PGA.current()` = last good answer (memory, else the copy in localStorage `pga-last`, with its read time in `.at`); `PGA.loadEvent(id, startMs)` = another event via `?dates=YYYYMMDD` (NOT VERIFIED live).
  - Cut inference: once any player has round-3 holes, a player without them is out if the event is final, round 4 exists, he has no finished 36 holes, or his 36-hole score is worse than every round-3 starter (weekend tee times go out worst score first, so better scores with no round-3 holes are waiting to tee off). `cutLine` from the field is given only when someone was actually cut by that rule. For a Calcutta payout, do not rely on 'cut' vs 'wd': the feed does not tell them apart.
- SCREEN: header card (This week / Last event / Next event tag, name, dates, "Round 2 in progress" / "suspended" / "Final" / "Starts Thu 6:00 AM your time", cut line when known) with drill-down tiles (Leader, Played the weekend, Players; "‹ Back to all players" returns); wrapped rows Pos / Player / To par / Today / Thru on one fixed grid (headers over their columns, numbers right); tap a row for R1–R4 and status, "▲ Close"; ☆ follow saved in localStorage `pga-stars`, followed players pinned in "My players"; Show menu (All / Top 10 / Made cut / My players) + name search; View menu This week / Schedule; between tournaments: the final leaderboard stays as "Last event" with a "Next event" card, and once the next event is the one on the feed the last event's top 10 is read once (`loadEvent`) with an honest note if it does not load.
- LIVE: one read on open; every 60 s only while the page is visible AND status is 'in'; every 10 min otherwise; no reads while hidden; a read at once on return if one is due. Changed rows (position or score) flash gold for 3 s (no animation under reduced motion). "Updated h:mm · Scores from ESPN" + Refresh. If ESPN fails: last good scores with "ESPN did not answer at … Showing the last scores read, from …" or, with nothing to show, the failed card; "Try again" = exactly one read. Event days are shown in US Eastern (ESPN stamps days at midnight ET/PT; end date minus one minute); tee times in the phone's own time.
- The page has no floating My Bets banner and no score pop-up (like Pickleball), so nothing can be covered. It loads `gate.js` like `sports.html` (same sign-in wall as the other score pages). DECISION for the owner/manager if golf scores should be free: remove that one script tag.
- TESTS: `node scripts/pga/pga.test.js` (14 groups; fixtures in `scripts/pga/fixtures.js` are HAND-MADE to the shape above, names invented). Playwright: `scripts/pga/shots.js` (serve `pics/` on 8812 first; the menu round trip and the v260 comparison were session scratch scripts built on the earlier NFL mock library, not in the repo): 309 checks at 390/430/1280 on the page (leaderboard, expand, follow/filter/search, tiles, schedule, live change at 60 s, hidden/visible, idle 10 min, stale, failed, empty, between tournaments, round 3 cut, suspended, playoff, flagged, reduced motion, no sideways scroll, tap targets 40+, header alignment, 0 page errors); 72 of 72 NFL captures identical v260 vs v262; menu round trip NFL -> PGA -> NFL with view, bets and league intact; the v260 score pop-up suite passes unchanged on v262 (159 at 390 and at 1280).
- NOT VERIFIED (no live tournament data was reachable from the build shell; the manager saw one FINAL response): a round in progress (partial hole lists = "Thru"; whether unplayed rounds appear as 0/"-" placeholders; where tee times sit before a round), the status names for suspended/delayed play, a playoff (assumed: an extra linescore with period 5, winner = lower `order`), `?dates=` for a past event, whether `comp.status.period` carries the round (falls back to the highest round anyone has played), team events (Zurich), and the real page on a real phone. Check during the next tournament week: open the feed URL on Thursday and Saturday and compare with the page.
- OPEN: winners in the Schedule (needs one `?dates=` read per event; not built because unverified); a server cache if many phones poll ESPN at once (today each phone reads ESPN directly, one request a minute at most).

## Activity events to My Apps — Nov 16 2026 (coder agent; server only; NO app change, NO version bump)
Owner: "Setting on my apps for notification like new bets, new group, Calcutta started etc." The Oddable server now tells My Apps what happened; My Apps (build .34, its HANDOFF section ".34 / API contract 3") records it in Activity and decides whether the owner's phone gets an alert.
- **Nothing reaches the owner's phone for bets until** My Apps build .34 is live AND he turns "New bets" on in My Apps (Home > Alerts to your phone; it is OFF by default there; the other types default ON). Until My Apps has the route the server gets a 404, says nothing and waits 5 minutes.
- **Contract** (built against it; field names fixed): `POST /api/v1/event` `{slug, secret, type, account?, title, detail?, key?}` through `acct.js ma()` (adds slug + secret). `type`: `bet_new`, `group_new`, `group_join`, `contest_start`, `calcutta_start`, `calcutta_sold`, `golf_start`, `pickleball_start`, `other`. `title` 80 characters at most, `detail` 160, `key` 120, `account` = the player's My Apps account email (a phone account = `acct.phoneEmail(row id)`, as `/sync/admin` does). My Apps answers 400 `contact` to an email address, 7+ digits in a row, or a number written like a phone number (956-555-1234, (956) 555-1234, 956.555.1234) in title / detail. Answers `{ok:true}`, `{ok:true,dup:true}`, 400, 403, 404, 429 `busy` (over 120 recorded events in 10 minutes), 500: ALL swallowed.
- **`odds-api/events.js`** (new; `acct.js` NOT changed):
  - `emit(type, {account, title, detail, key})` -> true (queued) / false (dropped). Never throws, never waits, never sends inside the caller's request (a timer does). Text is cleaned first with My Apps' own three checks copied in (`clean()`): contact-looking text, every `@`, tags' brackets and control characters are taken out, then it is cut to length. Dates, odds and money stay. Only strings and numbers count as text.
  - Dropped when My Apps is not set up (`maReady()` false = no `MYAPPS_URL` / `MYAPPS_SECRET`) or `EVENTS_ENABLED` is `0` (also `off` / `false` / `no`). **`EVENTS_ENABLED` defaults ON**; it is read on every call, so setting it on Render (service ODDABLE / calcutta-odds) turns everything off with no code change.
  - Queue in memory: 200 at most (the oldest go), ONE send at a time, 100 ms between sends, 5 s limit per send. A failed send (429, 5xx, no answer, network) gets one more try. After a 404 or 403, or three failures in a row, nothing is sent for 5 minutes (one log line `events: paused for 5 minutes (...)`; nothing else is logged, never a title or an account). An event that waited more than 10 minutes is thrown away (there is no "happened at" field, so a late one would read as new). A restart empties the queue.
- **New bets (`bet_new`)**: `odds-api/sync.js` line 144 (`/sync/push`, after the conditional write succeeded and AFTER `send()`) calls `betEvents()` (line 154): on `setImmediate`, inside try / catch, it only READS the row before and the stored result. New = an id in the stored result that was not in the row the merge ran against, whose own `t` is within 15 minutes of now (either side). So edits, results, deletes, a pull, and a new device uploading old history announce nothing. ONE event per push: title "Added 1 bet" / "Added N bets"; detail = the first new bet ("LAC +4.5 (LAC at DEN)", "3-leg parlay", a prop's text) + " (first of N)"; no stake or payout (the phone's stake is dollars or units by a setting only the phone has; there is no units field), and "$50" typed into a bet text is removed; key = account + "|" + first new bet id. The owner's own accounts are NOT filtered here (My Apps records them and does not push). Sign-up (`/sync/signup`) never announces.
- **Groups (only once `GROUPS_ENABLED=1`; dark = no calls)**, `odds-api/groups.js`, each after the write succeeded and after the answer was sent, through `tell()` (-> `D.ev().emit`, in try / catch):
  - line 841 create -> `group_new` "New group: <name>", detail "Started by <name>. Up to N people.", key `group_new|<group id>`.
  - line 914 join with the commissioner's direct link -> `group_join` "Joined group: <name>" (only when the person was really added: not for a repeat join or a request). line 941 `decide` approved -> `group_join` (no account: only the member's hashed key is known there; the member's shown name is in the detail). key `group_join|<group id>|<member key>`.
  - line 999 in `tick()` -> `startedEvent()` (line 986): a picks contest whose start has passed -> `contest_start` "Contest started: <contest name>", once per contest per process, only in the 12 hours after the start, key `contest_start|<group id>:<contest id>`. A restart inside those 12 hours sends it again with the same key; My Apps only drops a repeat inside 10 minutes, so a restart can alert twice. The tick is the only place a start is noticed (nothing writes "started"), so the alert comes up to 5 minutes after the start (the check runs on every 5-minute server tick, before the tick's own 5 / 30 minute spacing), and never if the service is down for those 12 hours.
  - A join REQUEST, a denial, leaving and deleting send nothing (no type for them).
- **Hooks for the code not built yet** (`odds-api/events.js`; call them AFTER your write succeeded; each returns true / false and never throws): `calcuttaStarted(group, name, o)` line 138, `golfStarted(group, name, o)` line 139, `pickleballStarted(group, name, o)` line 140, `calcuttaSold(group, lot, buyer, price, o)` line 142. `group` = the group's name or the group object (`{id, name}`); `o` (optional) = `{account, key, detail}`. PASS `o.key` = the auction's / round's / session's own id (plus the lot for a sale): without it the key is type + group + name + today's date (UTC), so two same-named things on one day read as one. For anything else use `emit('other', {...})`. A new type must first be added to `EVENT_TYPES` in My Apps `store.js`, else My Apps answers 400 `type`.
- **Proof the bet sync is unchanged** (scratch script, NOT in the repo: main 90a8a90's `sync.js` against this tree's, one fake database each, the same generated requests, fixed clock, compared after every request): 10 runs of 20,000 pushes = **223,209 requests, 200,004 pushes, 0 different** in status, headers, body, every database call (address, body, order) and the stored rows. Runs: Groups off and on, each with events on, `EVENTS_ENABLED=0`; plus My Apps answering 404, not set up, throwing, and never answering. Inputs: adds, edits, results, deletes, old history, junk, `gl` / `sa` fields, 1.6 MB bodies, wrong PINs, sign-ups, pulls, `/sync/admin`, two to four pushes at once for one phone, another writer getting in first (the retry), database errors. In the two runs with no races the number of events sent was compared with an independent count from MAIN's answers: 670 of 670 and 667 of 667. The script catches a changed answer and a 16-minute window (two planted faults); a fault planted in the 4th retry was NOT reached by the generator.
- **Tests**: `node odds-api/events.test.js` 13 checks (contract, cleaning incl. 30,000 random texts, never in the request path, switches, queue cap / pacing, every answer swallowed, the pauses, the 5 s limit, which bets are new, the bet text, the real `/sync/push` with events on / off / failing, the hooks, the real `acct.ma()`). `groups.phase1.test.js` + 5 (V1 create, V2 join / approve, V3 contest start, V4 dark = no events and a broken events module cannot break Groups, V5 real events.js with a hostile group name): 21 checks now. Every `odds-api/*.test.js` passes (15 files).
- **NOT verified**: anything live (no deploy, no real My Apps call, no real phone alert); My Apps' real answers (its checks were read from its `store.js` and copied, not called); the whole-server HTTP differential of the "Dark check" entry was not re-run (its scripts are not in the repo; only `sync.js` and `groups.js` changed and server.js is untouched); a Kalshi connect that imports many fills at once (they would count as new bets if their `t` is now).
- **Decide (manager / owner)**: (1) join requests are not announced: want an event ("<name> asked to join")? needs a type in My Apps or `other`. (2) Contest start can alert twice after a restart (My Apps de-dup is 10 minutes); a longer de-dup on My Apps for keyed events would close it. (3) Bet detail carries no stake; send it once the phone stores units vs dollars. (4) Approved joins carry no account (the handle is not shown; the name is in the detail). (5) First real check after both deploys: add a bet, look at My Apps > Activity > App activity.
- To-do for the owner (unchanged): upgrade The Odds API plan (about $30/month) if the app sells. Events spend no Odds API credits.

## v263 — Nov 16 2026: Request free time + the payer's Cash App handle (Oddable side of My Apps build .32)
- Owner (Nov 16 2026): "I would also like a request for time that notifies me." and "Add their Cash App Handle to the subscription so I can know who paid." My Apps build .32 holds the other half (its HANDOFF has the API contract: `/api/v1/time/request`, `/api/v1/time/status`, `cashtag` on `/api/v1/pay/request` and in `/api/v1/pay/info`). **Both need My Apps .32 deployed; until then the new routes answer a plain "try again later".**
- **FREE FOR NOW IS UNCHANGED.** No price, trial, expiry or paywall rule was touched. "Free for now" (Steve, Nov 14) is NOT a switch in this repo: the code gates only on what the server and My Apps say (`acct.js` `on()` = `MYAPPS_URL` + `MYAPPS_SECRET` set and `GATE_OFF` not `1`; `allowed()` = owner, or plan `pro` with no end date or an end date not passed). So it lives in the Render env / the accounts in My Apps, and which of the two is live could not be checked from the build shell (the proxy refuses calcutta-odds.onrender.com). Both were tested: with the gate off, and with the gate on and an account with no end date, the page makes exactly the same requests as v260 and shows none of the new UI.
- **Server** (`odds-api/acct.js`, test `odds-api/acct.test.js`). The account always comes from the signed token (`x-token`), never from the body. No new request header, so the CORS Allow-Headers list is unchanged.
  - `POST /acct/time/request {note?}` -> My Apps `/api/v1/time/request {account, note?}`. Note = text only, control characters and line breaks to spaces, spaces collapsed, trimmed, 140 max (longer or not text = 400 `code:"note"`, My Apps not called). Answers `200 {ok:true, status:"pending", already}`; `429 {ok:false, code:"limit", error:"You have asked 3 times in the last 7 days. Please try again later."}`; `429 code:"busy"`; anything else from My Apps (bad secret, unknown app, down) = `502 {ok:false, code:"failed", error:"Could not send your request. Please try again later."}` and never the upstream text. Rate limit like `/acct/redeem`: 8 per 10 minutes per IP and per account.
  - `POST` or `GET /acct/time/status` -> `/api/v1/time/status` -> `{ok, status: none|pending|granted|declined, days, requested_at, decided_at}` (those fields only; `days` only when granted). 30 per 10 minutes per account (`throttled(k, max)` got an optional cap; default still 8). A grant clears the server's 2-minute entitlement cache for that account.
  - `/acct/pay` takes an optional `cashtag` (trimmed, cut to 40) and sends it on; My Apps owns the rule, and its 400 message is passed to the phone as a 400 (other failures stay 502 as before). The answer gains `cashtag`.
  - `/acct/me` (`shape()`) now returns `cashtag`: the customer's own saved handle from `/api/v1/pay/info` (`""` when none or not in the handle format). Do not confuse with `tag`, the OWNER's tag. No extra My Apps call.
  - The owner-PIN token has none of this (400 "Owner has full access", as for every other customer route). The exports line of acct.js was NOT touched.
- **Phone** (`pics/gate.js` only; index.html / sports.html got the version bump only).
  - **Request free time**: a "Need more time?" box with an optional note (140 characters, live counter) and the button "Request free time". It is on the subscribe screen (trial ended / access ended / renewal waiting / the "tap to renew" toast) and in the Account box under ⓘ Disclosures, but there only when the account has ended or its last day is 5 calendar days away or less (`timeNear`, `daysLeft`). An account with no end date never sees it. After sending: "Pending. Request sent. Sir Oddable will answer soon." and the note box and button are gone. Other answers: "You got 7 days." / "Not this time." (shown for 7 days after the answer). The last answer is kept on the phone in `odd-treq` (wiped on sign-out and when another person signs in: added to `oddWipe`).
  - **One status read per app open** (`timeCheck`), and only when this phone has a request waiting or the account is ending / ended. No polling, no retry: a failed send or read is one message ("Could not send your request. Check your connection and try again." / the server's words for limit and busy) and the button stays; after the limit message the button is hidden until the next app open. "Check again" on the subscribe screen also re-reads the status when a request is waiting (his tap, one call).
  - **When granted**: the days are already on the account, so the normal account read opens the app. If the grant lands between the account read and the status read, the gate re-reads the account ONCE (pending -> granted while still closed) and opens without a reload; no loop if the account still reads ended.
  - **Cash App handle**: on the subscribe screen, above "I paid — unlock me": "Your Cash App $handle", helper "So we can match your payment.", prefilled from the saved handle. Required for "I paid" (the only way to pay today is Cash App). Phone rule = My Apps rule: optional `$`, then 1 to 20 letters, digits, `_` or `-`; the wrong-format message is My Apps' own sentence, and a 400 from the server is shown as it comes. Errors now sit under the box and stay (before v263 an "I paid" error was drawn and wiped by the redraw in the same tick).
  - Everything shown is escaped or set as text (saved handle, server messages; days are numbers). No phone number, email or secret in any message.
- **Decisions (coder; manager / Steve to confirm).** (1) After "Not this time." or "You got N days." he may ask again when the button's conditions hold (My Apps allows 3 in 7 days). (2) In the Account box the STATUS line shows whenever this phone has a request waiting or answered in the last 7 days, even with more than 5 days left (otherwise "You got 7 days" would never be seen there); the BUTTON keeps the 5-day rule. (3) "Within 5 days" = calendar days to the last day (0 = ends today). The old "ends in N days" toast keeps its own older maths. (4) The handle is required on the phone but optional on the server, as the contract says. (5) The version footer names the build ("request free time · Cash App handle"); it is the only new wording a free-for-now user can see. (6) GET `/acct/time/request` is not a route (POST only); GET is accepted for the status only. (7) If My Apps answers "none" while this phone holds "pending", the phone drops its pending line (My Apps is the truth).
- **Tests.** `node odds-api/acct.test.js`: 31 tests (fake My Apps behind `ma()`'s fetch): forwarding, account from the token only, note rules, limit / busy / failures, no upstream text or secret in answers, 401s, owner token, both rate limits, status whitelist, cashtag forward / 400 pass-through / unchanged old behaviour, `/acct/me` cashtag and its unchanged two My Apps calls, gate off = `{gate:false}` and My Apps never called. alert / appcfg / cfbd / courts / propideas tests still pass. Real server booted locally: preflight for `content-type,x-token` answers 204 with the unchanged Allow-Headers; the new routes answer 401 with the CORS header. Playwright, gate ACTIVE (no `?mirror=1`), service workers blocked, every outside call mocked, at 390x844, 430x932 and 1280x900: 579 of 579 checks pass, 0 page errors. Covered: free for now two ways (gate off; gate on + no end date) = request list identical to v260, same `/acct` calls, no new header, no new UI, Account box text identical; 6 and 10 days left = nothing; ended trial -> button -> send -> pending -> declined -> ask again -> granted opens the app; grant on app open opens it with exactly one re-read; limit, busy, 502, 500, gate off, network down; Account box at 5 / 3 / 1 / 0 days, with the real My Bets banner on screen and not over the box at four scroll positions; handle empty / six bad formats / server 400 / valid / prefilled / hostile text; redeem code still works; sign-out wipes `odd-treq`; sports.html shows the same screen; no sideways scroll; new tap targets 40 px or more. Harness and screenshots were in the session scratchpad (`timereq/pw.js`), not in the repo.
- **NOT verified / open.** The live site and a real phone; the real My Apps .32 endpoints (only the written contract was faked: no end-to-end call was made); the cross-origin call from the real page in Chrome (header set is unchanged, but this file's rule says try it live after the calcutta-odds deploy); which way "free for now" is set on the live service; Cash App's real handle rule (My Apps' note says the same). The owner sees the request and answers it in My Apps, not in Oddable. A request is tied to the account, so a second phone shows it on its next open only if that account is ending / ended.
- Version v263: index.html (comment, header pill, footer "Oddable v263 (request free time · Cash App handle)", trophy.js / gate.js / alert-frozen.json `?v=263`), sports.html `gate.js?v=263`, sw.js `pics-v263`, pickleball.html (comment, `pickleball.js?v=263`, footer). SERVER: acct.js needs the calcutta-odds deploy. Picks, locks, the Alert and the model were not touched. Built on v260 while v261 / v262 were in progress elsewhere: the manager settles the version lines at merge.

## v264 — Nov 16 2026: 📍 Golf GPS (your own round: live position on a satellite picture, yards to the pin, flag colour sets the pin)
- OWNER: "Once a group picks a golf course, how can I get their phone to GPS a live play Google view and distance to the hole?" then "Build and allow for input of the color of flag to determine placement." Wording for the placements is his: **"Long center short"** = Short (pin at the front of the green) / Center / Long (back). The player never sees Front / Middle / Back; inside the code the three are `front | center | back`, and the server stores a colour's meaning as `short | center | long`.
- Built as a self-contained page (Group golf days are not live yet): `pics/golfgps.html` (screen) + `pics/golfgps.js` (data layer `window.GolfGPS`, no DOM code, loads in Node) + `pics/leaflet.js`, `pics/leaflet.css`, `pics/leaflet-LICENSE.txt` (Leaflet 1.9.4, vendored, FLAT in pics/ since Nov 17, was `pics/leaflet/`; got with `npm pack leaflet@1.9.4`, no CDN at run time; the `sourceMappingURL` line was removed) + server `odds-api/golfcourse.js` (one line in `server.js` next to `/courts`).
- WAYS IN: League menu item "📍 Golf GPS" after PGA Tour on `index.html`, `sports.html` and `pga.html` (straight to the page, no "not a pick model" warning: nothing to bet on); a "📍 Golf GPS (your round)" button in the PGA page header. No seventh bottom tab.
- **DEEP LINK for the Golf-days coder:** `golfgps.html?course=<way|relation|node>/<OpenStreetMap id>[&hole=<1..36>]` opens the hole screen for that course (the id is what `/golfcourse/search` returns as `id`, e.g. `way/123456`). Without `course` the page opens on the course picker. A bad id falls back to the picker.
- SERVER ROUTES (`odds-api/golfcourse.js`; all data from OpenStreetMap through Nominatim + Overpass; the endpoint rotation / retry / time budget / stale fallback are COPIED from `courts.js`, not shared, because `courts.test.js` replaces `courts.js`'s own fetch and a shared helper would have meant rewriting those tests):
  - `GET /golfcourse/search?q=<name or city>` or `?lat&lon` (+ `r` miles, default 15, 1..50) -> `{ok, center, radius, courses:[{id, name, lat, lon, miles, holes, holesMapped}], count, source, asOf, stale?, note?}`. `holesMapped` here = different `golf=hole` numbers inside the course's bounding box (an estimate; the hole screen has the real count). List kept 24 h per ~1 km point + radius.
  - `GET /golfcourse/holes?id=<type/id>` -> `{ok, id, name, center, holes:[{n, par?, handicap?, mapped, tee?, line?, green?:{center, front?, back?, outline?, marked?, approx?}, hazards:[{type:'bunker'|'water', center, outline}]}], holeCount, holesMapped, marks, flags?, pendingFlags? | flagsPending?, source, asOf, stale?, note?}`. Two Overpass reads (the course itself, then everything `golf=*` in its box, kept to the inside of the course outline when that is a closed shape). Kept 7 days per course; an old copy is served with `stale:true` when the map servers do not answer. FRONT / BACK = where the line of play (last leg of the `golf=hole` way) crosses the green outline, taken through the green's centre. A hole with no green drawn comes back `mapped:false` with `green.center` = the end of the hole line (or a mapped `golf=pin` within 40 m) and `approx:true`. A numbered green with no hole line has no tee and no front/back. Fairways are read but not sent (payload size). Two holes with the same number inside one course (27 / 36 holes drawn as one course): the first is kept and the note says so.
  - `POST /golfcourse/mark` (signed in, `x-token`; `acct.check` + `acct.who`, acct.js not edited): `{id, n, what:'green'|'tee', point}` = a player's own green centre / tee. Within 3 km of the course centre, 60 marks a course, 10 changes a minute per address, last write wins, who/when stored and never sent out. `/holes` lays marks over the map data (`marked:true`; a mark inside a drawn green only moves its centre). Also `{id, what:'flags', flags:{red|white|blue|yellow: 'short'|'center'|'long'}}` = what the colours mean at this course FOR EVERYONE (see below).
  - `POST /golfcourse/flagreport` (signed in): `{id, flags, note?}` = "the course changed its colours" from a player who may not change them; or `{id, action:'accept'|'dismiss'}` from someone who may.
  - STORAGE: ONE shared row per course through `sync.js`'s existing `dbGet / dbPut / dbPatchIf` (sync.js not edited), id **`lock:golfmark:<way-123>`** (the slash becomes a dash), data `{course, marks:{'5:green':{lat,lon,by,at}}, flags:{map, by, at}, pendingFlags:{flags, by, at, note}}`, optimistic write on `updated` like `alert.js`. With no Supabase the routes still answer; marks/colours are just absent and a POST says 503.
- **FLAG COLOURS** (the owner's ask, and his answer to "once colour is made, lock for the course?" = lock the MEANING per course for everyone):
  - Today's colour: big buttons Red / White / Blue / Yellow / None, plus "Pin today: Short · Center · Long" (the three distance tiles are the buttons) for courses that post the pin position without coloured flags. Defaults Red = Short, White = Center, Blue = Long, Yellow = Long. Short = 4 yards in from the front edge toward the centre, Long = 4 yards in from the back edge (`GolfGPS.cfg.PIN_IN_YD`; never more than half way to the centre on a tiny green). A green with no outline: buttons disabled, "This green has no outline on the map yet, showing centre."
  - "Same colour all day" is ON by default (a normal tap changes it for all holes; NO lock and NO confirm, per the owner). OFF = each hole remembers its own colour today. "Tap the green to place the pin" = exact spot for that hole only, overrides the colour there ("Pin placed by you", "Remove my pin").
  - Today's round lives on the phone only: localStorage `ggps-round` `{course, day, allDay, sel, holes, pins}`; it starts clean the next day or when another course is opened (with the course's default colour if the player chose one).
  - What a colour MEANS is per course: stored on the server (`flags` in the row above) by the first signed-in player who saves it, then LOCKED: everyone sees "Course flag colours: Red = Short, … 🔒" and just uses it. Who may change a locked meaning: the owner (`acct.ownerOk`, or signed in on an owner phone / owner PIN) and the account that set it; anyone else gets 403 "Ask the organizer to change it". **HOOK for Golf days:** `golfcourse.js` `_deps.organizer(req, courseId)` (async, returns false today) is asked last in `mayChangeFlags()`: make it answer true for the commissioner of a group playing that course.
  - Not signed in, or the server cannot be reached: the meaning is saved on the phone (`ggps-flags` `{<course id>: {map, def}}`) and the page says "Saved on this phone only". A course meaning from the server always wins over the phone's.
  - "Report a change" (signed-in player who cannot change it): sheet "What do the flags mean at this course now?" prefilled, optional note (100). Server keeps it as `pendingFlags` (last report wins; identical to the current colours refused; 3 a day per account per course) and tells the owner through My Apps: **event type `course_change`**, `ma('/api/v1/event', {type, account:<acct.who>, title:'Flag colours changed at <course, 40>', detail:'Red=Short, White=Center, …' + the note when it fits in 160 and has no e-mail / 7+ digit run, key:'flags:<course id>'})`, fired from ONE function `notifyOwner(type, fields)` in `golfcourse.js`, never awaited, every failure swallowed. **MANAGER AT MERGE: swap the body of `notifyOwner` for `events.emit(type, fields)`** (odds-api/events.js is on main, not on this branch). Player sees "Sent. The organizer will review it." and "Change reported, waiting for review."
  - Review inside the page: an account that may change the colours gets `pendingFlags {flags, at, note, from}` from `/holes` ("Proposed change from <from>: … [Accept] [Dismiss]"); everyone else only `flagsPending:true`. Accept = the report becomes the locked colours (who/when recorded); Dismiss clears it; a direct change by someone allowed also clears it. `from` is "Player xxxx" (first 4 characters of the account id) or the owner name: account ids are never sent out.
- **`window.GolfGPS` CONTRACT** (full list in the header of `pics/golfgps.js`): `search({q}|{lat,lon}, miles)`, `loadCourse(id)` (device marks laid over it; last good copy of 3 courses kept on the phone as `ggps-c:<id>` and used with `offline:true` when there is no signal), `hole(course, n)`, `flagMap(course)` -> `{map, src:'course'|'phone'|'default', locked, canChange, pending, review?}`, `saveFlagMap`, `reportFlags`, `reviewFlags`, `round(courseId)`, `selFor`, `setSel`, `setAllDay`, `setPin`, `clearPin`, `pin(course, n, round)` -> `{point, place, color, by:'you'|'color'|'place'|'center', label, noOutline}`, `placePoint(green, place)`, `distances(from, hole, pinPoint)` (yards), `mark(course, n, what, point)`, `teeWatch`, `gpsState`, `yards`, `bearing`, `toward`. Other phone keys: `ggps-marks` (marks made while not signed in / no signal), `ggps-recent` (6), `ggps-wake`.
- HOLE SCREEN: big yards to the pin + Short / Center / Long; satellite map with the player's dot and accuracy circle, tee, green outline, hazards, hole line and a flag in the chosen colour. The map is TURNED so the line from the player (or tee) to the green points up ("North up" button turns it back and then allows drag / pinch; ＋ / － / Whole hole). Leaflet cannot turn a map, so `#maprot` (a square as wide as the map window's diagonal) is CSS-rotated and taps are converted by the page itself (`pointAt` / `screenOf`); Leaflet's own click position is never used. Tap anywhere = yards to that spot and from there to the pin, tap again clears. Hole dropdown + Prev / Next. "Looks like you're on the 5th tee. Switch?" after 10 s within 30 yd of another hole's tee (suggests, never switches; "No" holds while standing there). Mark green / tee by standing on it (needs GPS ±15 m or better) or by tapping the map (the map pulls back to ~280 m).
- GPS: `watchPosition` (high accuracy) only on the hole screen and only while the page is visible; it starts by itself only if the browser already has permission, otherwise the player taps "Turn on GPS" (the permission question never appears unasked; "Courses near me" asks only on its tap). States in plain words: denied, no fix, weak ("GPS ±22 yd"), "You're not at the course. Distances are from where you are." (over 2 miles). Before a fix, distances are from the tee and say so. Wake Lock toggle ("Keep the screen on", default on where supported) + battery note. "Phone GPS is usually within 3 to 5 yards. Not a rangefinder."
- MAP PICTURES: Esri World Imagery tiles, attribution shown under the map with "Course map © OpenStreetMap contributors". The source is ONE object, `GolfGPS.cfg.TILES` in `pics/golfgps.js`. **Google later (NOT integrated):** needs a Maps JavaScript API key restricted by website (HTTP referrer = the app's domains; such a key is visible to the browser by design), a new whitelisted field in `/appcfg` (`odds-api/appcfg.js`, read from My Apps settings like `card_theme`) to hand it to the page, and the page loading Google's script and drawing the same shapes on a Google map (Google's tiles may not be used inside Leaflet under its terms). Billing applies per map load. Esri's terms for the free World Imagery tiles on a paid product should be checked before the app sells.
- LAYOUT (owner, Nov 16: "Keep the expand to page just like Oddable... all views"): content 720 wide below 900 px, 1200 from 900, 1360 from 1500, with a 104 px rail down the left from 900 px (Oddable / PGA Tour / Golf GPS) so the header and content edges equal `index.html`'s exactly (checked at 768, 820x1180, 1180x820, 1440). From 740 px the map is large on the left and the numbers + flag controls sit beside it; search results are a grid. Phones: numbers, flags, then the map full width. Tap targets 44 px+ (most 48+). The page has NO floating My Bets banner and no score pop-up, so nothing can be covered. It loads `gate.js` like the PGA page (same sign-in wall); remove that one script tag if Golf GPS should be free.
- Version v264 on the whole bump list (v263 is taken by the `time-request` branch; the manager fixes the version lines at merge): `index.html` (comment, header pill, footer now "Oddable v264 (Golf GPS · PGA Tour leaderboard)", `trophy.js` / `alert-frozen.json` / `gate.js` `?v=`), `sports.html` `gate.js?v=`, `sw.js` `pics-v264`, `pickleball.html`, `pga.html`, and the new `golfgps.html` (added to the bump list above).
- TESTS: `node odds-api/golfcourse.test.js` (219 checks: distances against known values within 1 yd, centroid, front/back on a green turned 45°, played across, offset line, tiny green; hole/green matching; course build with a missing green, duplicate hole, neighbour's hole left out; marks merge, validation, cap, rate limit, lost race; colours set / locked for another account / owner override / organizer hook / invalid refused; report accepted / same again / identical refused / 3 a day / not signed in; the event call made once with the right fields and its failure never breaking the request; accept / dismiss; stale copies; all 4 map servers tried). `node scripts/golfgps/golfgps.test.js` (116: the phone data layer). ## Map lookups: fallbacks when Overpass is down (Nov 16 2026, server only, no app change, no version bump)
- WHY: live on Render tonight (9:35 PM and 10:26 PM CT) BOTH free map searches answered 502: `/courts?q=McAllen, TX&r=15` and `/golfcourse/search?q=McAllen, TX&r=20`. Log: `overpass-api.de: fetch failed` (a browser got its 502 "server is currently at capacity"), `overpass.private.coffee` and `overpass.kumi.systems` timed out at 12 s, `maps.mail.ru` 504 then timeout. Nominatim (the place lookup before it) worked. The owner has never seen a real result. Rule from now on: **no map feature may depend on Overpass alone.**
- NEW FILE `odds-api/osmfall.js` (test `odds-api/osmfall.test.js`): the plumbing `courts.js` and `golfcourse.js` now SHARE (the v264 copy of the Overpass loop in golfcourse.js is gone). It has: the Overpass loop, the "Overpass is busy" memory, ONE request queue for `nominatim.openstreetmap.org` + `api.openstreetmap.org`, the Nominatim / OSM API callers, and `toElements()` (OSM API JSON → the elements Overpass gives with `out geom`). Each module passes its own `_deps` object in, so each module's tests still replace their own fetch / clock.
- THE CHAIN, per route:
  - **`/courts`** (provider free map): Overpass → an older FULL list for that area still in memory (`stale:true`, as before) → **the short list** = Nominatim search by name → 502. Google Places (key in My Apps settings) is still the first choice whenever a key is set; if Google fails it drops into this same chain (`fallback:true`, plus `partial:true` when the short list answered).
  - **`/golfcourse/search`**: Overpass → an older FULL list in memory (`stale:true`) → **Nominatim** course list → 502.
  - **`/golfcourse/holes`** (and `/golfcourse/mark`, which reads the course through the same function): Overpass → **the main OSM API** → an older copy in memory (`stale:true`) → 502. Here the fallback comes BEFORE the stale copy because it is the same data, only fetched another way.
  - `/golfcourse/mark` and `/golfcourse/flagreport`: not touched.
- DECISION, Overpass first or Nominatim first for golf search: **Overpass first.** One Overpass query gives every course in the radius AND how many holes are drawn in each; Nominatim gives at most 40 results per search, by its own ranking, and no hole counts. The price of "first" is capped: see the next bullet.
- OVERPASS HANDLING (changed for both modules; supersedes the 12 s / 36 s / all-four-servers numbers in the v261 section): 8 s a server (`TIMEOUT`), one more try on the same server after 1.5 s when it says busy (429 / 502 / 503 / 504), and the step **stops after TWO servers have failed**, never more than 18 s (`BUDGET`). Then Overpass is **left alone for 10 minutes** (`DOWN_TTL`, one memory for courts and golf together: `osmfall.down()`), every request in that time goes straight to its fallback, and after 10 minutes it is asked again, starting with the two servers that were NOT tried last time (`rot`). The server that answered last still goes first for 10 minutes.
- THE SHORT COURT LIST (`courts.js` `fromNominatim` / `nomiPlace`): up to three searches inside a box the size of the radius (`viewbox=left,top,right,bottom`, `bounded=1`, `limit=40`, `extratags=1`, `addressdetails=1`): `q=pickleball`, `q=pickleball court`, and, when the person typed a place, `q=pickleball courts <what they typed>`. Kept: results whose class/type is a sports pitch, sports centre / hall, park, recreation ground, fitness centre, stadium, community centre or sports club AND whose name says pickleball, plus anything whose `extratags.sport` says pickleball (or `pickleball=yes`). Not kept: shops, streets, `access=private`. Each result is turned into the same tagged point the Overpass parser reads, so the 60 m de-dup, court counts, lights, fee, website and the answer shape are the SAME code. The answer carries `partial:true` and the note "Short list: the detailed map server is busy, so only named pickleball places are shown." (empty: that note + "No named pickleball places were found within N miles..."). **It is weaker than Overpass: Nominatim searches names, so a court with no name on the map (most single courts drawn inside a park) is missed.** The v261 phone shows the note as it shows any note; it does not know `partial` (nothing needed).
- THE GOLF COURSE LIST (`golfcourse.js` `nomiCourses` / `nomiCourse`): `q=golf course` (one of Nominatim's special phrases = `leisure=golf_course`) and `q=golf club` in the same kind of box; `q=[golf course]` (the older spelling of the special phrase) only when those two found nothing. Only `leisure=golf_course` results are kept (no mini golf, hotels, driving ranges). Same shape as before: `id` = `way/123`, `name`, `lat`, `lon`, `miles`, `holes` (the `holes` tag if Nominatim sends it, else 18). **Hole counts are not known from Nominatim**, so the answer has `partial:true`, a note, and each course has `holesKnown:false` and **`holesMapped:'?'`**: the v264 phone prints `holesMapped || 0`, so a `null` there would have read "0 of 18 holes mapped" (wrong); `'?'` reads "? of 18 holes mapped" with no phone change. LATER PHONE BUILD (tiny): in `pics/golfgps.html` `cardHtml`, when `c.holesKnown === false` print "holes not checked yet" instead of "? of N holes mapped"; the server can then send `null`.
- THE HOLES FROM THE OSM API (`golfcourse.js` `holesViaApi`): `GET https://api.openstreetmap.org/api/0.6/map.json?bbox=left,bottom,right,top` for the course's box + about 150 m, → `osmfall.toElements()` → the SAME `build()` as the Overpass path (only `golf=*` things are passed in). The box comes from the search list this server already gave (`BOX`, filled by both the Overpass and the Nominatim list), else from one extra read of the course (`/api/0.6/way/<id>/full.json`, `/relation/<id>/full.json`, `/node/<id>.json`). The API's limits are 0.25 square degrees and 50,000 points; one course is far smaller (ours are refused above 0.12 x 0.2 degrees, as before). 404 / 410 = "That course is not on the map" (404); 400 (too many points) / 509 (rate limited) / anything else = the usual 502, or the stale copy.
- POLITENESS: every request to Nominatim AND the OSM API, from courts and golf together, goes through ONE queue (`osmfall.turn`): one at a time, starts at least 1.1 s apart, real User-Agent (`Oddable/1.0 (...; https://steves-pics.onrender.com)`). This fixes the open item in the v264 report ("Nominatim is asked from two queues", up to 2 requests a second). At most 12 wait in the queue; more are told "busy" at once. Kept in memory: place lookups 1 day (as before), a complete short list / Nominatim course list 7 days, a course's holes 7 days. A short list sits in the normal list memory for only 10 minutes, so the full Overpass list replaces it as soon as Overpass is back.
- LOGS (Render, service ODDABLE): one line per answered request with the source only, never the search and never a key: `courts: answered by overpass overpass-api.de` / `nominatim short list` / `google` (+ `(from memory)` or `(saved list, the map is not answering)`), `golfcourse: search answered by ...`, `golfcourse: holes answered by overpass` / `osm api`, `... answered by nobody (502)`; plus `map lookup failed (<server>: <why>)` and `detailed map marked busy for 10 minutes`.
- TIME (worst case, phone waits 70 s): courts = place 10 + settings 6 + Google 12 + Overpass 18 + short list 20 = 66 s; golf search = 10 + 18 + 20 = 48 s; holes = 18 + two OSM API reads (20 s limit each, 1.1 s apart) = 60 s. Normal case with Overpass down: about 16 s for the first search, a few seconds for everything in the next 10 minutes. If several people search at the same moment they share the one queue, so the last of them waits longer.
- TESTS (all mocked, fake clock): `node odds-api/osmfall.test.js` (41 checks: a hand-typed `map.json` → exact Overpass-style elements; courts + golf in one process use ONE queue, every Nominatim / OSM API request at least 1.1 s after the one before, never two in flight; one shared "busy for 10 minutes" memory; the two untried servers go first afterwards; queue cap). `node odds-api/golfcourse.test.js` (309 checks, was 219: the OSM API fixture and the Overpass fixture written from the same coordinates give deep-equal courses, incl. a course and a pond drawn as relations; end to end the holes for way/100 through the OSM API equal the holes through Overpass; box padding 150 m; one read when the box is known, two when not; node course; too big → 404 with nothing asked; API 400 / 509 / 500 → 502; 404 / 410 → 404; stale; Nominatim list mapped / filtered / `'?'`; 10-minute memory; Overpass back → full list). `node odds-api/courts.test.js` (section 5c: mapping, filter, 60 m de-dup, `partial` + note, three searches 1.1 s apart, 10-minute memory, 7-day memory, empty state, one search failing, 429, Google + Overpass both down). **Old checks that changed ON PURPOSE** because the behaviour changed: Overpass tries per request (8 → 4, 4 → 2, "all four servers" → two), the 12 s / 36 s limits (now 8 s / 18 s), and "the next request tries Overpass again" (now only after 10 minutes). In those old checks the fallbacks are switched off in the fakes, so they still prove the 502 / stale paths. Run everything: `for f in odds-api/*.test.js; do node $f; done`.
- **NOT VERIFIED (the build shell is refused by nominatim.openstreetmap.org and api.openstreetmap.org: one try each, both refused by the sandbox proxy):** every real answer. Least sure, in order: (1) whether Nominatim answers a bare `q=golf course` / `q=pickleball` with `viewbox` + `bounded=1` the way the code expects (a list of the things of that kind / name inside the box), and how complete that list is; (2) the field names in its jsonv2 answer: the code reads `category` (falls back to `class`), `type`, `osm_type`, `osm_id`, `name` (falls back to the first part of `display_name`), `lat`, `lon`, `boundingbox` [south, north, west, east], `extratags`, `address.{house_number, road, city|town|village, state, postcode}`; (3) whether `extratags` carries `sport` / `holes`; (4) the OSM API's JSON (`elements` with `nodes` on ways and `members[{type, ref, role}]` on relations) and that Render's address is not blocked or rate-limited there; (5) how many nodes a real course box returns (a course inside a densely mapped city could pass 50,000 → 400 → 502). Browser checks for the manager:
  - `https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/search?format=jsonv2&q=golf%20course&viewbox=-98.56,26.51,-97.90,25.90&bounded=1&limit=40&extratags=1` (McAllen TX, about 21 miles each way: expect a list where `category` is `leisure` and `type` is `golf_course`)
  - `https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/search?format=jsonv2&q=pickleball&viewbox=-98.49,26.43,-97.97,25.97&bounded=1&limit=40&extratags=1&addressdetails=1` (McAllen TX, 16 miles each way)
  - `https://api.openstreetmap.org/api/0.6/map.json?bbox=-98.2400,26.2000,-98.2300,26.2080` (a small box in McAllen: expect `elements` with nodes `lat` / `lon`, ways with `nodes`)
  - then, after the deploy: `https://calcutta-odds.onrender.com/golfcourse/search?q=McAllen,%20TX&r=20` , `https://calcutta-odds.onrender.com/courts?q=McAllen,%20TX&r=15` , and `/golfcourse/holes?id=<an id from the first>`; read the `answered by` lines in the Render log.
- OPEN / DECISIONS for the manager: (1) `holesMapped:'?'` (a string in a number field) vs `null` + the tiny phone change above; (2) the stale FULL list is preferred over a fresh short list for courts and golf search (a 7-hour-old complete list beats a fresh incomplete one): say if the owner would rather always see fresh; (3) if the Nominatim checks above show the bare-phrase searches do not work, the fix is in two small places (`asks` in `courts.js` `fromNominatim`, the query list in `golfcourse.js` `nomiCourses`); (4) the short court list is thin by nature: a Google Places key in My Apps settings remains the real answer for courts; (5) for golf, a paid or self-hosted Overpass (or the key-based golf course APIs) would remove the dependence on volunteers' servers altogether.

## v266 — Nov 16 2026: 🔔 phone alerts for my bets (app closed), every score and the final. BUILT, SWITCHED OFF (`BETPUSH_ENABLED` unset)
- OWNER: "Alerts on my phone for my bets?" (the v260 pop-up, but as a real notification with Oddable closed), then "Live update every score.", then "Current users will get a notification to add notifications?". Branch `bet-push`, one commit, not pushed. NOTHING changes for anyone until the server switch is on.
- **How push worked before this build (read end to end):** the phone gets My Apps' public key from `POST /acct/push-key` (acct.js → My Apps `/api/v1/push/key`), subscribes in the browser (gate.js `pushOn`, the "Turn on renewal reminders" button in the Account box) and posts the subscription to `POST /acct/push` (acct.js → My Apps `/api/v1/push/subscribe` → table `ma_push`, one row per device, keyed by app + account). My Apps holds `VAPID_PUBLIC` / `VAPID_PRIVATE` and is the ONLY sender (`web-push`): `pushRun` (renewal reminders at 5 / 3 / 1 / 0 days, tag `renew-<slug>`), `pushTest` (console only), owner alerts to the owner's own devices. The Oddable server holds no push key and has never sent a push. At the start of this build My Apps had no route to send a given title / body to an account's devices; My Apps build .36 added it (next line).
- **SENDER = option A: My Apps `POST /api/v1/push/send`** (My Apps build .36, its HANDOFF "API contract 4a"; the My Apps repo was not touched from here). Oddable sends `{ slug:"oddable", secret, account, title, body, tag, url }` through acct.js `ma()` and reads `{ ok:true, sent:<devices reached> }`. Kept inside its limits by `betstatus.compose` and tested: title ≤ 80, body ≤ 240 on ONE line (My Apps turns line breaks into spaces, so the bets are joined with "; " and only whole bets are kept), `tag` = `bet-<game id>` (only `A-Z a-z 0-9 : _ -`), `url` = a path inside the app (`/?open=bets`). `sent:0` = the player has no phone on file: his device flag is cleared. `429 busy` (60 a minute per player, 600 per app): that alert is dropped, not counted, no retry (the next score carries the current state). `404` / `405` / `503 nopush`: one log line and no sends for 10 minutes. NOT CHECKED against the deployed route (only the written contract; a fake of it in tests). OPEN for My Apps: its send keeps an undelivered push for as long as its own TTL says; a score alert that arrives hours late is wrong, so a short TTL for these would be better (the same `tag` at least replaces a late one with the newest).
- **Server switch:** `BETPUSH_ENABLED` on calcutta-odds (the Render service named ODDABLE): only `1` = on. Unset: `start()` sets no timer; no ESPN / database / My Apps call; `/betpush/*` answer "not switched on" without reading anything; `/appcfg` is byte-identical to v262. `BETPUSH_KEEPAWAKE` (default on when enabled; `0` = off): see keep-awake below. `BETPUSH_URL` (optional): where a tapped alert opens, a path inside the app, default `/?open=bets`. ROLL BACK = set `BETPUSH_ENABLED=0` (or remove it): the watcher stops at the next restart, the card and setting go quiet on the next page load.
- **Files.** NEW `odds-api/betstatus.js` (pure: ESPN event → game, grading, wording, change rules), `odds-api/betwatch.js` (watcher, sender, `/betpush/*`), tests `betstatus.test.js`, `betwatch.test.js`, `betpush.sw.test.js`, `scripts/betpush/shots.js`. `server.js`: one route line, one start line. `appcfg.js`: `/appcfg` adds `betpush:true` ONLY when the switch is on (how the page learns it with no extra request). `pics/sw.js`: push + notificationclick. `pics/index.html`: CSS, `#bpset` in ⓘ Disclosures, `#bpinv` slot above the Games list, one hook in `bcLoad`, one self-contained `<script>` block before the gate loader. NOT touched: gate.js, sync.js, groups*.js, events.js, acct.js.
- **Watcher (`betwatch.tick`).** One self-rearming timer. FAST = every **30 s** while a game that a subscribed player has an open bet on is live or within 20 minutes of its start; otherwise a look every 10 minutes (5 when games are on but nobody subscribed has a bet on them). The 10-minute look reads today's scoreboard of the six leagues (NFL, college football, NBA, college basketball, MLB, NHL; the league paths of grade.js `LG`, US-Eastern date, plus yesterday's before 6 AM Eastern): 6 ESPN reads per 10 minutes, and only when at least one player is subscribed. The fast look reads only the boards that hold a watched game (at most 8). grade.js's own `day()` reader was NOT reused: it caches a day for 10 minutes (right for Groups grading, useless for live scores) and is Groups code. A college Saturday board is ~1 MB every 30 s: fine on a paid instance, heavy on the free one.
- **Who gets an alert:** accounts with the setting on, a phone registered, and an OPEN bet (no `fin`, no `res`, not sold) on that game in their synced bets row. Only subscribed players' own rows are read (`sync.dbGet(rid)`, sync.js unchanged), 5 at a time, each at most once per 5 minutes, at most 300 players per rebuild; the game → players index is rebuilt every 5 minutes, so a bet placed during a game starts alerting within 5 minutes (its first look is silent). ONLY phone + PIN accounts: a bets row belongs to a phone number, and an account id is a one-way code of the row id (`acct.phoneEmail`; found once through `sync.dbIds` when the setting is turned on, then stored). Email accounts and the owner-PIN sign-in get "Sign in with your cell number and PIN to get alerts for your bets".
- **What is sent** (`betstatus.compose`): title `LAC 17, BAL 7 · 2nd 1:56`; body = his bets on that game, at most 3, joined with "; ", then `+N more`: `LAC +4.5: covering by 14.5 right now; Over 44.5: needs 21 more right now; BAL ML: losing by 10 right now; +2 more`; parlay leg `LAC -3 (3-leg parlay): covering by 7 right now · 2 of 3 legs hitting` (the phone's count: legs won or winning now; "parlay lost" once a leg has lost). Correction: `Score corrected: …`. Final: `Final: LAC 27, BAL 20` with `LAC +4.5 won` / `lost` / `push` (no dollar amount: the phone assumes −110 when the odds are missing, the server does not guess). `tag` = `bet-<game id>`, so a new alert REPLACES the older one for that game on the lock screen; `url` opens My Bets.
- **Grading = the phone's, stricter.** `betstatus.test.js` cuts `legGrade` / `betGradeOne` / `betGradeLive` / `scPill` out of `pics/index.html` and runs them beside the server's: 1,568 single bets and 252 parlays, same state, same margin, same words (arrows dropped). So a change to the phone's grading that is not made on the server FAILS that test. Stricter on purpose: the side must be one of the two teams (the phone treats anything that is not the home team as the away team; WAS / WSH, JAX / JAC, LAR / LA all match), the type must be sp / ml / ou / tot, the line must be a number. Anything else, a player prop, or a parlay leg whose game is not on today's boards = the bet's name only (no status, no leg count). "Right now" is never called a result.
- **Rules.** First look at a game is silent. EVERY score change in a watched game = one alert per bettor (football, hockey, baseball; the app has no soccer bets); two scores inside one 30 s poll = one alert with the new score. Basketball: quarter ends / halftime and the final by default, EVERY score if the player picks it ("Every score in basketball can mean 150+ alerts a game."). Kickoff is not an alert. A final game never alerts again. Safety cap **400 a day per player** (Central day). An alert is held back while that player's app is open with its own score pop-ups on (the page posts `/betpush/here` every 25 s; 45 s memory). Sent 5 at a time, at most 300 per pass. My Apps answering `sent:0` = no phone left: the player's device flag is cleared and the setting shows Off until he turns it on again.
- **Restart / sleep.** Last score per game and the day's counts are stored in the shared row `lock:betpushst` (settings: `lock:betpush`; the `lock:` prefix keeps both out of player lists and owner counts). After a restart with a look less than 10 minutes old the comparison simply continues (nothing repeated, nothing lost). Older than 10 minutes (the server slept, ESPN was away): the first look is silent again. No burst either way.
- **Keep awake.** While, and only while, a watched game is LIVE the watcher asks for `RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL/health` every 10 minutes. FOUND: `server.js` already does this ALL the time unless `KEEP_AWAKE=0`, so on today's settings the instance does not sleep anyway and this ping is a second, redundant one; it matters only if `KEEP_AWAKE=0` is ever set. A paid instance removes the need for both.
- **Routes** (`x-token` only; no new request header; the CORS allow-list is unchanged): `GET /betpush/status` → `{enabled, on, hasDevice, mode, bb, finals, test}`; `POST /betpush/set {on, mode:'bets'|'final'|'off', finals, bb:'q'|'all', subscription?}` (a `subscription` is passed to My Apps `/api/v1/push/subscribe` for the same account, exactly what `/acct/push` relays for the renewal reminders: ONE `ma_push` row per device serves reminders, broadcasts and bet alerts; it also marks the account as having a phone, and the page sets `odd-push` so the Account box says reminders are on); `POST /betpush/test` (one test alert, 30 s apart; tag `oddable-test`); `POST /betpush/here`. `mode:'final'` (finals only) and `finals:false` (no final) work on the server but have NO control on the phone yet.
- **Phone.** ⓘ Disclosures, under "Score pop-ups": "Phone alerts for my bets: every score, and the final." Off / On, helper "For when Oddable is closed. Needs Oddable added to your Home Screen on iPhone or iPad, and notifications allowed."; when on: "Basketball: Quarter ends and final / Every score" with its warning, and "Send me a test". Turning on runs ONE flow (`turnOn`, the same for the card): server off → "Alerts are not switched on at the server yet."; iPhone / iPad in a browser tab (iPadOS reports "Macintosh" with touch points) → "Add to Home Screen first. 1. Tap Share. 2. Add to Home Screen. 3. Open Oddable from that icon and tap Turn on again."; old iOS → "Alerts need iOS or iPadOS 16.4 or newer."; the permission question (asked ONLY on this tap); denied → "Blocked in Settings. …"; then key (`/acct/push-key`), subscribe, `/betpush/set`; "Allowed. Alerts are on for this phone." The select shows On only when the account is on AND this device is subscribed.
- **Invitation card** (`#bpinv`, in the page flow above the Games list; never fixed, never a modal): "🔔 Get score alerts for your bets. A notification every time a game you bet on scores, even with Oddable closed." [Turn on] [Not now]. Shown when `/appcfg` said `betpush:true`, `/betpush/status` says enabled, alerts are not on for this device, the player is signed in; never in `?mirror=1`, behind the sign-in gate, or first shown while a sheet / team page / Multiview / live view / dashboard is open; once per app open. On My Bets it shows only for a player with an open bet today. "Not now" = 14 days; the second = never (`localStorage pics-bpinv {n,t}`); the setting in Disclosures always stays. A phone that already allowed notifications (renewal reminders) gets the one-line version "Score alerts for your bets are available. [Turn on] [✕]" and no permission question. The iPhone-tab path keeps the card and is not a dismissal.
- **Requests from the page:** switch off = ZERO new requests (the flag rides on the `/appcfg` call the page already makes once per load). Switch on = one `GET /betpush/status` per load for a signed-in player; `/betpush/here` every 25 s only while alerts are on for that device.
- **Service worker** (`pics/sw.js`, `pics-v266`; this build owns it). PUSH: title / body / tag as sent; `actions` are now passed on (the reminder's "Pay now" button; at most two, only `action` + `title`); `renotify` for `bet-…` tags or when the payload asks. With an Oddable window VISIBLE a bet alert is skipped on Android / desktop Chrome (the in-app pop-up has it) but ALWAYS shown on iPhone / iPad: Safari takes the permission away from a site whose pushes show nothing, so the brief's "skip in the worker" was not done there (the `/betpush/here` hold-back does it on the server instead). TAP (body or a button, `action:'pay'` = the same url): the payload `url` is followed. A path inside Oddable: an open window is brought forward and navigated there (`/pga.html`, `/?pay=1`), or opened if none; a bet alert (`?open=bets`) tells the open window to show My Bets with no reload (`bp-open`). An absolute address is followed ONLY on `https://my-apps-management.onrender.com` (the hosted pay page of the renewal reminders: opened in a new window); any other address goes to the front page. Before v266 a tap only focused an open window and ignored the url.
- **TESTS.** `node odds-api/betstatus.test.js` 6,909 checks; `node odds-api/betwatch.test.js` 215 (switch off in 4 spellings = no ESPN / database / My Apps call and no timer; first look silent; one alert per bettor per score; correction; final; basketball both choices incl. a missed break; hockey / baseball / college football; finals-only, no-final, app open; cap 400 and the new day; quick restart, restart with a new score, long sleep; keep-awake only while live; pacing and read counts; ESPN down; My Apps 404 / 503 / 429 / no phone / throwing; My Apps' field limits; every route); `node odds-api/betpush.sw.test.js` 112 (bet alert, renewal reminder with its button, broadcast: shown and tapped, on Android / iPhone / iPad / Chrome on iPhone / desktop; 9 addresses a tap must refuse); `appcfg.test.js` (+ the flag); all 17 `odds-api/*.test.js` and `scripts/pga/pga.test.js` pass. Differential, origin/main vs this branch, no env: 33 of 33 existing-route answers identical (status, headers, body), outbound calls and timers identical (9 each). Playwright `scripts/betpush/shots.js` at 390 / 430 / 820 / 1180: 584 of 584 (146 a width; serve `pics/` on 8813; the odds server, ESPN, the push service and the clock are fakes, service workers blocked; the page fully loaded with one live game): switch off = no card, zero `/betpush` requests, one `/appcfg` as before, choosing On says the server is off; signed out; `?mirror=1`; the card's text, place (in the flow, above the list), 44 px buttons; the My Bets banner, bottom bar and header menus in the same place and size with and without the card (banner up, on Games and on My Bets) and never under or over it; no sideways scroll; Not now (stored, not again this app open, hidden on day 13.9, back on day 14.1, second = never, the setting still works); Turn on (asked once and only on the tap, calls in order, body, token, confirm, basketball menu, test, off, the 25 s "app is open" calls start and stop); blocked / dismissed question / no push / server refusal; iPhone tab and iPad-as-Macintosh tab (steps shown, card kept, not a dismissal, there next time), a real Mac, iPhone from the Home Screen, old iOS; the one-line version (no question, no key); already on; on elsewhere; gate; a sheet open; My Bets with / without an open bet today; `?open=bets`; 0 page errors. One flake was seen in an earlier run (a view change checked after one clock jump that landed 0.6 s short: Playwright's clock also runs in real time); the check now jumps twice and the page also re-checks the card on any tap; 120 repeats of that case then passed. Screenshots were looked at.
- **NOT VERIFIED.** A real push to a real phone (impossible here: headless browsers have no push service); the deployed My Apps `/api/v1/push/send` (built against its written contract); the iPhone Home Screen flow on a device; real ESPN feeds (fakes in ESPN's shape); the live database rows; the real cross-origin calls from steves-pics.onrender.com (`GET /betpush/status` with `x-token` sends a preflight: GET and x-token are already allowed, check it live); whether iOS replaces by `tag` and honours `renotify` the way Android does; load on the free instance with a ~1 MB college board every 30 s.
- **THE OWNER, on his phone, once it is switched on:** 1. open Oddable from its Home Screen icon (iPhone / iPad: Share → Add to Home Screen first); 2. sign in with cell number + PIN; 3. tap "Turn on" on the card (or ⓘ Disclosures → Phone alerts → On) and allow notifications; 4. "Send me a test".
- **TO SWITCH ON (manager):** 1. My Apps build .36 live with `VAPID_PUBLIC` / `VAPID_PRIVATE` (the owner already got an owner alert on his iPad, so they are set). 2. Render → ODDABLE (calcutta-odds) → Environment → add `BETPUSH_ENABLED` = `1` (exactly that name and value), save, let it redeploy. 3. Owner does the four steps above; the test alert arrives. 4. Watch one live game with a bet on it. Left OFF by default on purpose: nothing here has met a real phone or the deployed My Apps route. Consider a paid instance first.
- **DECISIONS LEFT:** parlay wording "2 of 3 legs hitting" (the phone's count) instead of the brief's "alive"; no dollar amount on a final; 30 s polling on the free instance; whether finals-only / no-final need a control on the phone; email-account players cannot use it (their bets are not tied to the account); the redundant keep-awake; a stale 5-minute bets cache can alert once on a bet deleted moments ago.
- Version v266: index.html (comment, header pill, footer "Oddable v266 (phone alerts for my bets · PGA Tour leaderboard · score pop-ups)", trophy.js / gate.js / alert-frozen.json `?v=266`), sports.html `gate.js?v=266`, sw.js `pics-v266`, pickleball.html (comment, `pickleball.js?v=266`, footer), pga.html (comment, `pga.js?v=266`, `gate.js?v=266`, footer). Other coders hold v263 to v265: the manager fixes the version lines at merge. Picks, locks, the Alert and the model were not touched.

## v267 — Nov 16 2026: 📣 Send to everyone (owner broadcast + in-app message) and ⏳ days-left reminders with Pay now
- Owner (Nov 16 2026): "Ability to send to every user a notification from my Oddable app and from my app." / "Add alerts to the free days left at 5 days left and every day with a pay now." / "On Monday of each week, remind days left and a pay now." My Apps builds .36 (broadcast engine + console screen, "API contract 4" in its HANDOFF) and .37 (the daily / Monday PHONE reminders) hold the other half. **Needs My Apps .36+ deployed and the calcutta-odds deploy; until then the owner's send answers "My Apps is not ready for this yet." and no user sees anything new.**
- Coder agent, branch `broadcast`, built on v263 (7c31070) while v264 (golf-gps), v265 (pga-wide) and v266 (bet-push) were in flight. **The manager settles the version lines at merge. `pics/sw.js` was NOT touched (bet-push owns it): its `pics-v###` still has to be set at merge. `pics/golfgps.html` (v264) is not in this base: it needs the v267 bump at merge too.**
- **FREE FOR NOW IS UNCHANGED.** An account with no end date sees nothing new. Its page makes the same requests as v263 plus two: the new file `announce.js`, and (signed in only) ONE `GET /acct/announce` per app open. Signed out or gate off: `announce.js` only. Proved against a v263 copy at four sizes (request lists, same `/acct` calls, same picture).

### Server (odds-api)
- **New `odds-api/announce.js`** (test `announce.test.js`, 26 tests), wired in `server.js` one line above the `/acct` line. `acct.js` was not edited at all.
  - `POST /acct/broadcast` **OWNER ONLY**: `acct.ownerOk(req)` (owner-PIN token, or owner-phone token + the dashboard unlock in `x-dash`, the same rule as `/acct/dash`). Everyone else: 403 "Owner only" (signed-in customer), 401 "Sign in again" (no / bad token), 401 `need:"owner-pin"` (owner phone without the unlock); My Apps is never called for them. **No new request header**, so the CORS Allow-Headers list is unchanged.
  - Body `{title, body, link?, audience?, test?}`. Title 1-60, message 1-240, plain text (control characters and line breaks become spaces). `audience` = `all` (default) / `active` / `expired`. `link` = a NAME from the fixed list `LINKS` (`home` `/`, `bets` `/?open=bets`, `live` `/?open=live`, `sports` `/sports.html`, `pga` `/pga.html`, `pickleball` `/pickleball.html`, `golfgps` `/golfgps.html`); the URL is what goes to My Apps. Anything else = 400 and nothing is sent. Only those fields are forwarded (to `/api/v1/broadcast`); the answer is rebuilt as `{ok, test, id, people, devices, sent}` (`id` is null on a test).
  - Friendly errors, never My Apps' own text: `limit` 429 "You have sent 5 today. Try again tomorrow."; `dup` 409 "You already sent this same message in the last 10 minutes."; `busy` 429; `contact` 400 "Take the phone number or email out of the message."; My Apps unreachable / 404 / 5xx / not set up = 502 "My Apps is not ready for this yet."; any other 400 = "My Apps did not take that message. Check the words and try again."; a refused secret = "Could not send. Please try again later."
  - Slow-down like the neighbouring owner routes: 8 per 10 minutes per address, 16 per 10 minutes overall, counted only AFTER the owner check (a stranger cannot use up the owner's tries). A body over 20 KB gets a 400, not a dropped connection.
  - `GET /acct/announce` (signed-in token; POST also accepted): asks My Apps `/api/v1/announce/for {account}` with the account FROM THE TOKEN, one read per account per 5 minutes (a failed read is tried again after 1 minute, 6 s limit), 30 per 10 minutes per account. Always answers `200 {ok:true, announce|null}` when signed in (a My Apps failure is `null`, never an error on the phone). Owner-PIN token and gate off = `null` without calling My Apps.
  - `clean(a)` is the ONE check for an announcement on its way to a phone: id (1-64 of `A-Z a-z 0-9 _ . : -`, a number is fine), title <= 60, body <= 240, `at` (ISO text or milliseconds) not in the future and not older than 14 days; `link` through the fixed list (a name or its exact URL), anything else becomes `""`. Too long / wrong type = the WHOLE announcement is dropped, never cut. It returns a new object with five fields.
- **`appsettings.js`**: in the My Apps answer `announce` sits NEXT TO `settings` (contract 4c), so the cached read now keeps both; `get()` is unchanged (courts.js still gets only the settings) and the new `announce()` returns the raw announcement from the SAME read (never a second My Apps call). A good read that says `null` clears it (that is how "Hide" in My Apps reaches the phones); while My Apps is down the last one is kept until it is 14 days old.
- **`appcfg.js`** (`GET /appcfg`, public, whitelist): adds `announce: {id, title, body, link, at}` when `clean()` passes, else the field is absent. Rebuilt field by field in `handle()` as before. `appcfg.test.js` still proves `google_places_key` never leaves (now also with an announcement that carries extra fields), plus valid / oversized / wrong types / bad links / script tags as plain text / Hide / an `announce` wrongly placed inside `settings` is ignored / one shared read with courts.

### Phone
- **New `pics/announce.js`** (index.html only, loaded in the `document.write` line, so never in `?mirror=1`). index.html itself got: the version bump, that script tag, and ONE hook in `bcLoad` (`window.ODDCFG = j; oddAnn(j)`): the announcement rides the `/appcfg` read the page already made, no second read.
  - **Message card**: first thing inside `<main>` (normal page flow, under the header; nothing floats, so it cannot cover or sit under the My Bets banner, the menus, the bottom bar or the v260 score pop-up). "📣 title", the message, the date, **Open** (only with a link) and **✕ Dismiss**. All text with `textContent`. Dismissed ids are kept on the device (`odd-ann`). A new id shows again.
  - **Signed-in users: once per app open** the phone also asks `GET /acct/announce` (chosen over a "may be targeted" flag in `/appcfg`: the contract has no such flag and the call is one small cached read). The NEWER of the two that is not dismissed is shown. No polling, no retry; a failed read changes nothing and never signs anyone out.
  - **ⓘ Disclosures → "📣 Messages from Oddable (n)"**: the last 14 days seen on this device (at most 12), so a dismissed one can be read again. Absent when there are none. A message aimed at one account is listed only for that person on the phone (`sync-owner` stamp). **Hide**: the server always answers with the latest live message, so anything saved that is newer than a good answer (or everything, when the answer is none) leaves the card and the list.
  - **Open** goes to: Home / My Bets / Live (`setView`), or the page (sports.html, pga.html, pickleball.html, golfgps.html). `/?open=bets` and `/?open=live` are also handled on load (what a phone alert's link opens) and the address is cleaned.
- **Days-left card** (same slot and look, amber edge, always ABOVE a message): for a signed-in account that HAS an end date and is still open. Every day of the last 5 days ("⏳ 5 free days left" on a trial, "⏳ 5 days left" paid, "1 day left", "Ends today") and on every MONDAY (device time) however far the end is ("⏳ 23 days left"). Line "Keep your picks and bets. $99 a month." with the account's real price (no price part when it is 0 or unknown). **Pay now** opens the existing subscribe screen; **Request free time** (only at 5 days or less) opens Disclosures and scrolls to the existing box; **✕ Not today** hides it for that account until tomorrow (`odd-dlc` = account|date). Not shown: no end date, the owner, gate off, an ended account (it has the wall), a payment waiting for confirmation. It is drawn from what the gate already read (`/acct/me`): **no request of its own**.
- **`pics/gate.js`** (small): `oddPay()` opens the ONE subscribe screen for an account that still has time (first line "3 free days left. $99 per month unlocks everything.", a **← Back to Oddable** button at the top that asks the server nothing; above 5 days the free-time box is left off). `oddMe()` gives the card the days / wording / price (no token). `oddTimeBox()`. The old "ends in N days — tap to renew" toast now opens the same screen through `oddPay()` (it had no way back before). **`/?pay=1`**: after sign-in go straight to that screen (index.html, sports.html, pga.html); with no end date or for the owner it does nothing; the address is cleaned.
- **Owner Dashboard (`pics/dash.js`)**: card "📣 Send to everyone" on the unlocked dashboard home (it is part of `home()`, which only exists once the server has given the players to the owner; the locked PIN screen and a customer never get it). "✏️ Write a message" opens: Title (60, counter), Message (240, counter), Who (Everyone / Paid or trial only / Expired only), Open to (the fixed list), a live preview drawn by the same code as the phone card, **Send test to my devices**, **Send to everyone** (label follows Who) with a confirm step that repeats the text, and Close. After sending: "Sent: 12 people, 7 devices." and a "Sent today" list (this session only). What was typed survives a repaint (period chips, Refresh) and every error. Errors are the server's sentences, shown as text.
- Version v267: index.html (comment, header pill, footer "Oddable v267 (messages from Oddable · days-left reminders)", trophy.js / gate.js / alert-frozen.json / **announce.js / dash.js** `?v=267`; dash.js had been stuck at `?v=219`), sports.html `gate.js?v=267`, pga.html (comment, footer, pga.js, gate.js), pickleball.html (comment, footer, pickleball.js). NOT sw.js, NOT golfgps.html (see the top of this entry).

### Decisions (coder; manager / Steve to confirm)
1. 2026-11-16 — The contract has no "how many will get it" call for an app, so the confirm step says "everyone with an Oddable account" (or "with paid or trial access" / "whose access has ended") with no number. The numbers come back after the send. Why: no dry count in API contract 4b.
2. 2026-11-16 — Signed-in phones ask `/acct/announce` once per app open instead of waiting for a flag. Why: no flag in the contract; one cached read.
3. 2026-11-16 — The message card shows on every view of index.html (Games, Live, My Bets, Survivor), not only the games list. sports.html / pga.html / pickleball.html do NOT show it (skipped; `announce.js` is index-only) but `/?pay=1` works on sports.html and pga.html.
4. 2026-11-16 — No days-left card while a payment waits for confirmation. Why: he already paid; nagging him to pay again is wrong.
5. 2026-11-16 — Trial or paid wording comes from `source === 'trial'` in `/acct/me`. My Apps .37 also returns `days_left`, `trial` and `pay_url` on `/api/v1/account`; they are NOT passed through (acct.js was off limits: "add routes only"). The phone counts calendar days to `expires_on` on the device, as v263's "Request free time" rule does.
6. 2026-11-16 — The card's "Pay now" opens Oddable's own subscribe screen; the PHONE reminders from My Apps open My Apps' hosted pay page (`pay_url`). Two places to pay. `/?pay=1` is there if My Apps should point at Oddable instead.
7. 2026-11-16 — "Monday" and "tomorrow" are the phone's own clock; "Not today" is per account per device.
8. 2026-11-16 — Open-to list = the four My Apps offers (`/`, `/sports.html`, `/pga.html`, `/pickleball.html`) + `/?open=bets`, `/?open=live`, `/golfgps.html`. A message sent from the My Apps console can only use the first four.
