- Server: every `odds-api/*.test.js` passes (17 files). `announce.test.js` 26 tests; `appcfg.test.js` gained sections 10-16; `acct.test.js` 31 unchanged. Nine mutations (no owner check, any link passes, no length check, no cache, body passed through, upstream text, no rebuild, raw announcement, Hide ignored) each failed a test. The real server was booted locally: preflight for `content-type,x-token,x-dash` answers 204 with the unchanged header list; `/acct/broadcast` 401 without a token, 502 "not ready" for the owner with My Apps unreachable; `/acct/announce` 401 / null; `/appcfg` unchanged shape; `/acct/me` still served by acct.js.
- Playwright (gate ACTIVE, service workers blocked, everything outside the page mocked, the clock fixed to a Wednesday / Monday / Tuesday because the build day was a Monday) at 390x844, 430x932, 820x1180, 1180x820; scripts in the session scratchpad `bcast/` (`t_user.js`, `t_days.js`, `t_owner.js`); 0 page errors. ## v269 — Nov 16 2026: 📣 the "Send to everyone" confirm step says how many people get it (count first, nothing sent)
- Owner (Nov 16 2026): why does the Oddable dashboard's confirm step not show how many people the message reaches before sending (the My Apps console does)? v267 decision 1 said "no dry count in the contract"; My Apps build .39 adds one, so this replaces that decision. Coder on `main` at v267 (aa96e93) while `guest-mode` (v268: gate.js, index.html, acct.js `/acct/status`) was in flight in another worktree: only `odds-api/announce.js`, `odds-api/announce.test.js`, `pics/dash.js`, the version lines and this file were touched. **Committed locally, not pushed; the manager settles the version lines against v268 at merge.**
- **Needs My Apps .39 deployed AND answering `caps:["broadcast_dry"]` (see the gate). Until then the confirm step looks like v267 plus the small note "Count not available right now." and sending works as before.**

### The capability gate (read before touching `/acct/broadcast`)
- My Apps .39: `POST /api/v1/broadcast` with `dry:true` answers `{ok:true, dry:true, people, alerts, devices, left}` (people who will see it in the app, how many of them have phone alerts on, devices, sends left today), same validation and error codes as a real send, sends and stores nothing; `dry` with `test` is a 400. **An OLDER My Apps does not know `dry` and would SEND the message for real.**
- So `odds-api/announce.js` never sends a dry call to `/api/v1/broadcast` unless My Apps has said it can count: `canDry()` asks `POST /api/v1/app/settings` and needs `ok:true` and a TOP-LEVEL `caps` ARRAY that holds the text `broadcast_dry`. Anything else (field missing, a string, an object, inside `settings`, other spelling, 404 / 5xx / refused / down / slower than 6 s) = no.
  - "Yes" is kept 5 minutes, "no" 1 minute, then asked again (in memory, per process: a restart asks again). So an updated My Apps starts counting within a minute, and a My Apps rolled BACK stops getting dry calls within 5 minutes.
  - The read is announce.js's own call (`appsettings.js` was off limits and its cache keeps only `settings` + `announce`). One small extra My Apps read per 5 minutes, only while the owner is using the count. If `appsettings.js` later keeps `caps`, `canDry()` can use that read instead.
  - No capability: `/acct/broadcast` with `dry:true` answers `200 {ok:false, code:'nodry', error:'nodry'}` and `/api/v1/broadcast` is NOT called.
  - Second lock: a count is shown only when My Apps' answer itself carries `dry:true`. `ok:true` WITHOUT it (My Apps claimed the capability but behaved like a send) = `502 code:'drysent'` "My Apps may have SENT this message instead of counting. Check My Apps before you send it again.", counting is switched off for a minute, and the phone does not open the confirm step.
  - **The My Apps coder must add `caps:["broadcast_dry"]` to the `/api/v1/app/settings` answer in build .39** (top level, next to `settings` and `announce`). Without it Oddable never counts.

### Server (`odds-api/announce.js`)
- `POST /acct/broadcast` accepts `dry` (strictly `true`; `false` / absent / null = a real send; any other type = 400 `bad`, never a send). Owner only exactly as before (the owner check is the first thing in the route, before `dry` is even read). `dry` + `test:true` = 400. Same title / message / audience / link checks. Forwarded as the same cleaned fields plus `dry:true`; **a real send or a test never carries `dry` and never reads the capability.**
- Answer rebuilt field by field: `{ok:true, dry:true, people, alerts, devices, left}` (numbers or null). Errors use the same table as a real send (`limit`, `dup`, `busy`, `contact`, other 400, not ready).
- A count has its own slow-down (20 per 10 minutes per address, 40 overall) so counting does not use up the 8 / 16 real sends.

### Phone (`pics/dash.js`)
- Tapping **Send to everyone** (or "Send to paid or trial" / "Send to expired") now asks for the count first (button reads "Counting…", all buttons off), then opens the confirm step: **"Send to 42 people?"** and under it "9 of them have phone alerts on (11 devices) and get it on the phone now. Everyone else sees it the next time they open Oddable. 3 sends left today. This cannot be unsent." Other cases: "Send to 1 person?" / "1 of them has ... and gets it"; "None of them has phone alerts on. They see it the next time they open Oddable."; "All 5 have phone alerts on (8 devices) ..."; 0 people = "Nobody is in this group right now, so nobody would see it." (the Yes button stays); a count with no alerts number = a general sentence; no `left` = that part is left out.
- **No count** (`nodry`, My Apps not ready, slow-down, 5xx, no network, an answer without `dry:true` or without a people number): the v267 wording ("Send this to everyone with an Oddable account?" ...) plus the small note "Count not available right now." Sending still works.
- **Errors from the count** (`contact`, `limit`, `dup`, any 400, `drysent`): the server's sentence in red on the form, the confirm step does NOT open, the text is kept, nothing is sent, no retry. 401 `owner-pin` = the PIN screen, as on a send.
- "Yes, send now" is the same real send as v267 (no `dry`). "Send test to my devices" is not counted first. No drill-down on the counts (not asked for here).
- Version v269 on the whole list: index.html (comment, header pill, footer, trophy.js / gate.js / alert-frozen.json / announce.js / dash.js `?v=269`), sports.html `gate.js`, pga.html (comment, footer, pga.js, gate.js), golfgps.html (comment, footer, leaflet css + js, golfgps.js, gate.js), pickleball.html (comment, footer, pickleball.js), sw.js `pics-v269`.

### Decisions
1. 2026-11-16 — The capability is read by announce.js itself and re-checked every 5 minutes instead of "once per process". Why: a My Apps rollback after a first good answer would otherwise turn a count into a real send.
2. 2026-11-16 — `nodry` is a 200 with `ok:false` (it is not a failure of the request; the phone just shows the wording without a number).
3. 2026-11-16 — A count that reaches 0 people still lets the owner send (My Apps decides what a send to nobody does); the sentence says nobody would see it.
4. 2026-11-16 — `busy` / `slow` / not ready on the COUNT fall back to the no-number confirm step rather than blocking; the real send then gives its own sentence if it is still refused.

### Tests
- `odds-api/announce.test.js` 36 tests (10 new: capability absent in 11 shapes + down / slow = `nodry` and NO `/api/v1/broadcast` call; "no" kept 1 minute; present = forwarded with `dry:true`; "yes" expires after 5 minutes; a real send / test never carries `dry`; bad `dry` types and `dry`+`test` = 400; owner only; same error sentences; answer without `dry:true` = `drysent`; separate slow-down). Nine mutations of the gate each failed a test. Every `odds-api/*.test.js` passes (23 files).
- Playwright (owner dashboard, everything outside the page mocked, service workers blocked) at 390x844 and 1180x820: 308 of 308 checks, 0 page errors. Script and pictures in the session scratchpad `dry/` (`t.js`, `lib.js`).

### NOT verified / open
- The real My Apps .39 (not committed when this was written: the contract in the task brief was faked) and the live site / a real phone. **After both deploys: open the real dashboard in Chrome, tap Send to everyone, check the number against the My Apps console, then Back (nothing must be sent).**
- Whether My Apps counts a dry call against its `busy` limit, and what `left` says when 0 are left (expected: the `limit` error instead of a count).
- 430 and 820 widths were not run for this change (390 and 1180 only, as asked).

## v268 — Nov 16-17 2026: GUEST MODE (no wall on open; sign up for picks, My Bets and Groups) + an ended account is "a guest until they pay"

Coder agent, branch `guest-mode` from main 3a022df (v266). v267 (`broadcast`) was in flight on another branch at the same time: every edit here sits in marked blocks ("v268 GUEST MODE" / "v268 guest") so the two can be merged. NOT pushed, NOT merged by the coder.

**Owner decisions (his words are in "Decisions" at the top).** (1) "Open the rest": no account needed for scores, standings, PGA, Golf GPS; picks and the Lock, Groups and keeping My Bets need a (free) account. (2) An account whose time has ended is a guest until it pays; everything of theirs is locked, nothing is deleted. **FREE FOR NOW IS UNCHANGED**: no price, trial length, expiry rule or payment step was touched; an account with no end date is never "expired".

**What a visitor with no account sees (no wall).** They land straight in the app on `index.html`, `sports.html`, `pga.html`, `golfgps.html`, `pickleball.html`. The header has **Sign up free** and a smaller **Sign in**. Both, and every locked card, open the EXISTING sign-up / sign-in screen of `gate.js` (phone + PIN first, name or handle required, email second, its own trial text untouched) as a full-screen sheet with **‹ Back**. After signing up or in the page is where it was, now unlocked, **without a reload**; the card they tapped stays at the same place on the screen for the next 6 seconds while the unlocked content fills in.
- OPEN: Games list (teams, records, kickoff, live and final score, market line and total, TV, 📚 books with the best price, 🏟 Field view, 📡 Live), Live page (score, clock, down and distance, field, last play, ESPN's win-probability chart on a tap), Multiview, Best odds (for a guest: this week's lines + best price to shop + Super Bowl odds; it does not rank by a pick), Line moves, Standings, Team stats, Season, team pages (Games, Stats, Standings, Roster, News), NBA / MLB / NHL / NCAAB scoreboards and Upcoming (spread, moneyline, total, starters), PGA Tour, Golf GPS (viewing, distances, marks and colours on this phone), Pickleball incl. Find local courts.
- LOCKED (a locked card in the same slot: 🔒 + one line + the button). **Nothing locked is drawn or fetched** (never hidden with CSS): `gRender()` replaces `renderInner()` for a guest and never calls `pred()`.

| Pick surface | How it is locked for a guest |
|---|---|
| 📣 Oddable Alert of the week (Lock, road dogs, Helper, units, history) | not drawn: card "Sign up free to see the Lock and this week's Alert". `alertPull` / `alertPush` (`/alert`), `trophyLoad` (trophy-model / -hist / -season.json, alert-frozen.json) return at once: no request |
| 🎯 Prop ideas of the week (+ the 🎯 badge on a team's roster) | not drawn: card "…this week's prop ideas". `piLoad` (`/propideas`) makes no request |
| Game card: win bar, win %, "Model leans / Toss-up", spread lean and cover %, model margin, the Win / Spread record strip, 🐶 dog off blowout, 🏠 division road dog, 🏆 trophy, 🎯 teaser, 💨 wind, 💰 arb / middle tags | `gNCard()` draws the card without them; ONE card in their place "Sign up free to see Oddable's pick for this game" (finals: "…how Oddable's pick did on this game") |
| The shared pregame picks (`/locks`) and the phone's own snapshots (`lk26-*`) | `lkPull` / `lkPush` / `lockSnap` do nothing: no request, nothing stored |
| Live page cards (win % buttons, WIN / SPREAD panel) | `gGameCard()`: the same one locked card |
| Field view "Oddable picks on this game" | card in that slot |
| Multiview and every chart box | `gLiveView()`: no pregame model chart, no "our pregame pick" line |
| Stats view (model lab), Results view (graded picks, trophy / division boxes, season tiles), Rest of season (predictions) | whole view = one locked card; the season tiles (`#sums`) are emptied |
| "Best odds" ranking by our win %, Winning / Losing (bottom bar) | Best odds shows the market board instead; Winning / Losing add a locked card above it |
| Team filter tile "Our win picks", power rating / efficiency | tile replaced by a small locked card; ratings left out |
| Team page "Our picks" tab | locked card |
| Survivor (suggestions and entries) | whole page = locked card |
| Ask AI | `aiOpen` opens the sign-up sheet (guarded in gate.js); no `/ai/*` call |
| Arbitrage bar and box, wind / teaser / dog / division sheets | `arbOf()` answers null, `windLoad` makes no request, the sheets (`piOpen`, `alertDrill`, `divOpen`, `dogOpen`, `teaserOpen`, `windOpen`, …) open the sign-up sheet |
| Classic view (picks top to bottom) | a guest always gets the new look; the "Use classic view" link is hidden |
| sports.html: pick line, win %, expected margin, total lean, 🏆 Bet of the Day, Biggest Upset Pick, "Best odds" (most confident first), "Stats & locked", the model check line | `gCard()` / `gDraw()`: one locked card per game, one in the Bet of the Day slot, the two views = a locked card. The model is not built: its 45 days of scoreboards are not fetched, `spm-*` / `splk-*` are not written |
| sports.html hidden copy of the main app (`./?mirror=1`, it grades the banner and holds every pick) | not loaded for a guest |

- **My Bets**: a guest can open it and sees an explainer + "Sign up free to track and keep your bets". Add bet (header, game card, `?add=1`), screenshot import, Kalshi, Survivor entries, 📍 Watch, Today's bets and the floating banner need an account (the banner is not drawn). **No guest bet is ever stored**, so nothing is lost or merged later. The v260 score pop-up (`spScan`) and the v266 phone alerts (`bpCfg`: no invitation card, no `/betpush/*` call) are off; their two settings in ⓘ Disclosures are hidden.
- **Groups** (still dark): everything needs an account. **HOOK FOR THE GROUPS PHASE 2 CODER: `afterSignIn(target)`.** Open an invite / preview link with `window.oddSignUp('group:<type>:<code>')`; when the person is signed in, gate.js calls `window.afterSignIn('group:<type>:<code>')` (each page defines it; index.html has the `k === 'group'` comment where the invite should open). The target survives a reload (sessionStorage `odd-after`). Targets used today: `''`, `pick:<id>`, `alert`, `props`, `bets`, `add`, `bet:<id>`, `watch:<id>`, `ai`, `ai:<id>`, `surv`, `view:<name>`, `team:<abbr>`, `fv:<id>`, `golf`. After a sign-up from Add bet the bet form opens; from Ask AI the AI sheet; from Watch the game is watched.
- **Golf GPS**: a guest's mark or flag colours still work on this phone as before; where the page said "Not signed in…" it now shows the locked card ("Sign up free to share your marks with everyone who plays this course" / "…to set the flag colours for everyone…" / "…to report a change…").
- **Sign-out** lands in guest browsing (after `oddWipe`, as before), not on the wall.
- **Owner Dashboard, Account box, request free time**: signed-in only, unchanged.

**An account whose time has ended ("expired").** No subscribe wall on open: it browses like a guest, and every locked card says **"Your time has ended. Pay now to unlock your picks, bets and groups."** with **Pay now** (the existing subscribe / pay screen: Cash App step, $handle box, code, Request free time, Check again, Sign out; now a sheet with ‹ Back) and **Request free time** (the same sheet, at the v263 box). Small cards (per game) say "Your time has ended. Pay now to unlock." with Pay now. The header shows **Pay now**. My Bets shows **"Your N bets are saved and locked until you pay."** (N from the phone's saved list; no bet is drawn).
- **Data is kept.** `oddWipe` is NOT called on expiry. `sync.js` `syncNow()` does nothing while `window.__oddLock === 'expired'` (no push, no pull), Kalshi sync is skipped and no bet is graded, so the phone's `bets26` and the server row both stay as they are. The phone remembers the state in `odd-exp` (cleared on payment, sign-out and a change of person), so on later opens nothing pick- or bet-only is even requested.
- **Unlock without a reload**: every existing re-check (`/acct/me` on open, "Check again", a code, the request-status check) goes through `setMe()`; when the account is allowed again the sheet closes, the cards go, picks load, the bets and the banner are back and the sync runs once.
- **Server.** `odds-api/acct.js` `expired(account)`: true only when My Apps answers with the account and its time has ended (2-minute cache; gate off, owner, unknown account or My Apps down = false). `odds-api/betwatch.js`: no bet alert is sent to an expired account (its setting and bets row are not touched, alerts resume by themselves once paid); `/betpush/set` (turning on) and `/betpush/test` answer 403 `expired:true`; turning off and reading still work. `odds-api/groups.js`: after the existing switches say yes, a signed-in expired account gets `/groups/on` = false and 403 "Your time has ended. Pay to use Groups again." (`expired:true`) on every group call; `bet/status` keeps its safe "not in a group" answer. With Groups dark the new check never runs and My Apps is never asked. Golf GPS marks / flag changes and Ask AI were already refused for an expired account by `acct.check()`.

**THE SWITCH.** `GET /acct/status` now answers `{ gate, guest }`. `guest` follows Render env **`GUEST_MODE`** on the odds server (service "ODDABLE"): unset = ON; **`GUEST_MODE=0`** (also `false` / `off` / `no`) = the full sign-in wall before anything, and the subscribe wall for an ended account, exactly as v266. The phone keeps the last answer in `odd-guest`; if the server does not send the field (an older server) the constant `GUEST_MODE` at the top of the v268 block in `pics/gate.js` decides (on). If the status call fails on a phone with no account it browses as a guest (scores only) instead of "Could not reach the server"; a phone with a saved number + PIN or a token behaves as before.

**How it is built.** `window.__oddLock` = `''` (signed in and paid up, or no gate: everything exactly as before) / `'guest'` / `'expired'`. Each page makes a first guess from localStorage in a small inline block (so nothing locked is drawn before gate.js loads); `gate.js` owns it afterwards (`setLock`) and calls `window.oddGuestChanged(locked, mode)` on every change. gate.js: three marked blocks (switch + state; header buttons, `oddSignUp(target, mode)`, the `afterSignIn` hook, the one click listener for `[data-gt]` cards, `.glk` styles; `boot()`). index.html: block 1 (flag + `gk()` card) at the top of the main script, one-line guards in `nCard`, `gameCard`, `liveView`, `renderInner`, `newBoard`, `teamBox`, `fvPicks`, `fvHtml`, `tpPicksHtml`, `betGids`, `livePageRender`, `bpCfg`, and block 3 (its own `<script>` before the gate.js loader). sports.html: two blocks. golfgps.html: `gkGolf`. pickleball.html now loads gate.js with `window.ODD_OPEN_PAGE = true`: it never had the wall and still has none; for a phone with an account gate.js does nothing there and makes no request.

**`?ref=<code>` (sharing hook for a later build, no UI).** Letters, digits, `-`, `_`, at most 24: the first one seen on any page is kept in `odd-ref` and sent as `ref` with the sign-up request (`/acct/phone` with `create`, `/acct/register`), never with a sign-in. The server cleans it and passes it to My Apps `/api/v1/auth/register`, which ignores unknown fields today (`store.register` takes slug / secret / email / password / name / code): nothing happens until My Apps reads it.

**LIMITS (say this plainly).**
- **The lock is on the phone, the same soft paywall as before.** `/alert`, `/locks`, `/propideas`, `/games`, `/odds`, `/lines` answer anyone who asks; no server paywall was added (it could break the Calcutta pool app, see "Paywall" above). Someone who reads the code can still fetch them. Only `/ai/*`, the Golf GPS writes, `/betpush/*` and (when on) `/groups/*` check the account on the server.
- `?mirror=1` still skips gate.js and shows everything, and `classic.html` (the frozen classic build) never had the gate. Both existed before; a guest is not shown a link to either.
- The win %, leans and model record are worked out on the phone from ESPN data, so the code that works them out is in the page; a guest's page never runs it.
- The first time a paid-up account turns out to have ended, that open draws the picks for a moment before `/acct/me` answers, then takes them out of the page; later opens start locked.
- The locked card is compact and not the height of what it stands for; the tapped card is held in place instead (tested: it moves 2 px or less at 390 / 430 / 1180).
- Wording: the button says "Sign up free" (signing up costs nothing); the sign-up screen's own text ("New accounts get a 7-day free trial", "Start free trial") was NOT changed, so it reads as it will when subscriptions return. All three strings (`CTA`, `PAY`, `ENDED`) are one line at the top of the gate.js block.

**Kept open on purpose, tell the owner if he wants them locked:** ESPN's own live win-probability chart; the 📚 books sheet with "best price to shop" and the key-number note; Super Bowl odds (with the chance worked out from the price). **Locked although not strictly a pick:** 📍 Watch (it is saved to the account), arbitrage / middle alerts, wind and teaser tools.

**Version.** v268 on index.html (comment, header pill, footer, `trophy.js?v=`, `alert-frozen.json?v=`, `gate.js?v=`), sports.html (`gate.js?v=`), pga.html, golfgps.html, pickleball.html (now also `gate.js?v=`: add it to the bump list), sw.js `pics-v268`. `sync.js`, `ai.js`, `dash.js?v=219` have no version stamp, as before.

**TESTS.**
- Server: `node odds-api/acct.test.js` 44 tests (13 new: the `guest` flag in 7 spellings of `GUEST_MODE`, independent of the gate, account routes unchanged; `ref` on register / phone create, dropped when junk, never on sign-in). `node odds-api/guest.test.js` 20 tests (new: `expired()`; Groups dark = identical answers for paid / expired / signed-out and My Apps never asked; Groups on = expired refused; bet alerts not sent, data untouched, resume after payment; routes). Every other `odds-api/*.test.js` passes unchanged (alert, appcfg, betpush.sw 112, betstatus 6,909, betwatch 215, cfbd, courts, events 13, golfcourse 309, groups, groups.acct, groups.fix1, groups.owner, groups.phase1 21, groups.r2, groups.r3, osmfall 41, pairing 10, propideas, sync.phase1 8), plus `scripts/pga/pga.test.js` and `scripts/golfgps/golfgps.test.js`.
- Phone (Playwright, gate ACTIVE, every ESPN / odds-server call mocked, service workers blocked; scripts were session scratch, not in the repo). Mock values that must never reach a guest: Lock line 13.5, Helper 77%, the shared lock 91% / 88%, a prop idea for "Zzyzx Propman".
  - Guest, every page and view at 390 / 430 / 820 / 1180 / 1440: no wall, no page error, no sideways scroll, tap targets 40 px or more, the open things are there, each locked surface has its card, the page (scripts and styles removed, frames included) holds none of the pick markers and no percentage in the lists, no pick-only or account request went out, no pick or bet store was written.
  - Tap a locked card, Back, tap again, sign up (mocked): unlocked without a reload, the tapped game at the same screen position, the pick data requested only then, `afterSignIn` called once. Add bet, header Add bet, Ask AI, Results, My Bets, Sign in, sports.html, PGA / Golf GPS / Pickleball sheets. Sign out lands in guest browsing.
  - A signed-in user, v266 against v268 at 390 / 430 / 1180 (index 13 views, NBA 4 views, PGA, Golf GPS, Pickleball, NCAA, owner): same text, same requests with the same counts, screenshots identical (most byte for byte, the rest within 3/255 on a few dozen pixels of gradient). Counts on the last full runs: guest views 1,031 checks, sign-up / sign-out / status / ref 158, ended account 378, v266-against-v268 447 (108 screenshot pairs: 104 byte for byte, 4 within 3/255 on at most 22 pixels). KNOWN TEST NOISE, not a product difference: in the comparison suite 1 to 4 of the 447 checks fail on any one run and a different one each time (one upload to `/locks` landing just inside or outside the capture window, a trailing space in the wall text, in both directions); read with a longer equal window, v266 and v268 made the same 59 requests three times out of three.
  - `GUEST_MODE=0`: the wall of v266 for a visitor and for an ended account (text, screenshot, requests; on the very first open after the switch flips a few requests repeat, afterwards the counts are equal).
  - Status call failing with and without a saved account; an older server without the field; gate off; a saved PIN that no longer works.

Free time granted while closed, paid while closed, server asleep, no end date = never locked, the other pages, sign out.

## v270 — Nov 17 2026: merge of guest mode (v268) onto v269

Coder agent (merge only), worktree branch `merge-work` from origin/main ef930d6 (v269), `git merge --no-ff guest-mode` (fe24d48, built on v266). NOT pushed by the coder. Read "v267", "v269" and "v268" above first: this entry only says what was decided where the two met. No new feature.

**Hand-merged files.** `pics/gate.js` (4 hunks + 5 small edits), `pics/index.html` (version lines; the "v268 GUEST MODE (3 of 3)" block is kept in front of the loader line, and the loader line loads gate.js, ai.js, sync.js, announce.js, dash.js, the three stamped ones on `?v=270`), `pics/pickleball.html` (v269 lines + the guest `gate.js` tag), `pics/announce.js` (one line), version lines in `golfgps.html`, `pga.html`, `sports.html`, `sw.js`. HANDOFF: every section of both sides is kept (v267, v269, then v268); the bump bullet and the Groups bullet under "How it is built" stood there three times (one copy per branch) and are now one complete copy each. Everything else (`odds-api/acct.js`, `betwatch.js`, `groups.js`, `guest.test.js`, `acct.test.js`, `pics/sync.js`, `pics/sports.html` body) merged by git without a conflict.

**The six points.**
1. **ONE pay screen, one Back.** The subscribe screen in `draw()` is theirs (`sheet ? BACK : ''`, `wireBack()`), with v267's wording when `early` is set ("N days left." / "N free days left." / "Ends today."). v267's own `#gback` button, its inline wiring and `payBack`-as-a-handler are gone: `payBack()` is now called from `closeSheet()` (the Back button, Esc), so an account that still has time goes back to the app WITHOUT asking the server, as in v267; the "fake not-allowed" re-check of v268 stays for the fallback only. `payOpen()` sets `sheet` itself (always for an account that still has time; for an ended one only in guest mode). **`window.oddPay` is defined once** (block 2): signed in and not ended -> `payOpen()` (early wording); ended in guest mode, or nobody signed in -> `oddSignUp('', 'pay')` (the pay sheet / the sign-up sheet). Entry points and what they open: header "Pay now" and every locked card of an ended account -> pay sheet, ended wording; days-left card "Pay now" -> `oddPay()` -> early wording; the "ends soon" toast -> `payOpen()` (v268's own line is the fallback); `/?pay=1` -> `setMe()` calls `oddPay()` for an account with an end date that is still open (early wording) and, in guest mode, for an ended one (pay sheet; in v269 the wall was already up, in guest mode nothing is). `/?pay=1` on a phone with NO account in guest mode opens the sign-in sheet with a Back (v269: the sign-in wall), then the pay screen after sign-in.
2. **Message card and days-left card under guest rules.** A guest sees the owner's message card (it rides `/appcfg`, which a guest's page already reads once) and never the days-left card (`oddMe()` is null without a token). An ENDED account: the days-left card is HIDDEN (the simpler of the two choices; nothing had to be written for it: `days()` in announce.js only ever drew for `allowed` accounts) and the Pay now locks + header say it once. `/acct/announce` is only called with a token. One line changed in `announce.js` `targeted()`: the "asked" mark is set only when a token exists, so a guest who signs up or in without a reload is asked ONCE then (index.html runs `bcLoad` again after a sign-in); signed-in phones and `GUEST_MODE=0` are unaffected. An ended account still gets its targeted message ("expired only" is one of the audiences).
3. **Bet alerts (v266).** Unchanged from v268 and verified against v267's `bcLoad`: `bcLoad` -> `oddAnn(j)` then `bpCfg(j)`; `bpCfg` returns before any call when there is no token or `window.__oddLock` is set, `#bpinv` / `#bpset` are hidden by `html.gst`. Guest and ended account: 0 `/betpush/*` requests, no invitation. LIMIT (v268's, unchanged): on the first open on which a paid-up account turns out to have ended, a `/betpush/status` read can go out before `/acct/me` answers; later opens start locked.
4. **Owner Dashboard / Send to everyone**: `dash.js` is byte-identical to v269 (only its `?v=`); reached only from the signed-in owner's Account box / nav.
5. **Signed in, paid or free for now**: `lock` is `''`, none of the guest code runs. Compared against a copy of v269 (below).
6. **`GUEST_MODE=0`**: the v269 screens. The early pay screen there uses `BACK0` in gate.js = v269's "← Back to Oddable" button letter for letter (guest mode shows "‹ Back"). Wall for visitors and ended accounts, no Back on a wall.

**Version.** v270 on the whole bump list (now incl. `gate.js?v=` in pickleball.html, `announce.js?v=` and `dash.js?v=`): index.html (comment, header pill, footer "Oddable v270 (browse free · sign up for picks, bets and groups)", `trophy.js`, `alert-frozen.json`, `gate.js`, `announce.js`, `dash.js`), sports.html, pga.html, golfgps.html, pickleball.html, sw.js `pics-v270`.

## v271 — Nov 17 2026: email + password on every account, "Lost my PIN" / "Lost my password", Share Oddable with the share / influencer code

Coder agent on `main` (from 75710d4, v270). Committed locally, NOT pushed. Oddable side of My Apps builds .38-.41 ("API contract 5" and "API contract 6" in the My Apps HANDOFF: read them first). **FREE FOR NOW IS UNCHANGED**: no price, trial, expiry or guest-mode rule was touched.

**Owner (Nov 16-17 2026).** A. "All sign ups have an email login and a password and a PIN option, with lost my PIN or password option." B. Influencer codes: "track a user that gives a new user a code (influencer) to open the app… if I as an influencer give my code and the new user opens and he shares the app, the same influencer gets credit."

**WHAT THE OWNER MUST DO.** 1. In My Apps (Render > my-apps-management > Environment) set `SMTP_USER` and `SMTP_PASS` (steps in the My Apps HANDOFF, build .38), then System > Email > "Send a test email to me". **Do this BEFORE or right after this build goes live**: until then every email step answers "Email isn't switched on yet." (nothing breaks, see `nomail` below). 2. Nothing to set on the Oddable server (no new env var). 3. After both are live: make a test account on a phone, type the emailed code, sign out, sign in with the email + password on another device and check the bets are there; then "Lost my PIN".

### Facts found first (how it worked up to v270)
- **Sign-up / sign-in.** `pics/gate.js` had one screen: cell number + PIN first (with name / handle on create), "Use email + password instead" as the second path. `POST /acct/phone` (`odds-api/acct.js`): `sync.verifyLogin` checks (or creates) the row in `oddable_sync`, then the account is registered / signed in at My Apps as `<20 hex>@phone.oddable` with a made-up password. `POST /acct/register` / `/acct/login`: a plain My Apps email account. Auto sign-in = the phone keeps `sync-phone` + `sync-pin` and calls `/acct/phone` again when it has no token.
- **The token** (`x-token`, 30 days) carries only the account id `e` and the expiry `x`, signed with `GATE_KEY`. No issue time, no PIN, no row id.
- **The bets row.** Table `oddable_sync (id, salt, pinhash, data, updated)`. `id = sha256('oddable|' + cell number)`: THE PHONE NUMBER ALONE. The PIN is only a check: `pinhash = scrypt(pin, salt)`. `data` = bets, log, pins, locks. `/sync/pull|push|login` took `{phone, pin}` in the body and checked the PIN on every call; the token was not used.
- **The My Apps account of a phone user** = `phoneEmail(id)` = HMAC(`GATE_KEY`, 'phoneid|' + row id), its made-up password = HMAC(…, 'phone|' + row id). **Neither depends on the PIN.** The owner dashboard, My Apps' player feed, Groups, bet alerts and events all find a player from the row id.
- **Email-only accounts** (made with `/acct/register`) have NO row: their bets were never synced (this phone only), and they have no PIN.
- **`link`** (My Apps): an email + password that opens the SAME phone account; `login` then answers `account` = the phone account id, `via:"link"`. v270 minted the token from the TYPED email, which would have opened an empty account for a linked user.
- **`oddWipe` / `oddOwner`**: the phone clears one person's data when a different `sync-owner` signs in.
- **Share links**: the app builds NONE today. Pickleball "Share event" hands over a text code (no link), Golf GPS has no course link, there is no game share. So there was nothing to add `?ref=` to; the helper below is ready for the first ones.

### The identity / bets design (and why)
1. **A new account is still a cell number + PIN account, and the email + password is ATTACHED to it** (My Apps `link`, two steps). **Cell number + PIN stay REQUIRED at sign-up** (owner said "PIN option"; this is the coder's decision, owner to confirm). Why: the bets can only be stored in the row keyed by the cell number, the My Apps account id is made from that row id, and the dashboard / My Apps feed / Groups / bet alerts / events find a player through it. An account with no row would have nowhere to keep bets and would not show up as a player. The sign-up screen says it in one line: "Your cell number and PIN are needed: your bets are saved under them, and they sign you in fast on this phone. Your email and password get you back in if you lose the PIN."
2. **`/acct/login` uses `account` from My Apps' answer** (contract 5i), accepted only when it is the typed email or a phone account id. So an email + password sign-in of a linked account gets the SAME token identity as the cell number + PIN.
3. **A device signed in by email gets the bets WITHOUT typing the PIN: `/sync/pull|login|push` also accept `{token}` in the body** (no new header; the `/sync` CORS list is unchanged). `sync.js tokenRow()`: the token must be one `acct.js` signed (`tokenAcct`: gate ON, good signature, not expired, not the owner-PIN token, not signed before the account's last reset), the account must be a phone account, and its row is found from the account id (`rowIdFor`: `/acct/phone` notes the pair on every sign-in; otherwise one read of the row ids, at most once in 30 s for a miss). Why this does not weaken the rows: a token is only ever issued after the PIN was checked, after My Apps accepted an email + password whose address was PROVEN by an emailed code (build .40), or after a recovery code; a token opens only its own account's row; with the gate off (signing key guessable) no token is accepted at all; a body that carries a phone + PIN is still the PIN path (a token never rescues a wrong PIN); `/sync/signup` is not open to tokens. The PIN path itself is byte for byte what it was (the push loop moved into `pushRow()` unchanged).
4. **A PIN change keeps the bets because the PIN was never part of the key.** `sync.setPin(id, pin)` is ONE write on the row that sets only `salt` and `pinhash` (new random salt). The row id, `data` and `updated` are not in the write, so a push landing at the same moment cannot be lost, nothing is re-keyed, and the My Apps account and its made-up password are the same as before. The old PIN stops working at once (its hash no longer matches).
5. **Every device is signed out after a reset.** `acct.revoke(account)` stamps "now"; `verify()` refuses that account's tokens issued before the stamp (issue time = `x` − 30 days). The stamps live in the row `lock:pinrev` of `oddable_sync` (the `lock:` prefix keeps it out of every player list; tested) and are re-read at most once a minute, so a restart does not forget them. `/acct/me` answers such a token with `401 {code:"resign"}`; the phone then asks to sign in again and **clears nothing** (`reauth()` in gate.js; an ordinary 401 still signs out and wipes as before). A phone that still has a working PIN saved (only the password was reset) signs itself back in without a word.
6. **Same person, two ways in:** `oddOwner(id, acct)` now also takes the account id, so signing in by email on a phone that was signed in by cell number (same account) does not wipe the phone.

### Server
- **New `odds-api/recover.js`** (test `recover.test.js`), wired in `server.js` one line above the `/acct` line. No new request header: the CORS Allow-Headers list is still `content-type,x-token,x-dash`. It logs nothing. Every answer is rebuilt; My Apps' own text, an account id, a code or a ticket never leave (except the password ticket, which the phone needs for one step).
  - `POST /acct/link {email, password}` (signed in; the account is the token's; phone accounts only) -> My Apps `/api/v1/link` -> `{ok, pending:true, hint}` (a code was mailed) or `{ok, pending:false, verified:true, hint}`. `POST /acct/link/verify {code}` -> `{ok, verified:true, hint}`.
  - `POST /acct/recover/start {email, kind: password|pin}` -> **always `{ok:true}`** for a well-formed email (My Apps answers before it looks the account up; our slow-down counts an email with no account the same way). `POST /acct/recover/check {email, code}` (password only) -> `{ok, ticket}`. `POST /acct/recover/password {ticket, password}` -> `{ok}` + every device of that account signed out.
  - `POST /acct/recover/pin {email, code, pin}`: ONE request. Shapes checked first (a bad PIN never spends the code) -> My Apps `recover/check` (kind pin) -> `recover/redeem` HERE, once, right before the write, and it must name the same account -> `sync.setPin` -> `revoke` -> `{ok, token, me}`. The phone never holds the PIN ticket and never names the account. Anything but a clean yes = "start again", nothing written.
  - `GET|POST /acct/ref` -> My Apps `ref/mine` -> `{ok, ref}`, kept 1 hour per account (a failure is `{ok:false, ref:""}` and is not kept). `POST /acct/ref/attach {ref}` -> `{ok, attributed}`; `set` / `late` / `self` / unknown code are a quiet 200.
  - Error codes the phone gets: `nomail` (**200** `{ok:false, code:"nomail"}`), `limit` / `slow` 429, `taken` 409, `has_login` 409, `email` / `password` / `code` / `pin` / `ticket` 400, `expired` 410, `locked` 429, `mail` 502, `nopin` 409 (an email-only account has no PIN), `norow` 404, `failed` 502 (anything else).
  - Limits (per 10 minutes, in memory): link 8 per account and per address; link/verify 20 per account; recover/start 8 per address, 5 per email; recover/check 20 per address, 12 per email; recover/password 12 per address; recover/pin 12 per address and per email; ref 30 per account; ref/attach 8 per account and per address. My Apps has its own limits behind these. Body limit 4000 bytes.
- **`odds-api/acct.js`**: `/acct/login` (point 2); `/acct/me` (`shape()`) gains at the END `has_contact`, `contact_verified`, `contact_hint`, `has_email_login` (true / false / **null = an older My Apps did not say**), `influencer`, `ref_locked` (true when not said); `verify()` + `revoke` / `revLoad` / `tokenAcct`; `/acct/phone` notes the row; a revoked token gets `code:"resign"`. `ref` already went to `register` on both sign-up paths (v268); tested again.
- **`odds-api/sync.js`**: the token path, `pushRow`, `rowIdFor`, `noteRow`, `setPin`, `ready`.

### Phone (`pics/gate.js`, `pics/sync.js`; index.html = version lines + one condition)
- **Sign-up sheet**: Name or handle, Email, Password (8+, Show / Hide), Cell number, PIN (6 to 10 digits, masked, not a password box so a password manager saves the email + password), "Have a code? (optional)" prefilled from `?ref=`. What is in the box wins over the link code only if the person changed it. On submit: `/acct/phone` (create, as before) then `/acct/link`, then **"Check your email": the 6-digit code**, with Resend (30 s apart; the server limits too), "Use a different email" and "Skip for now". Then the success screen ("You're in", Share Oddable, Continue). The app is already open underneath.
- **`nomail`** (mail not switched on in My Apps): the account is made, the email is kept as pending on the phone (`odd-eml`; never the password), and the screen says "Email isn't switched on yet. Your account works with your cell number and PIN. We'll ask you to confirm your email later." Recovery screens say "Email isn't switched on yet. Ask Sir Oddable to reset it for you." Nothing ever stops at a mail step: a refused address (`taken`), an unreachable server or a mail failure all leave the account working, with the reason shown and "Not now".
- **Sign-in sheet**: both ways on one sheet, each in its own box with its own button: "Cell number and PIN" + "Lost my PIN", "Email and password" + "Lost my password". The way used last on the device comes first (`odd-way`; a new phone shows cell number first, since every account has one and almost none has an email yet).
- **Lost my password**: email -> "If that email has an account, a code is on its way." -> code + new password -> signed in. **Lost my PIN**: email -> code + new PIN (one screen) -> signed in, "Your PIN is changed. Your bets are just as you left them…". That phone then syncs with its token (`odd-stok`); the cell number + new PIN work everywhere else.
- **Existing cell number + PIN users**: a card in normal page flow at the top of the list on index.html, under the days-left card and the owner's message: "✉️ Add an email and password so you can recover your account." [Add email and password] [Not now]. "Not now" hides it 14 days; it comes back at most 3 times. Shown only when My Apps says `has_email_login:false` (an older My Apps says nothing: no card). It asks the server nothing. Also any time in the Account box under ⓘ Disclosures: "Email: s•••@gmail.com ✓" or the "Add email and password" / "Confirm your email" button.
- **Share Oddable**: a button in the Account box and on the sign-up success screen. Web Share where the phone has it, else the link is copied ("Link copied"). An influencer's Account box also shows "Your influencer code: MIKE" + Copy link. No commission numbers in Oddable (they are on the influencer's page in My Apps).
- **`window.oddShareUrl(path)` — FOR THE GROUPS PHASE 2 CODER AND ANYONE WHO BUILDS A LINK THAT LEAVES THE APP.** `oddShareUrl('')` -> `https://<site>/?ref=K7QP2M9X`; `oddShareUrl('golfgps.html?c=123')` -> `…/golfgps.html?c=123&ref=K7QP2M9X`. It adds the signed-in person's code (their influencer's code, their own influencer code, or their personal share code: My Apps decides), answers at once from what the phone knows (kept 1 hour) and gives a guest the plain link. `await window.oddShare(path, {title, text})` refreshes the code, then opens the share sheet or copies; it resolves to `shared` / `copied` / `cancel` / `fail`. Group, Calcutta and golf invites must be built with it so they carry the code.
- **A code for an account that already exists**: when a code is remembered (`?ref=` or typed) and My Apps says the box is open (`ref_locked:false`), `/acct/ref/attach` is called ONCE after sign-in; every real answer is silent and final, an unreachable server is tried once more on the next open.
- `pics/sync.js`: token sync (`odd-stok`), "sign in again" on a changed PIN, and `OddSync.kick()`: the two background jobs (push every 10 s when something changed; pull when the tab comes back) now also start after a sign-in that did not reload the page. Before, somebody who signed up without a reload was only synced again on the next app open.
- **What a signed-in user with nothing to do gets that v270 did not**: in the Account box (under ⓘ Disclosures, closed by default) the "Share Oddable" button and the Email line; ONE `GET /acct/ref` when Disclosures is opened (at most once an hour; an influencer's phone that does not know its code yet also asks once on open); one `/acct/ref/attach` ever, only on a phone that remembers a code. Nothing else: same requests, same text, same picture (tested, below). A guest: exactly v270.

### Known gaps (say these plainly)
- **While mail is off nobody can reset a PIN** (the owner can reset an email login's password from My Apps, but a PIN only changes through an emailed code). And the reminder card still shows to existing users; its first tap answers "Email isn't switched on yet" and it then waits 14 days. Set the SMTP variables first.
- An **email-only account** (old `/acct/register` sign-ups) still has no synced bets and no PIN; "Lost my PIN" tells it so. No screen makes such accounts any more (the route is still there).
- A new PIN must be **6 to 10 digits** (the rule new accounts already had); the brief said 4. Kept, because a 4-digit PIN would weaken the row.
- The sign-out stamps are read once a minute and fail open if the table cannot be read right after a restart. The row lookup reads up to 5,000 row ids (the same limit Groups and bet alerts live with).
- No "remove my email" (My Apps has no unlink). The Account line still shows the made-up `…@phone.oddable` id. The sign-up wording "7-day free trial" / "Start free trial" is untouched (owner has not confirmed new wording).

### Version
v271 on the whole bump list: index.html (comment, header pill, footer "Oddable v271 (email sign-in · lost PIN or password · share Oddable)", `trophy.js`, `alert-frozen.json`, `gate.js`, `announce.js`, `dash.js`), sports.html `gate.js`, pga.html, golfgps.html, pickleball.html (incl. its `gate.js` tag), sw.js `pics-v271`. `sync.js` / `ai.js` have no stamp, as before (the service worker goes to the network first).

### Tests
- `node odds-api/recover.test.js`: **47 tests** (fake My Apps + a fake `oddable_sync` table behind `fetch`; the real `acct.js`, `sync.js`, `recover.js` routes): link two steps, every error code, nothing leaked, account from the token only, limits; `account` from the login answer; token sync and everything it refuses; `/acct/ref` cache, `ref/attach`; no enumeration (same answer, same single My Apps call, same slow-down for an email with and without an account); lost password; **lost PIN: bets present -> PIN changed -> old PIN refused (sign-in, pull, push), new PIN works, same row id, same data, same `updated`, ONE write of `salt` + `pinhash`, same My Apps account, every older token answers "resign", also after a restart**; refusals that write nothing (bad shapes, wrong code, redeem refused / other account / wrong kind / unreachable, email-only account, database down); gate off; nothing logged. 28 mutations tried: 26 failed a test; the other two were an edit that changed nothing and "say yes when the database matched no row" (not covered: the row is looked up first).
- Every `odds-api/*.test.js` passes (24 files): acct 44, alert, announce 36, appcfg, betpush.sw 112, betstatus 6,909, betwatch 215, cfbd, courts, events 13, golfcourse 309, groups, groups.acct, groups.fix1, groups.owner, groups.phase1 21, groups.r2, groups.r3, guest 20, osmfall 41, pairing 10, propideas, recover 47, sync.phase1 8.
- **Differential against v270** (33 requests, twice: a My Apps that sends the new fields and an older one): cell number + PIN sign-up and sign-in (= auto sign-in), wrong PIN, no account, `/acct/me`, email register / login, owner PIN, `/sync/push|pull|login|signup`: answers, My Apps calls with their bodies and the stored rows are IDENTICAL, apart from the six new fields at the end of `me`.
- Real server booted locally: preflight for `content-type,x-token` = 204 with the unchanged header list; the new routes answer 401 / a plain 502 with My Apps unreachable.
- Playwright (gate ACTIVE, service workers blocked, every outside call mocked, 390x844 / 430x932 / 820x1180 / 1180x820; scripts and screenshots in the session scratchpad `v271/`, not in the repo; counts are in the commit report): sign-up with mail on / off / skipped / address taken / number already used / link unreachable / another person's data on the phone (wipe + reload, the code step comes back, the password is asked again); code entry, wrong code, resend; sign-in both ways, last way first, errors by the field; lost password and lost PIN incl. mail off and Back; the card (order under the two other cards, Not now, 14 days, 3 returns, flow, mail off, banner never over it); Account box; Share with Web Share mocked and with the clipboard; influencer line; `?ref=` prefill and attach; signed out by a reset (sheet, nothing cleared; silent re-sign-in when the PIN is still right); the wall with `GUEST_MODE=0`; the other four pages. Against a copy of v270: a guest, and signed-in accounts with nothing to do (linked, and on an older My Apps) = same text, same requests, screenshots byte for byte at all four widths. v270's own guest suites still pass on v271 (guest on every view 1,031; ended account 378).
- **NOT verified**: the live My Apps .41 endpoints and real mail (the written contract was faked: no end-to-end call); the live `oddable_sync` table (the PIN write is one PostgREST PATCH of two columns, same form as the writes already in use); a real phone: iOS code autofill, password managers, the iOS share sheet, Safari's `-webkit-text-security`; the cross-origin calls from the real page (header list unchanged, but this file's rule says try a real sign-up, a real "Lost my PIN" and Share in Chrome after the deploy); `SELECT count(*) FROM ma_logins` on the live My Apps should be 0 before this ships (see My Apps .40).

### Decisions (coder; manager / owner to confirm)
1. 2026-11-17 — Cell number + PIN required at sign-up, email + password added and required. Why: point 1 of the design. The owner said "PIN option".
2. 2026-11-17 — New PINs stay 6 to 10 digits. Why: not to weaken the rows.
3. 2026-11-17 — Email sign-in syncs the bets with the token, no PIN typed. Why: point 3.
4. 2026-11-17 — Lost PIN is one screen (code + new PIN) and does not ask for the cell number. Why: the contract's own hand-off; the ticket never reaches the phone.
5. 2026-11-17 — A password reset also signs the other devices out (those with a saved PIN come straight back). Why: that is what a reset is for.
6. 2026-11-17 — A skip or mail-off right at sign-up makes the reminder card wait (1 day / 14 days) without counting as a "Not now".
7. 2026-11-17 — The reminder card is on index.html only. sports / pga / golfgps / pickleball have the sheets but not the card.
8. 2026-11-17 — Suggested to the My Apps coder: a `mail` entry in `caps` of `/api/v1/app/settings`, so the card can stay away while mail is off.

### Roll back
Redeploy v270 (75710d4). The row `lock:pinrev` can stay (v270 never reads it). PINs that were changed stay changed. ONE THING TO KNOW: v270 signs a LINKED email in under the typed email, which is not an account, so people who added an email must use their cell number + PIN until v271 is back. Mail and codes can be stopped without a roll back by removing `SMTP_USER` / `SMTP_PASS` in My Apps (everything then answers `nomail`).

## v272 — Nov 17 2026: Groups phase 2 = Groups on the phone, OWNER-ONLY first (five-tab bar, my groups, create, invite, join, members, contest, leave; group bets)

Coder agent on `main` (from 9eba403 = v271 + the flat-Leaflet deploy fix). Committed locally, NOT pushed. **Groups stays DARK until the manager sets the variables below; with them unset v272 is v271 for every user** (compared screen by screen, see Tests). **FREE FOR NOW IS UNCHANGED**: no paywall, price, trial or expiry was added or changed. No money moves, no order is placed, no message is sent for anyone.

### The switches (Render, odds server: the service NAMED "ODDABLE", URL calcutta-odds). Exact names, nothing new:
- `GROUPS_ENABLED` = `1` turns Groups on (unset = off for everybody, the owner too).
- `GROUPS_OWNER_ONLY` = `1` = only the owner-PIN sign-in, the `OWNER_PHONE` accounts and the testers.
- `GROUPS_TESTERS` = account keys, comma-separated (24 hex each; how to find one: "Groups Phase 1" above, `POST /groups/access`).
- For the owner-only test set the first two (and the third if there are testers). To open Groups to everyone later: delete `GROUPS_OWNER_ONLY`.

### How the phone knows (no new call, no new route, no new request header)
- `odds-api/acct.js` `gFlag(me, account)`: while `GROUPS_ENABLED=1`, the `me` object the phone ALREADY gets (every sign-in answer, `/acct/me`, the redeem answer, the lost-PIN sign-in in `recover.js`) carries `groups: true` for an account the switches let in (`groups.js` `onFor()` = the same `allowed()` check as every `/groups` call). Everyone else, and everyone while the variable is unset, gets exactly v271's `me` (no field). `/acct/status` carries `groups: true` only while `GROUPS_ENABLED=1` (it means "Groups exists", nothing about any account; needed so a signed-out visitor's invite link can be read).
- The flag does NOT look at whether the account's time has ended: an ended account that is on the list keeps the flag, so the phone can show the Groups tab with the "Your time has ended" lock; the server still refuses every `/groups` call for it (v268 `lapsed()`, 403 `expired:true`).
- `pics/gate.js`: `setMe()` keeps the flag in localStorage `odd-grp` (`'1'` or absent) and calls `window.oddGroupsChanged()`; `status()` keeps `/acct/status` `groups` in `odd-gsw` and calls `window.oddGroupsSw()`. Sign-out and `oddWipe` clear `odd-grp` and `odd-gbl`. Turning the variable off on Render removes the tab on the phone's next account check (no deploy).
- `pics/index.html` `grpOn()` = `odd-grp === '1'` and signed in and not `?mirror=1`. False = no tab, no More sheet, `pics/groups.js` is never requested, no `/groups` call. True = `grpLoad()` fetches `groups.js?v=272` (1.5 s after open for a known Groups account, so the bet step is there before the first bet).

### The five-tab bar (`navRender5`, `navMore` in index.html)
- For an account Groups is on for: **Games, Live, My Bets, Groups, More**. The More sheet holds Survivor, Winning, Losing and (owner) Dashboard; More is lit while one of them shows; a tap outside or Close shuts it. On 900 px and wider it is the same five in the left rail.
- **DECISION (the plan was ambiguous: it says the bar "comes in with Groups' phone phase, owner-only first"):** everyone Groups is NOT on for keeps the v271 bar exactly (Games, Live, My Bets, Survivor, Winning, Losing, + Dashboard for the owner). Chosen because it changes nothing for non-Groups users. When Groups opens to everyone (delete `GROUPS_OWNER_ONLY`) every signed-in account gets the five-tab bar; guests keep the old bar (a guest has no Groups flag). Say so if guests should get the five-tab bar too.
- The preview's More sheet listed Calcutta / Golf / Start a game / My events / My stats: none of that is here (those engines are not on main).

### Screens (`pics/groups.js`, a NEW file of about 300 lines, not a port of the preview's 104 KB `groups.js`)
- **Why new:** the reviewed preview file (1e47e93) carries contests with entries and payouts, side pots, Kalshi-tagged contests, partner pairs, enterprise contact and pricing text, and hooks into preview-only code. The brief for this phase is a short list of screens with no money, so a small file that does only that was safer to check than cutting 100 KB down. The server (phase 1) is unchanged apart from the flag. The preview file stays the reference for later phases.
- Groups is an overlay `#ogrp` beside the tab bar, never over it: above the bar on a phone, right of the rail from 900 px (`fit()` measures `#bn`). z-index 45: it covers the page header and the floating My Bets banner (z 40), so the banner cannot sit on a Groups button; sign-up / pay sheets (200+) and the confirm step open above it. Another tab, or Close, shuts it. Content box max 1328 px like the Games view; cards flow in columns (`.oggrid`, `auto-fill minmax(300px,1fr)`), the create form is a grid, the contest screen is standings + rules side by side from 900 px. All classes start `og…` (the first names collided with index.html's `.gpl`, `.gst`, `.gh`, `.gtop`).
- **My groups** (`mine`): a card per group (name, Starts / Running / Final, members, sport, "N join requests waiting" for the commissioner), Create a group, Join with a code, "Waiting for an answer" with Cancel my request.
- **Group page**: three tiles that open the detail behind them, each with ‹ Back: Members (n of max, "N asking to join"), Contest / Standings, Rules. Invite. Delete this group (commissioner) or Leave this group (member), both with a confirm, both only before the start (server rule); after the start: "Members stay to the end".
- **Members**: names with Commissioner / You; the commissioner gets Approve / Decline on each request (`decide`).
- **Contest**: before the start a note + the rules; after it the standings as rows (place and name left, the ranking number right, record under the name, header over its columns; no table, nothing scrolls sideways) (`standings`). **Rules**: sport, dates, ranked by, bets that count, most bets a week, "Entry: None. Bragging rights only."
- **Create**: group name, sport, first day that counts, runs for, ranked by (Units / Win % / Return %), most members (2 to 8), bets that count, "Anyone with my invite link joins without my approval". Sent with entry 0, split `none`, take `none`, `open: false`, max bets a week 10, parlay legs 4. **DECISION: no entry, payout, take, side pot, Kalshi-only, honor or "Winnings ($)" choice on the phone in this phase** (the server still supports them; H1 / H2 / H4 are money questions still waiting for the owner). More than 8 people is not offered, so the enterprise price sentence the server holds is never shown.
- **Invite**: `window.oddShare('?join=g.<code>')` / `window.oddShareUrl(...)` from v271, so the link is `https://<site>/?join=g.ABCDEFGH&ref=<share code>`. The phone's own share sheet, else the link is copied; the link and the code are also shown on the card. The commissioner shares the commissioner's code (joins straight away only with "no approval" on); a member shares the members' link (always a request). Nothing is sent by Oddable.
- **Join**: type the code (ABCD-EFGH; checked on the phone before any call) or open an invite link -> preview (name, who runs it, members, rules) -> "Ask to join" / "Join this group" -> "Request sent" or "You are in" -> Open the group.
- **What other members can see:** a name or handle and a short member id (`pid`). The server never sends a phone number, account id, email, PIN, sync row id or member key (checked in `groups.phone.test.js` 8). Group ids are 10 random characters, codes 8.

### Invite links and sign-in
- `?join=g.<code>` is read ONLY when `/acct/status` says Groups exists; it is kept for the tab (sessionStorage `odd-join`) and taken out of the address bar (other parameters such as `ref` stay). With Groups dark the link is left alone and opens the app like any link.
- **Guest**: `oddSignUp('group:g:<code>')` opens the existing sign-up sheet with ‹ Back and one note ("You were invited to a group on Oddable…"). After sign-up or sign-in gate.js calls `afterSignIn('group:g:<code>')` (index.html, `k === 'group'`) and the join preview opens, without a reload. gate.js reports a sign-in twice (the account, then the hook); `GRPLAST` makes the invite run once.
- **Signed in, Groups on**: straight to the join preview.
- **Signed in, Groups NOT on for this account** (the owner-only test): one sheet, "Groups is being tested… not open for this account yet", with **Ask to be let in** (`POST /groups/access/request`, only on the tap; it lands in the owner's list from phase 1) and Close. No tab, no file.
- **Ended account** (time expired, not paid) that Groups is on for: the Groups tab shows only the SAME lock card as everywhere else ("Your time has ended. Pay now to unlock your picks, bets and groups." + Pay now + Request free time, gate.js `.glk` / `data-gt`); no Groups data is asked for. With an invite link the lock keeps the invite as its target (`group:g:<code>`), so after paying the person lands on the group. If the server finds the account ended first, its own 403 sentence is shown.

### Group bets and the review blockers
- `window.OddGroups.gate(bet)` (groups.js) is called for a NEW bet through `gHold()` in index.html (`betSave`, `parlaySave`, `propSave`, the single-slip import) and in sports.html `betSave`. It runs only while this phone last knew the player is in a group contest (`OddGroups.active()`, localStorage `odd-gbl` = `{n, at, tok}`), refreshed quietly on open, on coming back to the app, when the Add menu / a game's Add bet opens (at most once a minute), and after create / join / leave / My groups.
- **B1 FIXED.** A bet is refused ("Couldn't reach your group, so the bet was not added") ONLY when the phone last knew the player was in a group and the fresh status call fails. Never knew, or knew "not in a group": the bet is kept as a personal bet at once, nothing is asked and nothing waits. The status wait is 6 s (was 20 s). The owner-only switch answers bet/status 200 "not in a group" (phase 1).
- **B2 FIXED.** The confirm step is "Add this bet to your group? Bets lock when you add them." with **Add to my group · lock it** and **Cancel**: no "Just for me". A bet no contest can count comes back `personal: true` and is KEPT as a personal bet with the server's sentence (never "Not saved"). 503 (ESPN could not confirm the game) and 409 (already started…) keep the form open with the server's reason; nothing is kept.
- A group bet (`b.gl`) shows "🔒 Group bet · locked when added" instead of Edit / Delete; `betEdit` / `betDel` refuse it. Group bets need a stake. "Save all ready" (several screenshots) is stopped for a group member with a sentence (each bet needs its own step).
- `classic.html` is NOT touched: it does nothing but redirect to index.html (line 3), so there is no Add bet to hide there.
- NOT built in this phase: adding a Kalshi-imported bet to a group from its card, a member's bet list (drill-down from a standings row), marking entries paid, contest edit / add / delete, side pots, open-groups list, commissioner void, weekly view, partner pairs.

### Other small edits
- `pics/sync.js` and three places in index.html: no automatic reload while Groups is open (`#odash,#ogrp`); the score pop-up waits while Groups or the More sheet is open.
- `odds-api/groups.testenv.js`: the 15 checks in the ported suites that describe the PREVIEW phone file no longer switch on just because `pics/groups.js` exists (they would test the wrong file); they run only with `ODD_PREVIEW_ROOT=<checkout of 1e47e93>` and are otherwise skipped and counted, as before.
- Version v272 on the whole bump list: index.html (comment, header pill, footer, `trophy.js`, `alert-frozen.json`, `gate.js`, `announce.js`, `dash.js`, `groups.js`), sports.html (`gate.js`, `groups.js`), pga.html, golfgps.html (incl. `leaflet.css` / `leaflet.js`), pickleball.html (incl. its `gate.js` tag), sw.js `pics-v272`. `pics/groups.js` is a flat file (DEPLOY RULE); the build command printed exit 0.

New `node odds-api/groups.phone.test.js` (10 checks, real acct.js / sync.js / limits.js / groups.js over a fake database and My Apps): dark = `/acct/status` and every `me` are v271's for all five kinds of account and for six near-miss values of `GROUPS_ENABLED`; owner-only = flag for the owner PIN and OWNER_PHONE only; a tester key adds exactly that tester, a phone number or email in the list is ignored; the flag agrees with `/groups/on`; an ended account on the list keeps the flag and gets 403 `expired` on nine routes; the phone's create payload (entry 0, no payout, more than 8 refused); members' link -> request -> approve -> standings -> leave; nothing private in a member's view; delete; open; off again.

Not driven end to end in the browser: the sports.html Save with a group member (only that the step loads there), a parlay / prop / slip-import save through the step (same `gHold` path as the single bet), one real group through kickoff and grading (that is the owner-only test itself).

### Going live (manager)
1. Push; check BOTH deploys (ODDABLE and the static `steves-pics`). With nothing set, nothing changes for anyone.
2. Set `GROUPS_ENABLED` = `1` and `GROUPS_OWNER_ONLY` = `1` on ODDABLE (optionally `GROUPS_TESTERS`). The owner signs in with phone + PIN (the owner-PIN sign-in can run groups but cannot place group bets). His bar becomes five tabs on the next open.
3. Reminder from phase 1: from that moment every user's synced bets get the `sa` stamp and the 5-minute tick runs.

### Roll back

2. Code: revert this commit. Nothing was migrated; `odd-grp`, `odd-gsw`, `odd-gbl`, `odd-join`, `odd-gpend` are small phone-side keys nothing else reads.

## v273 — Nov 17 2026: ⛳ Golf days as a GROUP GAME (start a day, join, "Log my score", leaderboard, Golf GPS for the course), behind the Groups switches

Coder agent on `main` (from 2572ce5 = v272). Committed locally, NOT pushed. **Golf days sit INSIDE Groups, so they are DARK with Groups: with `GROUPS_ENABLED` unset v273 is v272 for every user** (compared screen by screen, see Tests). **FREE FOR NOW IS UNCHANGED**: no paywall, price, trial or expiry was added or changed. **No money**: no entry, no payout, no pot, nothing collected, held or paid, no order placed, no message sent for anyone. Built NEW and small (manager, rule 14): `feat/golf` (2fa88eb) was not ported, so its open blockers B1 / B2 / H1-H7 do not apply here (no disputes, no confirmations, no money game, no pro pools).

### The rules used: MANAGER DEFAULTS of Nov 17 2026, THE OWNER MAY CHANGE THEM
(The owner's 11-question doc, linked under "Manager notes, Nov 16 2026 evening", still has every "Your answer" box empty. Its suggested answers 3, 6, 7, 11 apply only where relevant: 7 is rule 4 below; 6 became rule 5 "logged, no second person, because there is no money"; 3 (skins) and 11 (match play) wait for those formats. The money ones (1, 2, 4, 5, 8, 9, 10) are not used.)
1. **Formats**: stroke play gross, stroke play net, Stableford (net points: 2 for a net par, +1 per stroke under, -1 per stroke over, never below 0). Skins and match play later.
2. **Holes**: 18, front 9 or back 9.
3. **Par** comes from the course map when it has it, else the commissioner types it (par 4 is prefilled on every hole; he must tick "I checked the par of every hole"; the server refuses without it). 3 to 6. **Stroke index** typed by the commissioner; if any hole in play lacks one (or a number is used twice), net and Stableford are NOT offered, only gross, with a sentence saying why.
4. **Handicaps**: each member types his own PLAYING STROKES when joining (whole number 0 to 54). The typed number IS the playing strokes: no index or course-handicap conversion. The commissioner may change a member's number before the first score only; it is shown to everyone and logged; frozen after the first score.
5. **Score entry**: each member enters only his own. The commissioner may correct a hole a member has entered; every correction is in a log everyone can read (who, hole, old, new, when). No second person needed (no money).
6. Scores count at once. No partner confirmation.
7. **Ties** share a place ("T2", the next place is skipped). No count-back.
8. **Unfinished cards** show "Thru N" and are ranked by score to par (Stableford: points so far). The commissioner closes the day; an 18-hole day may be closed as the front 9 or the back 9 only when EVERY player has all nine of those holes, and then only those holes count.
9. **Joining** until the first score is logged. The commissioner may remove a member before the first score. Players are members of the group, so never more than the group's member limit.
10. Signed-in members of the group only. No typed-in guests.
11. One open Golf day per group at a time.
12. A member may change his own hole until the day is closed (every change is logged).
13. **Course flag colours: NOT touched.** `golfcourse.js` `D.organizer` still answers false (the owner has not confirmed); `golfday.test.js` 17 checks it.
14. Built new and small; `feat/golf` not ported.

### Coder's decisions inside those rules (manager / owner to confirm)
- **Strokes by hole**: the playing strokes are dealt by stroke index over the holes in play: every hole gets floor(strokes / holes) and the (strokes mod holes) hardest holes one more. On a 9-hole day the typed number is for those nine. A day SHORTENED to nine keeps the strokes each hole already had (nothing is re-dealt after play).
- **A Golf day "starts" when the commissioner makes it** (that is when My Apps is told). Scores open on the day of the round (Chicago date); before it "Log my score" is replaced by "Scores open on the day of the round, <date>".
- **A member may re-type his own playing strokes, or leave, before the first score** (the re-type is logged). After the first score nobody's number moves.
- **The commissioner corrects only a hole the member has entered** (he cannot enter a first score for someone, and cannot wipe one). A member may also clear his own hole (logged).
- **Cancel** = a day with no score (it disappears). **Close** = a day with at least one score. A closed day cannot be reopened or changed by anyone. The open day and the last 5 closed days are kept.
- **The map's stroke indexes are prefilled too** when the map has them and the commissioner has typed none (rule 3 says "typed by the commissioner": he sees them in the boxes, is told to check them, and can change every one).
- **The course is not re-checked against the map by the server**: it stores the id and name the commissioner picked from the search (the id must look like a map id, `way/123`).
- **5 Golf days started per account per 24 hours** (each start tells My Apps); 600 Golf day calls per account per 10 minutes.
- **The commissioner plays only if he joins** like everyone else.

### The switches: the SAME three as Groups, nothing new
`GROUPS_ENABLED` / `GROUPS_OWNER_ONLY` / `GROUPS_TESTERS` on ODDABLE (see "## v272"). `odds-api/golfday.js` asks `groups.js onFor()` on every call: off, or not on for this account = 403 `{off:true}` and NOTHING is read from the database; an account whose time has ended = 403 `{expired:true}` "Your time has ended. Pay to use Groups again." (the phone shows the same lock card as everywhere else: the Groups tab of an ended account shows only the lock, `golfday.js` is never fetched). Events: `EVENTS_ENABLED` as before.

### Server: `odds-api/golfday.js` (new) + one line in `server.js` + one field in `groups.js`
- Routes, all `POST /golfday/<op>` with JSON and `x-token` (no new request header, so the CORS line is unchanged): `list {g}`, `get {g,id}`, `create {g,name?,course:{id,name,lat?,lon?},date,format,holes,par,si|null,parOk:true}`, `join {g,id,hcp}`, `leave`, `remove {pid}`, `hcp {pid,hcp}`, `score {hole,strokes|null,pid?}`, `close {short?:'front'|'back'}`, `cancel`. `g` = the group id. Every call checks the caller is a member of that group through `groups.js memberCheck()` (commissioner = that group's commissioner).
- **`odds-api/groups.js`: ONE edit**: `memberCheck()` also returns `group` (the group's name), used for the Activity event. Nothing else in Groups, `sync.js`, `acct.js`, `events.js`, `golfcourse.js` was touched.
- **Storage**: one row per group through `sync.js` `dbGet / dbPut / dbPatchIf`, id **`lock:gd:<group id>`**, data `{days:[…]}`; read, change, write-only-if-unchanged with back-off (the `groups.js` pattern). A day holds names and the group's short member ids (`pid`): never a phone number, email, PIN or account id. A group that is deleted leaves its `lock:gd:` row behind (small; nobody can read it: `memberCheck` answers null).
- **My Apps**: after the day is written and the answer sent, `events.golfStarted({id, name}, <day name>, {account, key:'golf_start|<group id>|<day id>', detail:'Group: … . <course>. <format>. <date>'})` = the existing type `golf_start`. One event per day really made; none for a refused or cancelled one. Text is cleaned again by `events.js` (no contact data can pass).
- Exports for tests: `board, card, strokesGiven, stablefordPts, holesOf, counting, canShort, cleanDay, view, _deps`.

### Phone: `pics/golfday.js` (new, FLAT in pics/) + small edits in `pics/groups.js`
- `groups.js`: the group page has a fourth tile **Golf day**; tapping it fetches `golfday.js?v=273` (first time only) and opens the Golf day home inside the Groups overlay (same stack and ‹ Back, same one-call-at-a-time `act`, same lock card). `post()` takes a path starting with `/` for another module of the odds server. Every Golf day button is `data-a="gd…"`. Nobody without Groups ever gets `golfday.js`.
- **Golf day home**: the open day, earlier days; the commissioner gets "Start a Golf day" when none is open.
- **Start a Golf day** (commissioner): 1. Course = the SAME search as Golf GPS (`pics/golfgps.js` data layer, fetched once: `GolfGPS.search`, then `GolfGPS.loadCourse` for the pars) · name (optional), date, holes, format · 2. Par and stroke index (Hole / Par / Stroke index, header over each column; two blocks side by side on a wide screen) · the tick · Start. Net and Stableford are greyed out, with the sentence, until the stroke index is complete (it follows the typing, without a repaint).
- **The day**: tiles Leaderboard, Players, Rules, Changes (each opens its detail, ‹ Back returns) · Join (with "My playing strokes" on a net / Stableford day) · **Log my score** · **📍 Open Golf GPS for this course** = the existing page, `golfgps.html?course=<id>` · the commissioner's card (Cancel / Close / Close as the front or back 9 only, each with a confirm).
- **Log my score**: a grid of hole buttons (6 across on a phone, 9 on a tablet) and a big − / + stroke box that starts at par; Save moves to the next hole without a score; Change / Clear on a saved hole.
- **Leaderboard**: rows, not a table (place and name left, the ranking number right, "Thru N · gross · strokes" under the name); a row opens that player's card (Hole / Par / Strokes / Net or Points, four columns, never scrolls sideways; • marks a stroke given). On another player's card the commissioner gets "Correct a hole". The open day's page and its leaderboard refresh by themselves every 30 s; there is also a Refresh button.
- **Wide screens**: every Golf screen uses the Groups content box (max 1328 px like the Games view) with cards in columns; checked at 768 / 820 / 1180 / 1440. The floating My Bets banner is under the Groups overlay (v272), so it cannot cover a Golf day button.

index.html (comment, header pill, footer, `trophy.js`, `alert-frozen.json`, `groups.js`, `gate.js`, `announce.js`, `dash.js`), sports.html (`gate.js`, `groups.js`), pga.html, golfgps.html (incl. `leaflet.css` / `leaflet.js`), pickleball.html, sw.js `pics-v273`, and the two new places (`GDV` in groups.js, `golfgps.js?v=273` in golfday.js: added to the bump list at the top). The footer's words in brackets are unchanged. No folder in `pics/`: the build command printed 0.

### Tests
- Server: every `odds-api/*.test.js` passes (26 files) + `scripts/pga/pga.test.js` (14 groups) + `scripts/golfgps/golfgps.test.js` (116). Member: no Start, join (55 refused, the typed number kept), re-type, leave, score, clear, a tie shown as T2, another player's card without "Correct a hole", a day closed meanwhile; a day dated tomorrow; joining closed. Ended account: only the "Your time has ended" lock, no Golf day call or file; time ending mid-way shows the server's sentence and changes nothing. Same text (version number aside), same bottom bar, same requests with the same counts, nothing Groups / Golf day requested or kept, screenshots equal: 390 = 68 byte-identical + 2 within 3/255; 820 = 67 + 3; 1180 = 58 + 12. **423 checks a width, all pass.** Known noise as before (one `POST /locks` more or less on one side: 1 / 13 / 13 comparisons, counted, not failed; the three widths ran at the same time).
