Not driven in the browser: a commissioner who has not joined, Stableford close as the back 9, more than 3 players.

### Limits (known, by design of this small build)
- **No signal = no score saved**: a failed save shows "Could not reach the server" and keeps the number on screen, but nothing is queued on the phone (the old review's H4). Tell the owner before his first on-course test.
- **Back from Golf GPS is 4 taps** (Groups, the group, Golf day, the day) to reach "Log my score" again. Golf GPS is the existing separate page. Next step if he wants it: a "Log my score" button on the Golf GPS hole screen for a member of an open Golf day.
- A closed day ranks an unfinished card by its score so far, like a live board (rule 8 as written): a player who stopped after a few good holes can place above a finisher. See the questions.
- A player who leaves the GROUP after joining the day stays on the board and can no longer score.
- Only one tee / one set of pars per day (no men's / women's tees, no per-player tee).
- No team formats, no flights, no skins, no match play, no pro pools, no money: none of that code is on main.

### Going live (manager)
1. Push; check BOTH deploys (ODDABLE and the static `steves-pics`). With no `GROUPS_*` variable set nothing changes for anyone.
2. Golf days appear for exactly the accounts Groups is on for. Nothing else to set.
3. First real check: the owner makes a group, taps Golf day, starts a day at a course he plays, joins, logs two holes; My Apps > Activity shows "Golf started: <name>".

### Roll back
1. No deploy: remove `GROUPS_ENABLED` (Groups and Golf days go dark together; nothing is deleted).
2. Code: revert this commit. Nothing was migrated; `lock:gd:<group id>` rows are small and nothing else reads them. There is no switch for Golf days alone: if one is wanted, say so (one env check in `golfday.js handle()` and one in the tile).

### Questions for the owner
1. The 14 rules above are the manager's defaults. Change any?
2. After the day is CLOSED, should a card that is not finished still be ranked by its score so far, or drop below everyone who finished ("did not finish")?
3. Should scores be allowed before the date of the round (now: from that day on, Chicago time)?
4. Stroke indexes found on the course map are prefilled for the commissioner to check. Keep, or always start empty?
5. Want "Log my score" on the Golf GPS hole screen (no trip back to Groups each hole), and scores kept on the phone when there is no signal?
6. Commissioner changing course flag colours on a Golf day (rule 13): still waiting for his answer.
7. To-do (unchanged): upgrade The Odds API plan (about $30/month) if the app sells. Golf days spend no Odds API credits.

## v274 (groups-stats) — Nov 17 2026: Groups Games catalog (a registry) + lifetime stats ("My stats", a tap on any member name), behind the Groups switches

Coder agent in its own worktree on branch `groups-stats` (from origin/main 9dbfd05 = v273 + the owner-instruction note). Committed, NOT pushed: the manager merges, sets the final version number and pushes. **Dark with Groups: with `GROUPS_ENABLED` unset v274 is v273 for every user** (compared screen by screen, see Tests). **FREE FOR NOW IS UNCHANGED**: no paywall, price, trial or expiry added or changed. **No money, no messages, no orders.** "Lock" still means the weekly Lock only; nothing about Double lock, Bet of the Day or Upset. **Owner instruction of 9:04 AM respected**: no Calcutta auction, no pickleball group game, no auction file, no Calcutta / Pickleball tile; `pics/pickleball.html` changed only on the bump list.

### 1. The Games catalog (`pics/groups.js`)
- The group page now has THREE tiles: **Members, Games, Rules** (was Members, Contest / Standings, Rules, Golf day). **DECISION**: the brief says "one Games tile", so the Contest / Standings tile and the v273 Golf day tile are folded into it (one tap more to the standings). Say so if the owner wants the Standings tile back as a shortcut.
- **Games** opens a list: one card per game with a one-line description and its state badge (**None yet / Open / Finished**) plus a state line. Cards fill tablet / desktop width (`.oggrid.ogfit` = auto-fit columns). A card drills into the EXISTING screen (Back returns to Games, Back again to the group):
  - **Picks contest** (v272): state from the group object already on the phone (no call): none (no picks contest) / Open "Starts <day>" or "Running" / Finished "Final"; opens the Contest screen (rules, or standings once started).
  - **Golf day** (v273): state from ONE `POST /golfday/list` (the existing route; `golfday.js` is NOT fetched for it): Open (the open day's name and date) / Finished (the last closed day) / None yet ("start one" for the commissioner); opens the Golf day home.
- **THE REGISTRY** (for the other session or a later game; one entry adds a game, nothing else to edit):
  ```js
  window.OddGroups.addGame({ key, title, blurb, open(gr), state(gr) })
  ```
  - `key`: a-z / 0-9, 1 to 16 characters, starting with a letter, unique (the same key again REPLACES the entry). `title`: the card name. `blurb`: one line. `open(gr)`: called with the group object when the card is tapped; push your own screen so Back works (inside groups.js: `push({t: ...})`; from a module: the `GDX`-style context, see how `golfday.js` is wired). `state(gr)`: optional, returns `{k: 'none'|'open'|'finished', text}` or a Promise of it; `text` is inserted as HTML, so escape it. A failed or slow state shows "Could not check right now"; the card still opens.
  - `addGame` returns false (and adds nothing) for a bad key, a missing title or a missing `open`. `window.OddGroups.games()` lists `{key, title, blurb}`. Inside `pics/groups.js` the two entries are the `addGame({...})` calls right above `function games()`; ONLY the two games that exist are registered.
  - The Games tile's count and sub-line (titles joined by " · ") come from the registry.

### 2. Lifetime stats
- Phone: **My stats** tile on the Groups home (under Create / Join); **a tap on any member name** opens that member's group record: Members cards (now buttons, "Group record ›"), every standings row (now a button, "record ›" under the name), and a new "<name>'s group record ›" button on a Golf day player card (`pics/golfday.js`, one line; it is a `data-a="mstats"` button that groups.js handles). The view: two sections, each with its source and period line, six tiles (Contests played / Best finish / Record; Golf days played / Best net to par / Best Stableford). **Every tile drills down** (no new call) to a list of rows (not a table: place left, name and details under it, the number right, header over its columns), with Back; an explanation card says how it is counted. Another member's record says "Groups you are both in: N. Only groups you are both in count."
- Server: **`POST /groups/stats`** (one line in `odds-api/groups.js handle()`, the code is the NEW file `odds-api/gstats.js`). Body `{}` = my own record; `{id, pid}` = member `pid` of group `id` (the viewer must be a member of that group, else 404; `pid` not a member = 404). Because it is a `/groups` op it has exactly the Groups answers: dark / not on the list = 403 `{off:true}` reading nothing; an ended account = 403 `{expired:true}` "Your time has ended…" (the phone shows the same lock / sentence); signed out = 401; GET = 405. 40 calls per account per 10 minutes (429 after).
- **Where the numbers come from — NEVER from anyone's private bets or synced bets row** (the server test proves the stats code reads only `lock:` group rows):
  - Picks contests = FINISHED picks contests (`cEnded`: the contest's last day is over, Chicago time) of groups BOTH people are members of now, read through the contest's OWN standings (`groups.js standings(g, c, 'season')`: the group book + ledger, graded by Oddable): the same numbers the Contest screen shows.
  - Golf days = CLOSED Golf days of those groups: the kept days in `lock:gd:<group id>` (`days`) plus the new **`hist`** list (see 3).
- **HANDOFF had no definition of lifetime stats** (the plan only says "catalog / invites / stats"; the preview's `statsFor` / `memberStats` feed a `stats.js` that is not on main and carries entry / payout money fields, so it was not used). **DEFINITIONS (coder's, manager / owner to confirm)**:
  - Contests played = a member of that contest when it finished. **Won** = place 1 on the final standings with at least one settled bet; a SHARED first (T1) counts as a win and the tile says "(n shared)". **Best finish** = the best place among contests with a settled bet ("T1 of 4"); a contest with no settled bet shows "–".
  - **Record (W-L-P) and units** = the sum of what each finished contest's standings show for that member (a Best-five contest counts its best five a week, as its standings do). Bets still being graded after the end are left out and counted as "still grading".
  - Golf days played = a closed day where the member logged at least one counting hole. **Wins** = place 1 on the final board (ties shared, counted). **Best net to par** = lowest (net strokes − par) on a FINISHED card (all counting holes), on ANY format (a Stableford day has net scores too; on a gross day no strokes are given, so net = gross). **Best Stableford** = highest points on a finished card of a Stableford day. 9-hole and 18-hole rounds are listed together, each labelled with its holes (a day closed as the front 9 counts as a 9).
  - Period: "Finished group contests since <month of the first one>", "Closed Golf days since <month>"; "No finished group contest yet" / "No closed Golf day yet" when there is none.
- **Privacy**: the answer holds the person's name, group / contest / course / day names, dates and numbers only. Never a phone number, email, account id, PIN, sync row id, member key or group id (all checked in `gstats.test.js` 9). Another member's record never shows anything from a group the viewer is not in.

### 3. `odds-api/golfday.js`: ONE change
- `close` now also appends the day's compact final board to `hist` in the same row (`{id, name, course, date, format, holes, short, closed, rows:[{pid, name, place, tied, thru, of, done, gross, par, net, pts, toPar}]}`; newest 500 per group, `HISTMAX`). Reason: the row keeps only the open day and the last 5 closed days, so without it a group's 6th Golf day would drop out of the lifetime numbers. Days closed BEFORE v274 have no `hist` entry and are counted from the kept days (so at most the last 5 of them). Nothing reads `hist` but `gstats.js`; `list` / `get` answers are unchanged.

### Limits / NOT verified
- NOT verified (the build shell cannot reach them): the live odds server and Supabase, a real phone, a real finished contest (the server test seeds the group ledger directly; the grading path itself is v272's), real Golf days across many weeks, the service worker update from v273.
- Cost: one stats call reads each shared group, its Golf day row, and for each finished contest the same ledger + book reads as opening its standings (ESPN only for bets not yet graded). With many groups / contests it is slower; 40 calls per 10 minutes per account. No cache beyond groups.js's 60-second contest cache.
- Golf days closed before v274: only those still in the kept list (last 5 per group) count. Contests: only groups the person is in NOW (a deleted group, or one he left before it started, is not counted).
- A member who leaves the GROUP after a Golf day stays on that board but is no longer "in both groups", so it drops out of others' view of him.

## Pickleball as a group game on main (from the feature session) — Nov 17 2026, branch `feat/pickleball-group-game-on-main`, NOT pushed, no version bump
Written by the Local events coder of the feature session (the "other session" in the OWNER INSTRUCTION at the top: Steve, Nov 17 9:04 AM CT, "Leave the Calcutta auction and group pickleball alone. Another session is building those two on live."). Owner decision PB-1 (Nov 16 2026, 3:03 PM CT): "pickle ball is in the oddable app not the group link i asked for" = pickleball and local games as a GROUP game, set up by a commissioner, joined from the group or by invite link. Built on main **8af1f799f5c7289b177b71df7343b80be45d2681** (v274 groups-stats). Engine source: side branch `local-v1` **11087cd** (Local events v1.5). The main-line session owns version numbers, pushes and deploys; nothing here is pushed.

**Review status:** the engine was reviewed four times on the side branch (last verdict "ready on its own"); the round-4 fixes (M1-M3, L1-L2) and the stats v1.5 adapter are coder-tested only. **This on-main wiring (gate, glist, group-only create, phone registration, invite capture) has NOT been independently reviewed.** Get one review of this branch before the switch is turned on.

### Switches (Render env vars on the odds server, service "ODDABLE" / calcutta-odds; exact names; read on every request)
- `LOCALEVENTS_ENABLED` — exactly `1` = on. Unset or anything else = OFF for everybody, the owner too (default). Off: `server.js` does not even load `localevents.js`; `/levents/*` answers what v274 answers (404 `{"error":"not found"}`); `/acct/status` and `me` are byte-for-byte v274's.
- Then the SAME Groups switches as Golf days: `GROUPS_ENABLED` (must be `1` too), `GROUPS_OWNER_ONLY`, `GROUPS_TESTERS` (asked through `groups.js onFor()`, exactly like `golfday.js`). Not on for this account = 403 `{off:true}`, nothing read; an account whose time has ended = 403 `{expired:true}` "Your time has ended. Pay to use Groups again." (acct.js `expired`, like Groups).
- No other new variable. `ENTERPRISE_MAX_PEOPLE` stays limits.js's. `EVENTS_ENABLED` is the Activity switch (unchanged; the new `pickleball_start` Activity event obeys it).
- Suggested go-live: with Groups already owner-only, add `LOCALEVENTS_ENABLED=1`; the owner's phone gets the card on its next account check. Off again = delete the variable (nothing is deleted; rows stay).

### Routes (all `POST /levents/<op>`, JSON, header `x-token`; no new request header, so the CORS line is unchanged)
`glist {groupId}` (NEW: one group's events for its members; `{events, comm, now}`; non-member 403, bad id 404) · `create {…, groupId}` (group's commissioner only; `groupId` required on main) · `gjoin {id, preview?, as, myName}` (members of the event's group) · `preview` / `join {code}` (the invite link; a non-member of the group gets `groupOnly` / 403 `code:'group'`) · `peek {code}` (public title only; during the owner-only test 403 like Groups' invite preview) · and the engine's own: `mine get edit entrant draw leave delete paid finish solo score confirm dispute live pick challenge accept decline claim tiebreak picklock poreset poredraw seeds trate partners teams coorg seatok review approve rset cosign` (see the side HANDOFF "Local events" sections in the feature session for each). Storage: `lock:evt:<id>` (one row per event, conditional writes), `lock:evtidx` (index; `g` = group id), `lock:evr:<id>` (built-in ratings stand-in), `lock:entuse` only for Enterprise organizers (limits.recordPlay).

### What a commissioner and a player can do
- Group's commissioner: Groups → the group → Games → **Pickleball & local games** → Start a game: pickleball (singles / doubles / mixed), tennis, basketball, cornhole, darts, bowling, pool, other; round robin (pools, courts, optional playoff), single / double elimination (rulebook bracket reset), ladder, heads-up, solo / skills, bracket pick'em; seeding (Oddable rating from app play, random, as listed); entry, main / side pot, split, take (information only: Oddable never collects, holds or pays); invite link `?join=e.<code>`; make the draw now; correct disputed scores; settle a tie (coin flip default, or the house-rule playoff); set pick'em close times; finish; mark entries paid; delete before the start.
- A member of the group: sees the group's games, joins from the group or with the link (player or spectator; never more than 8 people per game: limits.js), enters his match scores (the scorer side), confirms or disputes the other side's, sees matches / standings / bracket / history / money owed and prizes (every tile drills down, Back returns), pick'em (players and organizers never pick their own matches; organizers do not pick at all).
- Someone who is not in the group, with the link: the link survives sign-up / sign-in and lands on the event's join screen, which says "This event belongs to a group… join the group first" (the event cannot be joined without group membership; the commissioner sends the GROUP link first).

### Group link (why the event is not a Groups contest)
Like Golf days (v273), the event keeps the group id on its own row and every call checks `groups.memberCheck`; `groups.attachGame` is NOT called, so the group's row (contests list, main-pot split, MAXC slots) never changes and `groups.wire()` is not called with this engine. If the main-line session later wants local events in the group's pot / standings, call `groups.wire({ events: require('./localevents') })` (registerGame 'event' + registerInvite 'e') and give the `D.groups()` facade `attachGame` back; that is a Groups money decision (H1 / H2 / H4).

### NOT included (and what each needs)
- **Stats / ratings approval screen** (stats contract v1.5: organizer approves every number in money events, co-sign, review screen): the code is IN `localevents.js` (`ST`, `NEWM()`, `regStats`, routes `seatok review approve rset cosign`) but INERT, because main has no `odds-api/stats.js`. Needs: the stats branch's `stats.js` (v1.5, 1426e22 or later) on main, its phone script (for `makeupHtml`), and `stats.register('events', require('./localevents').statsFor)` (its auto-load `./events` would find the Activity module). Until then ratings are the built-in Elo stand-in (`STAND`, `lock:evr:`): confirmed games only, 1500 start, unofficial.
- **Calcutta of pickleball teams**: `localevents.ADAPTER.calcuttaSource` / `registerCalcutta` exist (Calcutta round-4 contract). Needs: `feat/calcutta-on-main` merged, then `ADAPTER.registerCalcutta(require('./calcutta'), <event id>)` per event or a source entry in calcutta.js `EVENTS` with a `plug` key, plus a "Calcutta this event" button (not built).
- **Enterprise billing** (more than 8 people, $500/month + $20 per player per game): `bill()` calls main's `limits.recordPlay` for organizers `limits.isEnterprise` says are Enterprise (rows in `lock:entuse`, once per player per game); nothing invoices. Because games are group-only, a game can never hold more people than the group's own limit. Needs: the owner's Enterprise sign-up / contact flow and an invoice step in My Apps.
- Import of games logged on the stand-alone pickleball page (plan below). Voice "Log my score" (shows only when a `window.VoiceLog` exists; none on main). Offline score queue works as on the side branch (`oevt-q`).

### The stand-alone pickleball page (main's v253, court search v259 / v261): KEPT, not deleted
- To HIDE its entry once the group game is switched on for everyone: in `pics/index.html` delete `<option value="pickleball">🏓 Pickleball</option>` from `#lgsel` (line ~277). Keep `if (v === 'pickleball') { location.href = 'pickleball.html'; return; }` (line ~3290) so an old `?lg=pickleball` link still lands, and keep `pics/pickleball.html` / `pickleball.js`: the group game's Info tab links to its court search (`pickleball.html#/courts`). To hide it only for accounts the group game is on for (before "everyone"): `{ const o = document.querySelector('#lgsel option[value="pickleball"]'); if (o && levOn()) o.remove(); }` after the `#lgsel` setup line (~3294).
- IMPORT PLAN (not built): the page stores ONLY on the phone (localStorage keys `pb1-ev-<id>`; events `{v, id, name, date, fmt: rr|king|chal|ladder|se|de|calc|skins|pick, ps:[{id, n, d}], g / ms: games, …}`). An import would take: (1) a "Bring my old games" card on the group's list when `pb1-ev-*` keys exist (phone only reads them); (2) a server route `POST /levents/import {groupId, ev}` (commissioner of that group) that maps formats with an equivalent (rr with fixed partners, ladder, se, de) and keeps the others (king / challenge court, rotating-partner rr, Calcutta, skins, pick) as a read-only record; players become typed-in guests with claim codes (so real accounts can claim them); every match is "made final by the organizer" = kept as a record and NOT rated (the rating rule); the event is created final with its date; (3) mark the phone copy imported (never delete it); (4) tests that nothing is rated and nothing is billed. Size: about a day with tests.

### Merge guide (main-line session)
1. `git fetch origin`; rebase `feat/pickleball-group-game-on-main` onto `origin/main`. Expected conflicts only with `feat/calcutta-on-main` (both touch `acct.js gFlag`, `gate.js setMe` / sign-out lists, `index.html` loaders and the four reload guards, `sync.js` guard, `server.js` before `/locks`): keep BOTH (each adds its own flag / key / overlay id).
2. Bump the version (next free number): `localevents.js?v=` in `index.html levLoad()` + your usual list; `sw.js`.
3. Run every suite (`for f in odds-api/*.test.js scripts/*/*.test.js; do node $f; done`, with `ODD_CALCUTTA` pointed at a non-existent path or the in-repo calcutta.js) and the static build command (no folder was added to `pics/`).
4. Push with `LOCALEVENTS_ENABLED` unset; check `/levents/mine` answers 404 like v274 and `/acct/status` has no `levents`.
5. Owner-only: set `LOCALEVENTS_ENABLED` = `1` (Groups already owner-only). The owner: Groups → a group → Games → Pickleball & local games → Start a game; My Apps Activity shows "Pickleball started: <name>".
